# 351: Johan: Oh No, I’m the Main Fighter?!

Candice had already expected to lose; she simply wanted to fight with all her strength. At the very least, she wanted to see just how large the gap between her and Johan was.

What she didn't expect was to lose so decisively.

But there was no denying the benefits—she had learned a lot.

"Johan, this is proof of your victory over Snowpoint Gym. Though you don't need proof of anything, I imagine this might still be useful if you plan to challenge the Lily of the Valley Conference."

Candice handed over a badge, shaped like a shard of ice.

"And this—TM Ice Breath. It might be of some use to you."

From her words and tone, it was clear just how much respect she had for Johan.

Ice Breath? A move that always lands a critical hit—not only perfect for his Ninetales but also highly compatible with a one-hit KO style.

After a brief thought, Johan accepted it without hesitation.

"Thanks, Candice."

"You're too kind—I should be the one thanking you."

A smile spread across her face. This battle alone would give her plenty to think about and train with for a long time.

Fighting strong trainers really was the best way to grow.

Johan couldn't help but reflect on that as well.

To be honest, compared to other regions, the Gym Leaders in Sinnoh tended to be weaker on average.

It couldn't be helped—Sinnoh's League was established later than others due to the influence of ancient Hisui's history.

But he also noticed a trend—Sinnoh was actively pushing for younger trainers to take up leadership roles.

Candice was one example, as were Maylene in Veilstone, Gardenia in Eterna, and Byron's son, Roark, in Oreburgh.

Even beyond the Gym Leaders, the Sinnoh League was making a deliberate effort to pass leadership to a younger generation.

That was one of the reasons why, in the future, figures like Flint, Aaron, and Lucian would rise to become the Elite Four.

Of course, that didn't mean they were weak.

Once Cynthia took the Champion's seat and the Elite Four lineup solidified, Sinnoh's League would be one of the strongest across all regions.

And that was, in part, thanks to this youth-oriented strategy.

"Johan."

As he turned, he saw Flint and Aaron approaching, their expressions unusually serious.

Johan's heart skipped a beat.

Their faces said it all.

He had a bad feeling.

Still, he forced a smile and turned to Candice.

"Candice, we should get going."

She nodded, sensing the shift in atmosphere.

"If there's anything I can do to help, just let me know. I'll do my best."

"We'll keep that in mind."

With that, Johan, Flint, and Aaron left quickly.

As soon as they stepped out, Johan checked his phone.

A message from Lucian.

"The volcano is acting up."

Sinnoh, Snowpoint Temple

Cynthia, Lucian, and Professor Carolina were already there.

"You're here," Cynthia greeted them, her expression grim.

"What's going on with the volcano? Wasn't it stable yesterday?" Johan asked.

"In just one night…"

Professor Carolina shook her head. "The previously calm volcano is now boiling over, as if someone poured oil on a fire."

Johan frowned. "Shouldn't we call in reinforcements from the League?"

"We already have," Lucian said, adjusting his glasses. "But…"

Right now, the Sinnoh League was in the middle of cracking down on the largest Pokémon poaching ring in the region.

That gang was cunning—almost as if they had insider knowledge of the League's movements. The League had already poured too much time and manpower into this operation.

Now, they had a difficult choice to make.

They could either abandon months of effort to respond to the volcanic threat, or they could gamble on Snowpoint's situation not spiraling out of control.

To make matters worse, Snowpoint was at the northernmost point of Sinnoh, while the League HQ was in the far southeast. Even if they mobilized immediately, it would take time to get there.

Simply put—no one was prepared for how quickly this situation escalated.

"Bertha is on her way," Lucian added.

Sinnoh's Ground-type Elite Four member—Bertha!

At least they would have one Elite Four-level fighter coming…

To be honest, though, if Regigigas truly woke up angry, the League's forces might not make a difference.

They were talking about a Pokémon that dragged entire continents.

If Cynthia hadn't reached her prime yet, there was no one in Sinnoh who could go toe-to-toe with Regigigas.

Maybe—just maybe—Jesse, James, and Meowth could take a few punches for them.

But Johan sighed in relief.

At least, for now, the team was strong.

Lucian, Flint, Cynthia, and Aaron—four of Sinnoh's future strongest figures.

They had this covered.

…Right?

Then he noticed something strange.

They were all looking at him.

Johan stiffened.

Wait. What?

In their minds:

Cynthia: Johan might be able to calm Regigigas. No—he definitely can. Lucian: No need to panic. Johan is basically an elder in this situation. Flint: Johan is stronger than me. I'll just follow his lead. Aaron: Wait, why are volcanoes and Regigigas even related?

Johan twitched.

Oh no. I'm the main fighter?!

He muttered, "Am I supposed to be the head of this operation…?"

"Huh?"

The others didn't hear him clearly.

"Johan, do you have a plan?" Lucian asked.

A plan?

A plan?!

Johan wanted to scream.

The only plan I have is getting on a plane and flying home!

He rolled his eyes, but before he could answer, Professor Carolina returned from a phone call.

"The League just appointed me as the temporary commander for this crisis," she said.

Then she turned to Johan, completely serious.

"But I don't know Pokémon like you do. I think you should take charge."

Johan pressed his hand to his forehead.

I'm just a guy from Hoenn…

But seeing their expectant expressions, he had no choice.

"First, we need to confirm whether the volcano's eruption will actually cause a disaster."

RUMBLE.

They all felt the ground shake violently.

From the peak of Snowpoint Mountain, they could see lava and smoke rising into the sky.

"Uh…" Aaron blinked. "It kinda looks like it's about to erupt."

Johan: "…"

He took a deep breath.

"Then we need to confirm whether the eruption will actually wake up Regigigas."

RUMBLE.

A deep, ancient tremor echoed from within the mountains.

In the distance, snow-covered peaks began to collapse, avalanches roaring down the slopes.

Then, a low metallic hum reverberated through the air—an overwhelming presence pressing down on them.

Like an ancient deity slowly awakening from its slumber.

Lucian gulped.

"Johan, could you not say things out loud?"

Everything he said just kept coming true.

Johan scowled.

Like I'm doing this on purpose?!

"Hey," Aaron said suddenly. "Johan, what if you just say, 'Regigigas went back to sleep'? Maybe the crisis will be over."

Flint's eyes lit up.

Johan glared at them.

You actually believe that?!

Carolina sighed. "It looks like the eruption really did wake Regigigas."

This could either save them—or doom them.

"Professor Carolina, start evacuating Snowpoint," Johan ordered.

"Will we have time?" she asked.

He glanced at the volcano. It was still building up.

They had some time.

"Yes," Johan said firmly.

"Alright."

Carolina sped off in her jeep.

Johan turned to Flint.

"Flint, the real powerhouse is waking up. You go first."

"Yes! …Wait, me?!"

Flint instinctively agreed, then did a double take.

He swallowed hard and looked toward Snowpoint Temple.

Could he win?

Johan chuckled.

He was just messing with him.

At least now, everyone was a little more relaxed.

Then he pulled Cynthia and Lucian aside to discuss a battle plan.

They needed to prepare for the worst.

Hopefully, Bertha would arrive in time.

If not…

Johan glanced at the shadows.

Marshadow had been following them for a while now.

If things went south…

Maybe it could stall Regigigas for a few seconds.

In the darkness, Marshadow shivered.

Why did it suddenly feel like it was being targeted?

# 352: Awakening—Regigigas!

"The ground's shaking, meow!"

Meowth, in the middle of filming, felt the tremors and quickly dove under a table.

At the same time, Matori received an urgent notice from the League directed at Snowpoint City's residents.

"The volcano on Snowpoint Mountain is showing signs of erupting. Everyone must leave Snowpoint City immediately!" Matori called out.

"Move, move!"

Hearing this, Jessie, James, and Meowth grabbed Wobbuffet and Pichu and ran out to a jeep parked outside the villa.

"Wait, where's Johan?"

James suddenly realized.

"Don't worry! With Johan's strength, forget a volcanic eruption—even if a Legendary Pokémon shows up, he'll be fine!" Jessie declared with confidence.

At that, the trio exchanged glances and then shouted in unison:

"Johan is incredible, meow!"

"Wobba~ Wobba!"

"Pii~chuu!!"

"James, step on it! We're evacuating!" Jessie ordered.

James floored the gas pedal, and the jeep sped off.

A few moments later, Matori emerged from the villa, watching the dust trail of the departing vehicle with a blank stare.

"…I wasn't in the car. I wasn't in the car!!"

She chased after them for a few steps but then came to a halt, her expression twisting.

"You're going the wrong way!"

But by then, the jeep had already disappeared into the distance.

On a research station overlooking Snowpoint Mountain

Flint let out a long yawn.

"Is this taking forever, or is it just me?"

His eyes teared up slightly from drowsiness.

To be honest, when he first heard that Regigigas was waking up, he'd felt nervous.

But…

He looked up at the sky.

The sun had already traveled from east to west.

"This thing is taking forever to wake up."

If he'd known, he would've taken a nap first.

Cynthia put away her phone and nodded toward Johan.

Because Regigigas was "waking up" at such a sluggish pace, the evacuation of Snowpoint City had gone relatively smoothly.

Then—

BOOM!

A deafening roar, far louder than before, exploded from the mountains.

Flint stumbled, nearly falling to the ground.

In the distance, massive sections of snow tumbled down the slopes, accompanied by large rocks cracking and scattering.

The sky itself seemed to darken—not just from volcanic ash, but as if heralding something ominous.

Everyone's expressions shifted.

Compared to the previous tremors, this was on an entirely different scale.

And that ancient, overwhelming presence grew even stronger.

"…It's awake," Aaron murmured.

"Let's move!"

Cynthia tossed out a Poké Ball, summoning her Garchomp. She swiftly leaped onto its back.

"GARRR!!"

With a mighty roar, Garchomp shot into the sky, heading straight for Snowpoint Temple.

Johan clicked his tongue, then called out his Dragonite.

"Follow Garchomp."

"Oowoo~~"

Flapping its stubby wings, Dragonite burst forward like a rocket.

Aaron summoned both his Dustox and Beautifly, using their combined strength to take off.

"Bronzong!"

Lucian released his Bronzong and hopped onto it.

Watching everyone take off, Flint opened his mouth… then looked at Lucian.

"…Lucian."

Lucian sighed and rolled his eyes.

"Bronzong, take him too."

Hum—

Bronzong used its psychic powers to lift Flint, and the two of them followed the others toward the temple.

As they soared through the air, Johan shouted, "When is Bertha getting here?"

Sure, their group looked strong at a glance—but they were all still in their 'rookie' phase!

They needed time to develop their full potential.

Having at least one Elite Four member on-site would be reassuring.

Then again…

If Regigigas was truly enraged, even an Elite Four might not make a difference.

"She's still on the way!" Cynthia called back from Garchomp's back.

"…Still on the way."

Did Bertha not have a Flying-type Pokémon?

Come to think of it, as a Ground-type specialist, she probably didn't.

BOOM!

The closer they got to Snowpoint Temple, the more intense the tremors became.

Suddenly, a blinding white light shot out from within the temple ruins, piercing the clouds above.

Even the heavy, swirling storm clouds were ripped apart.

Then—

A sound.

Not just a roar—a metallic crash.

Like the awakening of an ancient deity, filled with both rage and solemnity.

"REGI——!!"

A chill ran down everyone's spine.

Johan muttered under his breath,

"…This is some Ultraman-tier awakening."

They landed on an open field in front of the ruined temple.

The entire ground was shaking violently, the surrounding trees distorted, their snow-covered branches stripped bare by the tremors.

The blinding white light faded, replaced by a blood-red glow erupting from the shattered remains of the temple's ceiling.

BOOM!!

The temple collapsed.

Chunks of stone and debris flew in every direction.

A terrifying force surged forth, carrying a blast of ice and rock.

"Oowoo!!"

"GARRR!!"

Dragonite and Garchomp roared in unison.

Dragonite spun up a whirlwind of water to defend, while Garchomp shattered the ground to raise a wall of stone against the incoming shockwave.

From the debris, a colossal figure slowly rose.

"REGI——!!"

Sweat beaded on Lucian's forehead. He wiped it off before saying with difficulty,

"This is… the ancient titan… Regigigas?!"

The sheer pressure was overwhelming.

Were they seriously about to fight this thing?

Regigigas stood, its massive frame covered in vines and moss. Three golden stripes ran across its body, with seven glowing dots on its face, arranged like the Big Dipper.

Its arms and legs were adorned with additional red, blue, and white lights, all of which pulsed an angry crimson.

Johan's eye twitched.

"Professor Oak is full of crap—there's no way this thing is 'under four meters tall.'"

If Regigigas was under four meters, then Johan might as well claim to be eight years old.

By eye estimate alone, it had to be at least ten meters tall—and according to the ancient texts Cynthia deciphered, the real Regigigas was over fifty meters tall.

So technically, it still wasn't fully awake.

"REGI——!!"

Lucian and Flint instinctively braced themselves.

"…What do we do?" Flint whispered.

Even if all five of them attacked at once, would it even matter?

"Don't make any rash moves," Johan instructed. "We need to confirm whether Regigigas woke up just because of the volcano, or if something else is at play."

If it only woke up to stop the volcano, like it did in ancient times, it might go back to sleep afterward.

If that was the case, they wouldn't have to fight it.

Everyone nodded, remaining still.

Then—

Regigigas turned.

Not toward the volcano, but toward the ruins of the temple.

Its red lights pulsed brighter.

"Regi——!!"

It stomped down hard.

BOOM!

The remaining parts of Snowpoint Temple crumbled.

Johan's stomach sank.

This wasn't right.

If it was only angry about the volcano, why wasn't it heading toward the eruption?

"…Something's off," Cynthia said grimly.

Flint clenched his fists.

"Looks like we're fighting after all."

Despite the overwhelming power before him, Flint was the first to step forward.

Without hesitation, he released all six of his Pokémon.

"GOKA!!"

Infernape's flames roared to life, its fighting spirit ignited.

"Typhlosion, Flamethrower!" Flint commanded.

"ROOAARR!!"

The flames surged toward Regigigas—

And then vanished.

Regigigas barely even reacted.

It simply brushed the scorch marks off its shoulder and glared down at them.

Then, it slowly brought its massive hands together—energy building between them.

A catastrophic force surged forth.

"QUIET!"

For the first time, Johan activated his Aura.

The power of the Wave spread outward.

And finally, Regigigas responded.

"I… AM… SO… TIRED!!!"

# 353: No Choice—Then We Fight!

The volcano spewed black ash into the sky, swirling like a flock of crows descending from above. The biting cold winds mixed with waves of heat, creating an unsettling contrast of freezing chill and scorching warmth.

Johan stood with his eyes shut, brows furrowed, his outstretched hands glowing faintly with an azure light.

Cynthia and the others formed a protective stance around him.

They weren't just dealing with the immediate threat of Regigigas—countless Pokémon, startled by the seismic activity, were now fleeing wildly through the mountains.

But most importantly, they had to handle Regigigas itself.

Johan's Pokémon had all emerged on their own, standing loyally by his side.

"Goka…"

Charcadet gazed at the towering, mountainous figure ahead with a determined look.

Would the titan be willing to listen?

"Is it working?" Flint asked again, a hint of relief in his voice.

Lucian, too, eased up slightly, already impressed.

As expected of Johan—the renowned Pokémon psychologist. He could even calm an ancient titan like Regigigas…

"REGI—"

Regigigas leaned forward slightly, as if lowering its head to find the one speaking to it.

The sheer weight of its movements sent a wave of invisible pressure washing over them. Despite its seemingly slow motions, every step carried an undeniable sense of devastation—as if the mere flick of its hand could turn everything to dust.

The glowing signals on its body flickered rapidly, and amidst the ominous red lights, other colors finally began to emerge.

From Lucian's perspective, this seemed like a promising sign.

However—

Cynthia noticed Johan's hands lowering, and more importantly, the deep crease that remained on his brow.

"CHI—KA—SSSS!!"

Regigigas let out another earth-shaking roar, and the colors on its signal lights, which had briefly fluctuated, all turned blood-red once more.

At the same time, it pressed its hands together—then slowly spread them apart, raising them high above its head.

A powerful orange glow gathered between its palms, growing in intensity.

Johan's eyes flew open.

Without hesitation, he grabbed Cynthia's wrist and yelled—

"RUN!"

Shit! That's 'Crush Grip'!

Why the hell was everyone just standing there?!

MOVE!

Realizing the danger a second too late, Lucian and Flint finally reacted, stumbling backward.

Confusion was written all over their faces.

"Wait—wasn't it calming down?!" Lucian asked in disbelief.

Why did it seem even angrier now?!

Johan exhaled sharply.

The titan's rage wasn't just from waking up abruptly.

The eruption wasn't natural—it was man-made!

That was the real reason Regigigas was so furious.

For centuries, it had sealed away the volcano, allowing itself to finally rest.

Yet in the blink of an eye, these reckless mortals had triggered another eruption—and to make things worse, they had rudely woken it up halfway through its nap.

"The eruption was intentional?!"

Everyone was stunned.

It all made sense now.

The sudden instability of the volcano—it wasn't just nature taking its course. Someone had interfered.

But now, there was a bigger problem.

What do we do about an enraged Regigigas?

"We have no choice. We're going with Plan Three," Johan said, backing away.

"…Plan Three?" Flint blinked.

There were three plans?!

Since when?!

And more importantly—what the hell were Plans One and Two?

The first plan was simple: observe Regigigas and see where its attention was directed after waking up.

The second plan was for Johan to negotiate with Regigigas as an emissary of other legendary Pokémon.

And Plan Three?

The worst-case scenario.

A direct confrontation.

These discussions had happened earlier between Johan, Cynthia, and Lucian—but they had never bothered to explain them to Flint.

After all, he wouldn't have cared anyway.

"The snowfield," Lucian said, glancing at the landscape. "It's the best place. Fewer wild Pokémon, less collateral damage."

"The problem is…" Aaron frowned. "It's getting faster."

Johan cursed under his breath.

It really was.

Regigigas's legendary Slow Start ability was wearing off.

"We won't make it," Lucian muttered. "Not in time."

We have to fight it here.

Cynthia didn't hesitate.

She leaped from Garchomp's back mid-flight, her coat and golden hair billowing in the freezing wind.

"Garchomp, Dragon Rush!"

"GARRR!!"

Garchomp's eyes glowed red, and with a deafening roar, it cloaked itself in an aura of swirling blue and purple energy, becoming a raging dragon avatar.

A supersonic missile, hurtling toward Regigigas.

Its razor-sharp scythes carved a trail of sparks across Regigigas's body.

BOOM!!

"REGI!!"

The impact forced the giant to stumble back two steps.

It shifted its focus onto Garchomp.

This one… was strong.

But before it could retaliate, Garchomp swiftly turned and retreated into the sky.

On Dragonite's back, Johan held onto Cynthia, her hair whipping across his face.

He really wanted to sneeze.

"…Was that really necessary?" he grumbled.

Cynthia simply smiled and patted Dragonite's head.

"Damn, Cynthia's a beast!" Flint shouted.

Lucian gave him a side-eye.

Really? Now was the time for this?

He turned his head away.

He saw nothing. Heard nothing.

"Ohhh~~" Aaron suddenly understood.

With that single attack, Cynthia had bought them just enough time.

Regigigas paused, allowing the group to reach the snowfield.

"REGI!!"

Its roar shook the ground, sending tremors across the field.

The ancient titan stepped forward.

BOOM.

The ground trembled.

"CHI-KA-SSSS!!"

It raised both arms, its massive frame blotting out the sunlight.

The energy gathering between its palms carried the same raw power that once moved continents.

Cynthia exhaled, brushing loose strands of hair behind her ear.

"I don't even want to imagine how strong it was at full power."

Flint swallowed hard.

"…We're not seriously trying to beat this thing, are we?"

They didn't need to defeat it.

Even in its weakened state, it was too strong.

Instead, Johan had a different plan.

"Cynthia, Flint, Aaron—stall it."

Then, he looked at Lucian.

Lucian took a deep breath and nodded firmly.

"Bronzong, Trick!"

CLANG!

A mysterious, shimmering energy linked Bronzong and Regigigas.

Suddenly—

Regigigas winced.

It was poisoned.

"Again! Alakazam, Trick!"

A fiery red orb switched places—

Burned.

"Mr. Mime, one more time!"

A heavy iron ball took the place of the flame orb.

Flint watched, eyes wide.

"…Holy shit."

Toxic Orb. Flame Orb. Iron Ball.

Since standard debuffs wouldn't work, they used items instead.

This was pure strategy.

"I knew Johan was a genius," Flint muttered.

Now Regigigas was not only weakened—it was pissed off.

"CHI-KA-SSSS!!"

"Garchomp, Dragon Rush! Lucario, Aura Sphere! Milotic—"

"BURN, BABY, BURN!! INFERNAPE, FLARE BLITZ!!"

"Scizor, Giga Impact! Heracross, Close Combat!!"

They had to end this before Slow Start wore off.

Johan watched as their Pokémon clashed with the titan.

Then—an opening.

"Gengar! Ninetales! Now—Hypnosis!"

"Ka-kaah!!"

"Oowoo!!"

Johan channeled his Aura.

A triple-layered sleep effect.

Forced shutdown.

Regigigas froze mid-attack.

Its lights flickered.

It worked.

"…Oh, crap," Lucian suddenly muttered.

Johan turned.

Three figures emerged from the ruins.

Regice. Regirock. Registeel.

"…We forgot about them," Lucian whispered.

Regigigas's lights flashed red again.

"REGI——!!"

Johan sighed, rolling up his sleeves.

"…Guess we do this all over again."

# 354: The Three-Part Sleep Tactic!

"I need more time!" Johan said in a firm voice.

Cynthia gave a resolute nod.

"Leave it to me!"

No unnecessary words were exchanged. The two immediately sprang into action.

"Ninetales, Nasty Plot! Baton Pass!"

Ice Ninetales' eyes gleamed with a sinister black light before it vanished into its Poké Ball, wrapped in a crimson glow. In its place, Gengar emerged, inheriting the boosted power.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Gengar's pitch-black body glowed faintly, as if polished to a shine.

"Gengar, Nasty Plot again!"

At Johan's command, Gengar boosted its power even further, stacking its abilities to a dangerous level.

At the same time, Ninetales re-emerged. The snowstorm howled around them, and its nine tails spread wide.

"Light Screen!"

A shimmering barrier formed, stacking layers of protection before Johan, Cynthia, and their Pokémon, forming a near-impenetrable defense.

Meanwhile—

Johan's Aura flared violently.

His voice was calm yet commanding.

"Dragonite, Dragon Dance!"

"Draaa~~"

Dragonite flapped its wings, rising into the air in a rhythmic, graceful motion.

Then—

Johan's gaze sharpened.

"Outrage—activate!!"

"Garrr!!"

A surge of dark red energy erupted from Dragonite, a furious aura surging into its mind. Bloodshot veins spread across its eyes, its body trembling with primal fury.

Yet—thanks to Johan's guidance and its own willpower—Dragonite forced itself to stay in control.

"Tinkaton, Charcadet—Help!"

"Cha!!"

"Goka!!"

Both Pokémon shouted in unison.

Tinkaton raised its massive hammer, sending a white energy pulse toward Dragonite, further amplifying its already surging power.

Charcadet's body flared with fire, its golden light enveloping Dragonite, boosting its endurance.

"Kubfu—Coaching!"

"Ku!!"

Kubfu clenched its headband, its eyes shining with determination, casting a supportive aura over the entire battlefield.

By now, Dragonite had become a living weapon of destruction.

Even Lucian, who had been battling alongside them, turned to stare in awe.

Even Flint began doubting if his Infernape could take a hit from that thing.

Cynthia paid no attention to the commotion—she was completely focused on fending off Regigigas' relentless attacks.

Despite being far from its full strength, Regigigas was still Regigigas. With its Slow Start handicap and multiple status effects weakening it, it should have been significantly crippled.

Yet—even under these conditions—Cynthia was struggling just to hold her ground.

Johan had to time his intervention perfectly.

Then—his Aura picked up something unexpected.

A new presence.

"Wait a second…?"

On a nearby hill, a jeep tore through the snowy landscape, kicking up a trail of dust.

Inside—

"James, you're driving in the wrong direction! Why are the tremors getting stronger?! And what's with that roar?!" Jessie yelled, gripping his collar.

"You're the one who told me to drive this way!!" James protested.

"Quit arguing, meow!!" Meowth screeched, kicking Jessie aside and clawing at James' head. "Turn this thing around, I got a real bad feelin' about this, meow!!"

"Wobbuffet!!"

Johan blinked in surprise.

Then, he grinned.

Perfect.

The backup plan had just arrived.

"Lucario, Close Combat!"

"Rooo-kaah!!"

Lucario capitalized on Garchomp's opening, leaping toward Regigigas' massive frame, its fists raining down blows like a relentless storm.

"REGI——!!"

Even for a Legendary Pokémon, Regigigas was still Normal-type.

Fighting moves hit it hardest.

Now!

"Dragonite—"

Johan's eyes gleamed with confidence, his voice ringing across the battlefield.

"Extreme Speed—Dragon Rush!! CRASH IT!!!"

Dragonite forced down its raging instincts.

Its body ignited with a white-hot glow, before immediately being enveloped in draconic energy, transforming into a meteor of destruction.

With a deafening roar—it hurtled toward Regigigas.

"Draaa!!"

FALL!!

BOOOOOM!!!

The impact was earth-shattering.

Regigigas staggered back, its massive frame wobbling under the impact.

"Kubfu, Charcadet, Tinkaton!"

Already prepared, Tinkaton tensed up.

Kubfu and Charcadet threw it forward like a catapult, launching it straight at Regigigas.

"Cha!!"

Tinkaton shot through the air like a cannonball.

"Ninetales!!"

"Oowoo~!!"

Ninetales raised its upper body, psychic energy gleaming in its eyes.

A layer of frost formed around Tinkaton's hammer—this was Ninetales' Permanent Ice Wall!

"Smash it!!"

"Cha!!"

BOOM!!

With a furious battle cry, Tinkaton smashed its hammer straight into Regigigas' leg.

The ancient titan stumbled.

Then—

It toppled backward.

A mountain of divine power—collapsing like a fallen titan.

BOOOOOOOM!!!

The ground wailed in agony, ice and snow exploding into the air.

"Hold it down!!"

Cynthia didn't need Johan's instruction—her Pokémon were already on it.

Johan turned to Gengar.

"Now—Taunt it!!"

"Ka-kaah!! ヾ(▼▼;)"

Gengar grinned mischievously.

It turned its back to Regigigas—

And then—

SMACK. SMACK.

It slapped its own butt.

"CHI-KA-SSSS!! (▼皿▼)"

Regigigas' signal lights flashed with sheer rage.

Johan winced.

That wasn't just a taunt—that was straight-up insulting.

But—it worked.

Now—

"MEGA EVOLUTION!!"

A burst of multicolored light exploded from Johan's Mega Keystone.

Gengar's tongue glowed as its Mega Stone activated—

A storm of power erupted.

Its aura surged, its form twisting into something even more terrifying.

"Wh-what is THAT?!" Flint stammered.

"Mega Evolution?!" Lucian gasped.

Even Aaron's antenna-like hair stood up in shock.

Cynthia, however, smirked.

This was it.

"Ka-kaah!!"

The dazzling light faded—

Mega Gengar emerged, its sinister eyes flashing an eerie blue.

It raised its hands.

"Sleep. Now."

Hypnosis!!

Regigigas' signal lights flickered.

Its movements slowed.

But the three other Regis—Regirock, Regice, and Registeel—began to charge forward.

"Lucian, Flint, Aaron—hold them off!!"

Meanwhile, Johan doubled down on his Aura output.

This wasn't their first attempt at putting Regigigas to sleep.

They needed three full steps.

Step one—make it drowsy.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Mega Gengar's sinister waves weakened Regigigas' consciousness.

Step two—drop its guard.

"Ninetales!!"

"Oowoo!!"

Psychic power swirled—Regigigas staggered.

Step three—force shutdown.

Johan gritted his teeth, his Aura surging to its limit.

"Sleep, big guy."

Hypnosis—Three-Part Sleep Tactic—COMPLETE.

Regigigas froze.

Its signal lights pulsed twice—

Then—

Darkness.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Gengar wiped a ghostly tear from its eye.

Johan's Sleep Tactics—

Legendary Pokémon? No exceptions.

# 355: Regigigas: "You’re Actually a Pretty Nice Guy!"

The cold wind swept across the snowfield, where an ancient titan lay, the ground around it scarred with deep trenches and destruction.

With Regigigas now asleep, the three Regis stopped moving, standing by its side like sentinels.

Thud—

Flint and Aaron collapsed onto the ground, letting out long sighs of relief.

"It's finally over."

Between the two of them, they had sent out twelve Pokémon in total, and only four were still standing…

Lucian was exhausted too, but he managed to maintain his composure, leaning against Bronzong and adjusting his glasses.

Looking at the sleeping Regigigas, softly snoring, Lucian muttered in awe, "Johan is incredible!"

Defeating Regigigas?

That was probably impossible.

But putting it to sleep? That was another way of "winning."

Seeing the light fade from Mega Evolution, returning Gengar to its normal form, Lucian felt a surge of curiosity.

Mega Evolution?

What kind of power was that?

Mega Gengar felt significantly stronger after evolving—maybe not enough to match Regigigas outright, but its hypnosis ability had definitely improved.

Well, that was a question for later.

Johan, having finished the Mega Evolution, turned a little pale, his body swaying slightly as if he could collapse at any moment.

"You okay?"

Fortunately, Cynthia caught him in time.

Johan shook his head.

Mega Evolution still took a toll on him, and with his Aura almost completely drained, his body felt utterly exhausted.

"Milotic, Life Dew."

"Draaa~~"

Cynthia's Milotic gently swayed its tail, releasing a soothing blue light that wrapped around Johan, making him feel significantly better.

Then, Johan pulled out a Sitrus Berry and started eating.

"Johan, give me one too."

Flint and Aaron, helping each other up, walked over. Flint immediately reached out.

Johan wasn't stingy—he handed out berries all around.

Lucian hesitated, looking at the Sitrus Berry in his hand, then at Flint and Johan casually eating them alongside their Pokémon.

Wait…

Aren't these supposed to be Pokémon food?

"What about Regigigas?" Cynthia asked once Johan had recovered.

Although it looked like Regigigas was sleeping soundly now…

It hadn't reverted to its dormant "Stone of Light" state, and the three Regis remained standing guard.

If Regigigas woke up angry again…

Johan waved a hand dismissively.

"Yeah, its wake-up mood swings are a bit intense."

In the Pokémon world, plenty of legendary Pokémon had serious temper issues after waking up.

Groudon and Kyogre in Hoenn, for example.

Or Yveltal in Kalos…

"But right now, it's probably having a nice dream."

Johan glanced over at the giant.

Although Mega Gengar had orchestrated the hypnosis, Johan and Ninetales had intervened to ensure it wasn't a nightmare.

After all, what kind of nightmare would work on Regigigas?

Throwing it into a fight against Giratina?

It would probably start a dream battle with it.

A good dream was the better option.

"That's a relief," Flint and Aaron sighed in unison.

They really didn't want to fight Regigigas again.

A single punch from that thing could take out an entire squad—who could handle that?

"But the volcano—"

Lucian hesitated before bringing it up, knowing it would kill the mood.

But with Regigigas asleep, that was now their next big problem.

The human-caused volcanic eruption!

The very thing that had enraged Regigigas in the first place!

"Let the big guy rest first."

Johan continued, "Once it's in a better mood, I'll try talking to it."

Honestly, Regigigas wasn't that difficult to communicate with.

When Johan had pretended to be an emissary of Rayquaza earlier, Regigigas—despite its rage—had still been somewhat willing to listen.

The problem was just…

The sheer level of anger from being woken up.

Even if Rayquaza itself had come, Regigigas probably would've fought first and talked later.

It felt like the kind of Pokémon he could actually build a friendship with.

After resting for a while…

Johan felt ready.

He stretched, standing up and slowly approaching Regigigas.

"Regi!"

Regice, Regirock, and Registeel immediately turned their attention to him, their postures tensing.

Johan didn't go too close.

He stopped at a safe distance, with Cynthia and the Pokémon by his side.

He closed his eyes again.

A soft pulse of Aura spread from his body.

At the same time…

Johan noticed something.

His Aura felt stronger, more refined than before.

Could he finally use Aura Sphere?

With that thought, he used his Aura to "enter" Regigigas' dream.

Since he was the one who had created this dreamscape, he had left himself a "backdoor" in.

—

Inside the Dream—

In a vast, icy snowfield, a natural hot spring bubbled with warmth, steam rising into the cold air.

A shrunken-down Regigigas—less than four meters tall—lounged lazily in the water, a small white towel on its head, its face tinged red with relaxation.

Johan arrived, wearing a bath towel around his waist, stepping into the hot spring.

"Regi?"

Regigigas finally noticed him, blinking in mild confusion.

And a little irritation.

Even in a dream, it remembered what had happened before.

This human had thrown a ton of debuffs on it, and that Gengar…

Had mooned it.

But Johan just smiled.

He hadn't come empty-handed.

A floating wooden tray drifted across the water.

On it were all kinds of food—Pokéblocks, Poffins, fresh berries, Moomoo Milk, and juice.

Dream food was free, after all.

Seeing the feast, Regigigas immediately forgot about being mad, swallowing its saliva.

When Johan nudged the tray toward it, Regigigas didn't hesitate, grabbing a handful.

Johan had always wondered—how did Pokémon like Regigigas and the Regis eat?

He quickly got his answer.

Regigigas scooped up a handful of Pokéblocks, and the food simply dissolved into its body.

So that's how it eats?

"Regi!"

Its signal lights flashed in a strange rhythm.

"Well? How's it taste?" Johan asked.

[Pretty good~]

Regigigas leaned back lazily in the hot spring, giving a satisfied sigh.

Seeing it like this, Johan rubbed his chin thoughtfully.

So this was probably the state Professor Oak's Pokédex described.

That would explain a lot.

Using food as an icebreaker was always effective.

Whether for regular Pokémon or legendary ones.

Especially for a big, goofy legendary like this.

With the ice broken, this was Johan's specialty—Pokémon psychology.

"Sorry about what happened, Regigigas. We didn't mean to make you mad."

[I wasn't mad!]

"You weren't?"

[No!]

"Here, have some more Moomoo Milk to calm down."

[Well… I do have a little trouble sleeping.]

"A little trouble? You sleep for hundreds of years."

Still…

"I can help with that. Putting Pokémon to sleep is my specialty," Johan grinned.

[Really?]

Johan gestured at their surroundings.

"Aren't you having the best sleep right now?"

Regigigas blinked.

Was it awake or asleep right now?

And if it was asleep…

Had it actually eaten anything?

"Regi…?" (Confused noises.)

"Anyway, when you wake up, I'll get you a real meal, and then… I'll help you sleep even better."

[Hey, you're actually a pretty nice guy!]

And so…

Man and Pokémon soaked in the hot spring, chatting away.

When they got to Regigigas' legendary feats, the titan got excited and slung an arm around Johan.

[I tell ya, back then—massive meteors, shifting continents, crazy flames—I was there for all of it—]

…Why hadn't Johan realized earlier that Regigigas had such a thick accent and was a total chatterbox?

----------

# 356: Giovanni: "As Expected of Johan"

"Whirrrr—"

The latest model of the Rotom Berry Mixer roared as its high-speed turbines spun.

Under Johan's careful mixing and Rotom's assistance, plate after plate of steaming-hot Pokéblocks were quickly prepared.

"Regiiii—"

Regigigas sat on the ground, surrounded by Gengar, Tinkaton, Dragonite, Garchomp, and several other Pokémon.

Hard to believe that just moments ago, these very Pokémon had been fighting.

Or more accurately, it had been Gengar, Garchomp, and the others ganging up on Regigigas.

And still failing to take it down.

But now…

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah~!"

I'm done eating!!

Gengar patted its bowl, which was the size of a basin.

Regigigas looked serious.

When it came to eating, it had never conceded to anyone.

But today, it had met a true rival!

This little creature ate like a black hole, not even bothering to chew, swallowing everything in one go.

Regigigas now held Gengar in high regard.

Seeing that Gengar had finished, it also cleared its bowl in one go before turning its expectant gaze toward Johan.

[I'm done too!]

"Garrr!!"

Garchomp, having lost to Regigigas in battle, definitely didn't want to lose here.

Meanwhile, Johan was sweating profusely as he continuously poured berries into the mixer.

At this rate…

He'd rather fight Regigigas again!

This was way too much food!

Not to mention his own Pokémon, Cynthia's Pokémon—every single one of them looked like a hungry baby bird.

"Gengar, that's enough! Stop joining in!"

Johan finally couldn't hold back.

Even if the League was footing the bill, consider the one actually making the food—him!

Finally…

As the sun set and the golden glow of twilight painted the sky, Regigigas patted its stomach and let out a burp.

[I'm full~ Hic~!]

Johan and Ninetales then teamed up to give Regigigas a full "sleep aid package."

As Regigigas transformed into a "Stone of Light" and lay quietly on the ground, it marked the official end of this entire event.

A deep exhaustion washed over Johan.

Whether it was battling Regigigas, negotiating with it multiple times, exhausting his Aura through Mega Evolution, or simply cooking an absurd amount of food—he was drained.

He flopped down onto the ground.

"Roto~~"

Rotom, which had emerged from the Berry Mixer, collapsed in his hands. After making so many Pokéblocks, it was completely worn out.

If it weren't for the Thunder Stone Johan had gotten from Raikou, Rotom probably would've shut down halfway through.

"Don't worry, when we get back, I'll get you a whole generator!"

Johan promised.

"R-Roto?"

Rotom froze.

Was this generator…

To help it make even more Pokéblocks in the future?

Johan rubbed his temples.

Even Rotom was getting this sharp?

He was running out of ways to fool it…

But still—

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar suddenly opened its mouth wide.

Under Bertha's watchful gaze, it swallowed an entire top-of-the-line Rotom Berry Mixer worth several million Pokédollars into its dimensional pocket.

Along with all the remaining berries.

Bertha opened her mouth as if to say something… but in the end, she just sighed.

—

Night fell.

Snowflakes once again drifted down, and the restless volcano had returned to peace. The evacuated citizens of Snowpoint City slowly returned.

As streetlights flickered back on, casting a warm orange glow over the roads, and the sound of laughter echoed from homes, Johan and the others exchanged glances.

Smiles spread across their faces.

Flint walked with his hands behind his head, a smug grin on his face.

"You know… we kinda just protected all of Snowpoint City. Heck, maybe even all of Sinnoh."

For once, Lucian didn't argue.

He simply said, "Feels like it was worth the exhaustion."

"I've decided!" Flint suddenly clenched his fist.

"It's time for me to seriously aim for the Elite Four!"

Beside him, Aaron's signature ahoge stood straight up. "Count me in."

Lucian adjusted his glasses with a smirk.

"It's not that easy."

His gaze shifted toward Johan and Cynthia.

"Don't look at me," Johan shrugged. "I'm from Hoenn."

"But Drasna is a member of the Kalos Elite Four," Cynthia blinked, hinting at something.

Drasna was also from Celestic Town—Cynthia knew her well. Yet she had become Kalos' Dragon-type Elite Four member instead of staying in Sinnoh.

"Yeah, yeah!" Flint and Aaron immediately echoed.

After all, Bertha had already invited Johan earlier.

"Strictly speaking, the Elite Four doesn't have to be from the region they serve," Lucian added.

He was already thinking ahead.

Looking at the current state of things…

Everyone here had the potential to become Sinnoh's future Elite Four.

And if they did—Cynthia, with her strength, was a guaranteed Champion.

But the thought of working alongside Flint and Aaron?

Lucian could already see it—

Endless piles of paperwork!

If he could "recruit" Johan into Sinnoh, his workload would be significantly lighter…

As their expectant gazes turned to him, Johan's mouth twitched.

He quickly changed the subject.

"Hey, aren't you guys curious about what Mega Evolution is?"

Everyone's eyes immediately lit up—even Cynthia's.

"Sigh~"

Johan dramatically patted his stomach and gazed up at the sky at a forty-five-degree angle.

"After all this work, I haven't had a single proper meal today…"

"Johan, you definitely snuck in a few Pokéblocks earlier," Flint started—only to be silenced by Johan's glare.

Cynthia smiled sweetly, her eyes crescent-shaped.

"Lucian, you're treating us to dinner."

Lucian stiffened as if struck by lightning, staring at Cynthia in disbelief.

She's been corrupted too!

"Oh yeah!" Flint realized. "Lucian, I'm starving!"

"You— You stay away from me!!"

Lucian bolted.

Flint and Aaron immediately gave chase.

—

Viridian City, Team Rocket Headquarters.

After finishing his training, Giovanni wiped sweat from his forehead with a towel.

He gestured for his Pokémon to take a break, then reached for his phone.

There was a missed call from Matori.

He called back immediately.

"Boss, I sent you some news articles."

"I'm looking now."

Giovanni set down the towel, took a sip of water, and started scrolling through the reports.

"Hm?"

"'Ancient Titan Awakens'?"

The news covered Regigigas' awakening and the volcanic eruption in Snowpoint City.

Of course, to avoid public panic, the League had edited the details as usual.

They also officially recognized and commended Johan, Cynthia, and the others.

"The Regis?" Giovanni asked.

"According to our intel, it should be Regigigas!"

With so many people involved, even the League's censorship couldn't hide everything from Team Rocket's information network.

Matori added, "Johan put it to sleep."

Even Giovanni fell silent for a moment.

Then, after a brief pause—

He chuckled.

"As expected of Johan."

As expected of the person he had taken an interest in.

There was no doubt—Johan was the world's foremost Pokémon psychologist.

Even a legendary Pokémon could be put to sleep.

For most people, that would be unimaginable.

But for Giovanni?

After everything he'd seen with Mewtwo, it wasn't surprising at all.

Matori, however, was still stunned.

Especially having been in Snowpoint City herself—she had felt the sheer force of Regigigas' awakening.

Even from a distance, it felt like an earthquake.

She couldn't begin to imagine how terrifying it would've been up close.

"By the way," Matori hesitated.

"Boss, there's another video."

A reporter had interviewed Team Rocket's infamous trio.

Because of Wobbuffet's contributions, and with Johan's influence, the three had unexpectedly been hailed as heroes.

Looking at the flood of positive comments, Giovanni paused.

Since when… had "Team Rocket" become something the public admired?

Then, realization struck him.

This… was exactly what Johan had suggested—"bringing Team Rocket into the public eye."

And now…

Thanks to Meowth and Pichu, that plan was actually working.

Even while handling Regigigas, Johan had still managed to advance Team Rocket's goals?

Giovanni fell silent for a long time.

Then, he smirked.

"Did Johan say anything else?"

Matori recalled.

"He asked… when we're leaving. He has a gift for you."

A gift?

How long had it been since Giovanni last received a gift?

His smirk deepened.

Now, this… he was looking forward to.

# 357: "Sinnoh is Rich and Clueless, Get Here Fast!"

Sinnoh, Lily of the Valley Island.

The Sinnoh League's headquarters stood tall on this island.

At this moment, inside the League President's office at the top floor of the headquarters, the Sinnoh League President was in a video call with Elite Four member Bertha.

"You're saying that Johan— cough— Professor Johan, along with Cynthia and the others, successfully calmed Regigigas' fury?" the President confirmed.

On the large screen, Bertha nodded solemnly and added:

"To be more precise, Johan successfully put Regigigas to sleep and even established a friendly relationship with it."

She paused briefly before continuing:

"The eruption of Snowpoint Mountain's volcano—under Johan's guidance—was quelled by Regigigas and the three titans."

Hearing this, the Sinnoh League President smacked his lips, momentarily at a loss for words.

After a long silence, he finally asked:

"What's your opinion, Bertha?"

"Recruit Johan," Bertha stated firmly.

"And his response?"

"…He hasn't given a definitive answer."

No definitive answer meant no outright rejection, which meant there was still a chance.

However, given that Johan was from Hoenn, it wouldn't be easy to get him to switch regions.

With Drasna's situation as a precedent, the Sinnoh League had been paying close attention to homegrown talent, even implementing a youth-focused training policy to cultivate young, capable trainers.

Bertha hesitated for a moment before adding:

"However… Johan seems to have a very good relationship with Cynthia, as well as with Lucian, Flint, and Aaron."

"Cynthia, huh?"

The Sinnoh League President's eyes lit up.

That was excellent news.

The League had been monitoring Cynthia for a while now, investing significant resources into her development.

Otherwise, she wouldn't have been invited to participate in so many exhibition matches at League events.

Of course, part of the reason was that Cynthia herself had massive fan appeal.

The President immediately caught on.

"Next time we hold an exhibition match for Cynthia, extend an invitation to Johan as well," he said, grinning.

Sinnoh is rich and clueless—get here fast!

Reimburse his travel and accommodations. Show sincerity.

The more he visits, the more likely he is to stay.

And besides, Cynthia and Johan…

Bertha glanced at the League President's strangely amused expression.

Did he want Johan to stay, or was he just nosy about gossip?

"Oh, right."

Bertha suddenly remembered something.

"I heard that Johan has already obtained the Canalave and Snowpoint Gym Badges. He might be interested in the Lily of the Valley Conference."

"Oh?"

The League President raised his brows in surprise.

"With his level of strength, he's still interested in League tournaments?"

He could fight Regigigas, and he still wanted to compete in the conference?

Bertha pressed her lips together.

"…Maybe it's just a personal hobby of his."

After a pause, she added, "And technically, Johan is still only registered as a beginner trainer in the League database."

The President was speechless.

A beginner trainer?

Someone who could go toe-to-toe with Regigigas??

After a moment of thought, he said,

"Then send him an invitation to the next Lily of the Valley Conference."

Typically, trainers entered the League by collecting Gym Badges or through official academy routes. Those were the two most well-known pathways.

But there was another way: the League could directly invite a trainer, in which case Gym Badges weren't required.

"Understood."

Bertha agreed without hesitation.

"We should also properly reward him for this incident," she added.

Regardless of whether Johan ultimately stayed in Sinnoh, the trainers who had defended an entire city—perhaps even the entire region—deserved recognition and compensation.

The League President nodded.

"I'll put some thought into it."

Bertha nodded in return.

After thinking for a moment, she decided not to mention the fate of the League's top-of-the-line Rotom Berry Mixer.

High or low, it was just a small matter.

After everything Johan had done for Sinnoh, they needed to act with some generosity.

Snowpoint City.

Late at night, a gentle snowfall blanketed the city.

In the villa's courtyard, the group had set up umbrellas and a fire pit, chatting casually while enjoying the snowy night.

Lucian sipped the coffee Johan had brewed, casually crossing his legs as he flipped through a book.

Finally, some relaxation.

No need to bury himself in historical research.

No need to rack his brain strategizing against a powerful opponent.

This was…

"Truly relaxing," Lucian murmured, smacking his lips as he took another small sip.

And Johan's coffee-making skills were fantastic. No wonder he ran a café.

Not just coffee…

Lucian glanced at Cynthia, who was happily nibbling on a slice of cake, her eyes curved into contented crescents.

That cake looked amazing, too.

Damn it. Was he starting to envy Cynthia?

"Mmm, delicious," Cynthia said, licking her lips.

Lucario silently handed her a cup of warm water.

"Thanks."

"Lu-caa~~"

Lucario, ever the gentleman, glanced at Johan, gave him a slight nod, then moved aside to lean against the wall, arms crossed as he gazed at the night sky.

…Did Lucario just approve of him?

Johan chuckled softly, stroking Ninetales' fur as she curled up on his lap.

"I'm glad you like it. This is a cake I developed based on Poké Puffs. It'll be a new item in my café."

As he spoke, he quietly focused on his own Aura.

After repeatedly exhausting and restoring it during the Regigigas incident, it felt like his Aura had become more refined.

Was this… an unexpected breakthrough?

"Gengar's drinking the same thing?" Cynthia asked, blinking.

Both of them turned to look—

And saw—

Ka-kaah! (≧▽≦)

Under the moonlit snow, Gengar was holding a mug, exhaling a frosty breath before taking a slow, satisfied sip.

Its body shuddered slightly—then relaxed in sheer bliss.

Johan's eye twitched.

"…That's, uh… not for humans."

It was something Gengar had brewed itself.

A concoction made from Revival Herbs, a Toxic Orb, and various Poison-type Pokémon's secretions.

According to Gengar—

"One sip wakes you up, two sips give you infinite energy, three sips grant eternal life—OH YEAH!"

Cynthia tilted her head.

"…Did you just drink some?"

"I tasted it before it added the special ingredients," Johan said flatly.

A simple Revival Herb tea. Given how much he had drained himself today, a sip wasn't out of the question.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar waved over the other Pokémon.

"Hey, hey! The moon looks weird tonight!"

Dragonite and Garchomp immediately turned their heads skyward.

The other Pokémon, meanwhile, were busy with their own tasks.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Tinkaton was hammering away.

While filling the volcano, Regigigas had unearthed some metallic ore, which Tinkaton had eagerly gathered.

But now, no matter how hard it hammered, the changes felt… insignificant.

Still, Tinkaton believed in persistence.

One day, I'll forge the strongest weapon in the world!

Charcadet stood nearby, cradling a Pokémon Egg.

With how protective Charcadet had become, Johan was starting to suspect it had a hidden talent for parenting.

"Goka?" Charcadet asked.

When's my armor going to be ready?

After the Regigigas battle, Charcadet had realized just how lacking its strength was.

It had played its part—but not enough.

If I evolve… things might be different.

Kubfu had the same thought.

If I evolve… I could one-shot Regigigas!

But while Charcadet had a clear evolution path, Kubfu…

Kubfu had no idea how it evolved.

"…So what is in that Pokémon Egg?" Cynthia asked, noticing it.

"…I hope it's a Cutiefly," Johan replied, almost in denial.

Cynthia chuckled knowingly.

Just then—

"I've got breaking news!"

Flint burst out of the house, waving his phone.

"The League is rumored to be planning a Youth Masters Tournament!"

A Masters tournament?

A competition specifically for young trainers aiming to reach 'Master' status?

"Is this info reliable?" Lucian asked, setting down his book.

"See for yourself," Flint said, handing over his phone.

Lucian glanced at it—

Then promptly rolled his eyes and tossed it onto the table.

He wasn't sure if the news was true.

But the website Flint was browsing?

It was filled with sketchy clickbait articles—including:

"Unseen Photoshoot of Diantha!"

"Exclusive: Siebold's Secret Recipes!"

"Cynthia's Hidden Secrets?!"

Cynthia's eyes narrowed.

"…There's more of these?"

Johan glanced at the site's name.

And silently memorized it.

# 358: "Gengar’s Boxing Tournament!"

Sinnoh, Galactic Team Base.

Cyrus carefully reviewed the latest intelligence reports from Snowpoint City.

"A resurrected ancient titan… an extinguished volcano…"

His expression grew more serious.

Buzz—

The screen behind him suddenly lit up, displaying a woman with silver hair and large black windproof goggles.

Pokémon Hunter J.

She was standing atop a menacing Salamence, flying at high altitude somewhere.

Without turning around, Cyrus asked,

"Looks like you've gotten out of trouble."

Hunter J's tone was heavy. "But I suffered serious losses."

The League's raid came too suddenly and too swiftly, catching her off guard.

If not for Cyrus secretly reaching out and assisting her, her situation could have been much worse.

She herself might have been trapped.

"I've done all I can to help you," Cyrus said flatly. "Because of my involvement, we may have been exposed. We'll need to keep a low profile for now."

Intercepting Bertha was a calculated move—to lure more League executives to Snowpoint City.

As a result, Team Galactic wasn't exactly exposed… but they had definitely attracted the Sinnoh League's attention.

"I understand. We'll also be lying low for a while," Hunter J replied.

She needed time to replenish her lost personnel and Pokémon.

For that, she had already sent her subordinates out to secretly capture powerful Pokémon for training.

"When the time is right, I'll contact you," Cyrus said.

Hunter J nodded slightly.

Call disconnected.

Cyrus exhaled slowly, lifting his gaze from the tablet screen.

Softly, he muttered:

"…Johan."

Snowpoint City, Ampharos Express.

Given the volcano's instability and Regigigas' slumber, Johan and the others needed to stay in Snowpoint City for a while longer.

So, the group boarded the Ampharos Express, the city's famous scenic train, and set off for Lake Acuity.

Lake Acuity wasn't far from Snowpoint City. If anything happened, they could immediately fly back on their Pokémon.

Lake Acuity, along with Lake Verity and Lake Valor, were three of Sinnoh's most famous inland lakes.

Legends spoke of these lakes…

That when Arceus created the world, it also created Dialga and Palkia, the dragons of time and space.

Then, to bring emotion to the world, it created three beings—Uxie, Mesprit, and Azelf—representing wisdom, emotion, and willpower.

These three Pokémon were collectively known as—

"The Three Lake Mushrooms," Johan said with conviction.

"They're called the 'Lake Guardians,'" Lucian corrected him.

"But they really do look like mushrooms," Flint commented, staring at an ancient illustration.

Johan turned to Aaron and asked,

"Aaron, what do you think?"

Aaron studied the image for a long moment, then nodded seriously.

"…Mushrooms."

The three of them exchanged high-fives and laughed.

Lucian looked helplessly at Cynthia. "Champion, say something reasonable."

As an expert in history and mythology, he trusted Cynthia's judgment.

"Hmmm…"

Cynthia lowered her head slightly, golden hair cascading down, then smiled and said:

"They do look like mushrooms."

"Great eye!" Johan gave her a thumbs-up.

Lucian stared blankly.

I was a fool.

I actually expected Cynthia to support me against Johan?

The problem isn't me—it's this world!

Johan handed Cynthia a delicately crafted cake.

She lit up like a child receiving candy and eagerly took it.

"Mmm! Delicious!"

Lucian looked at Flint and Aaron, who were completely oblivious.

Then he turned to the window.

I shouldn't be inside this train. I should be under it.

Let the world burn.

On the Train.

Ka-kaah~~

Gengar waved enthusiastically at the Ampharos operating the train.

In just one short trip, it had already befriended the train's Ampharos.

Draaa~~!

Speed up!

Ampharos called out to the conductor.

As the train neared Lake Acuity, the vast landscape unfolded before them—pure white snow stretching as far as the eye could see.

Johan clicked his tongue.

"Other people go on spring outings. We're going on a snow trek."

Flint cracked his knuckles. "Nothing a little fire can't fix!"

He tossed a Poké Ball, summoning his Fire-type Pokémon.

"Leave it to me!"

He pounded his chest with confidence.

Johan smirked. "Alright, then bring out your Torkoal."

Flint froze.

"I-I don't have a Torkoal…"

Johan gave him a disdainful side-eye. "And you call yourself a Fire-type expert?"

"W-Why Torkoal?"

Lucian mused, "Torkoal has Drought, making it an excellent weather setter for Sunny Day teams."

Building a Sun Team was a solid strategy for a Fire-type specialist.

Certainly better than a Hail Team.

Hearing this, Flint's eyes gleamed.

A Sun Team, huh? That was worth considering…

…But wait. What about Charizard?!

Did this mean he had to abandon his dream of raising a Charizard?

"Not at all."

Johan pulled out cookware from Gengar's shadow pocket.

"Torkoal is the best mobile stove for outdoor picnics."

Lucian & Flint: "…"

Johan patted Gengar's head and whispered,

"This is the perfect chance to learn."

With Cynthia, Flint, Lucian, and Aaron all here, each with strong, well-trained Pokémon, now was the best time for Gengar to pick up some new tricks.

"Ka-kaah! (≧▽≦)"

You're right!

They had been traveling too much lately—no time to steal techniques.

But now? Now was the perfect opportunity!

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar leaned in and whispered,

"What should we learn?"

"I already scoped it out for you," Johan smirked.

Some key techniques included:

Garchomp's Scale Shot Lucario's martial arts Drapion's Crunch Bronzong's Trick Room setup

And most importantly—

Flint's Infernape.

Flint's Boosted Fire Punch technique was legendary, using Power-Up Punch as a base.

That was something both Gengar and Dragonite could learn.

If they mastered Power-Up Punch and incorporated it into their battle styles…

Yes! This was the path to the ultimate Ghost-type fighter!

Gengar clenched its fists excitedly.

Ka-kaah!

The path to becoming the ultimate Boxing Champion starts NOW!

It shadow-jumped right up to Flint's Infernape and shouted:

"Ka-kaah! Single combat, monkey!"

"Ku?"

Infernape, who had been shoveling snow, blinked.

Me?

A memory surfaced in its mind—Gengar Mega Evolving and hypnotizing Regigigas.

Its expression stiffened.

It liked fighting…

…but not fights where it got hit with five different status effects.

Gengar waved its fists.

"No moves—just fists!"

Ding!

Infernape's eyes sparkled.

A pure fistfight?!

At that moment—

Lucario, Kubfu, Dragonite, Tinkaton, and Scizor all stepped forward.

Even Lucian's Alakazam sighed,

"…I too… know a little hand-to-hand combat."

Johan's expression turned odd.

Fighting a Ghost with hand-to-hand combat?

What were these Pokémon thinking?

…Did they even know they couldn't physically hit Gengar?

And so, the first-ever "Gengar's Boxing Tournament" began!

To everyone's surprise—

Gengar didn't win easily.

Because—

Marshadow showed up.

And Marshadow, with its perfectly copied fighting techniques, wasn't about to lose.

…Of course, its real reason for joining had nothing to do with the delicious aroma coming from Johan's cooking pot.

None at all.

# 359: Giratina’s 'Ecstasy'!

"What… is this?"

On the way back from their "winter outing," Johan looked at the tiny claw Gengar had secretly taken out. He wasn't too surprised.

After all, he knew his Gengar all too well.

For Gengar, venomous fangs, poisonous claws, and toxin-laced spikes from other Pokémon were just after-meal snacks.

This small, dull-looking claw was most likely just another trophy from a Poison-type Pokémon that had tried (and failed) to attack Gengar.

But then—

Johan's Pokédex, which hadn't reacted for a long time, suddenly lit up.

[Uxie's Claw]:

A claw shed by the legendary lake Pokémon, Uxie. Said to contain the light of wisdom, it grants young Pokémon increased intelligence.

(Only those possessing great wisdom can obtain it.)

Johan: "???"

Uxie's Claw?!

His mind filled with question marks.

From THE Uxie? The yellow mushroom of the Three Lake Guardians?

"You…"

Johan opened his mouth slightly.

"Did you steal Uxie's claw?"

He originally wanted to ask, "What did you do to Uxie?" But after thinking about it, he realized—Gengar couldn't possibly harm a legendary Pokémon.

Not to mention that Uxie was a Psychic-type, which naturally countered Gengar.

Gengar, knowing Johan far too well, immediately recognized his stunned expression as a sign that this claw was something unusual.

So, it confidently raised a finger, flashed a smug grin, and lightly wagged it.

"Ka-kaah~~"

It was a gift from Uxie.

"A… gift?"

Gengar proceeded to explain how it met Uxie, how it gave Uxie an energy cube, and how Uxie, in return, gifted it a claw.

"Ka-kaah~!"

Seeing Johan's highly skeptical expression, Gengar immediately vouched,

"Marshadow can confirm it!"

Johan stared at it for a moment.

While Gengar might embellish details, it was probably telling at least 80% of the truth.

Which led to a troubling thought.

When did my Gengar become so good at scamming other Pokémon?

Or rather…

WHEN DID UXIE GO BLIND?!

How did it mistake Gengar for a being of great wisdom?!

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar waved its hand in front of Johan's face.

What's wrong?

"Nothing…"

Johan shook off the thought and looked at the claw again.

Normally, Uxie's Claw wouldn't have much practical use.

At most, it might help certain Pokémon "awaken" their intelligence faster.

But—

This claw was also the key ingredient to creating a certain object.

An object Cyrus had obsessed over.

The Red Chain.

A tool that could bind Dialga and Palkia—

A tool that could CONTROL the gods of time and space.

And Gengar just got it… as a gift?

If Giratina ever finds out about this…

That thing would jump with excitement.

Aside from wanting to beat Arceus senseless, the only thing Giratina seemed to enjoy was bullying Dialga and Palkia.

With this claw, Johan now had a very valuable "bargaining chip" in any future dealings with Giratina.

It had been a while since he last contacted Giratina…

Gengar had probably been keeping in touch with it all this time.

Johan turned to Gengar, who was grinning with a "praise me!" expression.

He patted its head.

Actually, this Uxie's Claw wasn't useless right now.

The Pokédex description said it could make young Pokémon smarter.

And Johan just so happened to have an egg.

If the hatchling ended up as a genius, it would be perfect for his role as a Pokémon psychologist.

"Charcadet, this claw is for the egg."

"Goka?"

Charcadet tilted its head, puzzled.

But it trusted Johan's decisions, so it solemnly nodded.

It strung the claw onto a piece of string and carefully tied it to the egg.

Rustle—

The egg wobbled slightly.

As a Pokémon with the Flame Body ability, Charcadet was accelerating the incubation process.

The little one inside would likely hatch very soon…

The Next Day.

Nothing unusual happened in Snowpoint City.

In fact—

When Battle Pyramid's Head Brandon heard about what happened at Snowpoint Temple, he immediately flew over.

With Brandon stationed here, even if something else happened, he could hold the fort until the League arrived.

That meant—

Johan and the others were finally free to leave.

Before departing, Johan specifically visited Matori and Team Rocket's trio, giving them gifts.

At the same time, he asked Matori to deliver a "special gift" to Giovanni.

As the trio tearfully waved goodbye, Johan boarded the plane and left Snowpoint City.

Arrival at Celestic Town.

Here, the group went their separate ways.

Aaron headed to Eterna Forest, looking for a Bug/Water-type Pokémon. Cynthia stayed behind to finish a historical research report with Professor Carolina. Once the report was done, the League would approve a new round of research funding. Some of that funding was Johan's. (This had absolutely nothing to do with Cynthia's training facility being newly renovated.)

At first, Johan planned to stay and help.

But then—

Flint mentioned he was heading back to Sunyshore City.

And Johan changed his mind.

Because—

Volkner.

Johan hadn't forgotten how Volkner's Pikachu once asked him for help.

Volkner was suffering from severe battle fatigue, and even Cynthia's attempts to help him hadn't fully worked.

So, now that Johan was in Sinnoh… and Flint just happened to be heading back…

He decided to go too.

As for Lucian—

At first, he wanted to return to Canalave City to rest.

He had absorbed too much historical knowledge lately. The constant crisis situations had worn him out.

But then—

After a single conversation, Flint changed his mind.

Flint claimed he couldn't afford to rent a private plane. Meaning he had to go with Lucian to Canalave City first. And Lucian instantly knew—

If Flint followed him home… he'd never leave.

So—

Lucian chose to take the long route.

Johan and Flint secured a free ride. Celestic Town, Departure.

Cynthia stood in the plaza, hands in her pockets, her black trench coat sharp and elegant.

She smiled slightly and gave Johan a small nod.

Johan nodded back, feeling a bit sentimental.

Then, he boarded the plane.

"Rotom, take off!"

"Understood, Roto!"

Vrrrrooom—

The engines roared to life.

…Lucian was filthy rich.

This wasn't just any plane—

It was a high-tech aircraft powered by a Rotom engine.

Johan was already fantasizing about a "Rotom Mecha."

If that ever happened—

"Anti-Regigigas Armor." "Anti-Groudon Suit." "Anti-Arceus Exoskeleton."

Suddenly, these were no longer just dreams.

He might single-handedly rewrite the world's tech tree.

"Hey!"

Flint, face swollen from bruises, suddenly shouted:

"That website from last night—it's gone!"

Johan smirked.

Cynthia deleted it.

She found out.

Luckily, he saved the good parts.

# 360: Johan – Gym Challenge!

Tuesday, clear weather.

Sinnoh, Sunyshore City.

The warm winter sunlight bathed every corner of the city, casting a golden hue over Sunyshore.

As Sinnoh's most famous port city, Sunyshore serves as one of the best routes to reach the Sinnoh League on Lily of the Valley Island, with its ferry service being a prime choice for travelers.

Built on a narrow cape, the city compensates for its limited space by constructing elevated walkways lined with solar panels, providing energy for the entire city.

It is said that Sunyshore wasn't always like this. Although it was a port city, its geographical constraints made development difficult.

It wasn't until Volkner became the Gym Leader and heavily invested in the city's electrical and high-tech infrastructure that Sunyshore transformed into the thriving metropolis it is today.

The name "Sunyshore's Thunder," belonging to Volkner, now resonates throughout Sinnoh.

Walking down Sunyshore's smooth streets with his hands in his pockets like a laid-back wanderer, Flint sighed in admiration.

"Every time I leave and return, I feel like this city has changed so much."

As a fellow native of Sunyshore and Volkner's close friend and rival, Flint had chosen a completely different path.

Volkner stayed behind, dedicated to developing Sunyshore, elevating both the city and his own reputation.

However, Sunyshore had become both Volkner's achievement and his cage—he was bound by it.

Meanwhile, Flint ventured beyond Sunyshore, experiencing different landscapes, battling different trainers, and constantly challenging stronger opponents to grow.

It wasn't a matter of which choice was better.

The fact was that Flint encountered increasingly powerful trainers, and through repeated setbacks and breakthroughs, his passion for battle only intensified.

Meanwhile, Volkner could only face the challengers who came to him. As his strength reached a certain level, those challengers could no longer ignite his fighting spirit—eventually, he even grew weary of battling.

And yet, Sunyshore remained Sinnoh's undisputed "Eighth Gym," and Volkner was widely regarded as one of the strongest contenders for the next Elite Four position.

This alone was enough to prove Volkner's remarkable strength.

In fact, in the animated series, manga, and special editions Johan had seen, Volkner was consistently recognized as being at an "Elite Four level." It wouldn't even be an exaggeration to say that Sinnoh could have five Elite Four members in the future.

That was Volkner—the fourth-generation powerhouse.

"Compared to Volkner, I feel like a complete failure," Flint muttered self-deprecatingly.

"Pfft."

Buck snorted. "For once, you're actually self-aware."

"But lately, Volkner…"

Flint didn't argue with Buck's teasing and instead sighed, clearly more concerned about his friend's condition.

Buck, seeing this, softened his expression slightly.

"Well, Johan's here now."

Both men turned toward the railing, where Johan was feeding Wingull alongside his Gengar and Ninetales.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar grabbed a handful of food—throwing one piece to the Wingull and shoving three or four into its own mouth.

Was it feeding the Wingull, or just feeding itself?

"Buffet?" Johan shook his head. "Let's not go to a buffet."

Gengar had spotted a buffet restaurant earlier and suggested "teaching the owner a lesson," but Johan figured—

One should be kind.

Buffets are dangerous places.

The Wingull hovering nearby looked on helplessly.

Come on, feed us already!

—

On the way to Sunyshore Gym.

Flint, eager, asked, "Johan, how do you plan to 'fix' Volkner?"

Fix him?

Johan had been pondering that very question the entire trip.

Unlike Pokémon, humans have more complicated thoughts. He could try replicating Ash's method from the anime, but…

He wasn't Ash.

So…

"Beat him up first, then ask him what's wrong during the battle?" Johan muttered, rubbing his chin.

After all—

Words don't work as well as Pokémon battles.

"A battle?" Flint's eyes lit up. "Maybe we can do a Double Battle—you and Buck versus me and Volkner!"

Johan blinked.

A Double Battle?

That could work.

Buck also seemed intrigued.

"Or, we could give Volkner a demonstration first. I'll battle Johan, then Buck will battle Johan, and finally, Volkner will—"

…

Wait.

Are you trying to "fix" Volkner, or are you "fixing" me?!

A gauntlet match against me, huh?

"I refuse!" Buck immediately shot down the idea.

"If you want to fight Johan, don't drag me into this!"

He shot a glare at Flint. "You should just challenge Volkner one-on-one."

Flint widened his eyes. "Buck, we're partners! We've been through life-and-death battles together!"

As the two bickered, the trio arrived at the Sunyshore Gym.

The doors were tightly shut.

But next to the entrance sat a box overflowing with Gym Badges—all Lighthouse Badges.

Anyone could just take one.

And yet, despite this, Volkner remained the Gym Leader—testament to his overwhelming reputation and strength.

Flint stepped forward and banged on the door.

"Volkner! It's me, Flint! Open up!"

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar reached into the box, grabbed a Lighthouse Badge, and flashed a victorious hand gesture at Johan.

Yes! I got a Lighthouse Badge!

"Pika?"

The noise drew out a yellow mouse from a side door.

"Pikachu!" Flint grinned.

"Pika~~"

Volkner's Pikachu recognized Flint immediately, waving in greeting.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar noticed Pikachu and grinned, waving back.

Long time no see, buddy!

Pikachu squinted at Gengar for a moment before realization struck.

Wait… wasn't this… the Gastly from back then?!

Its eyes lit up, and it rushed forward.

Flint squatted down with a big smile—only for Pikachu to zip past him and high-five Gengar.

Buck smirked at Flint's frozen expression.

"Idiot."

Flint fumed. "Buck! Let's battle right now!"

Buck scoffed. "Tch."

They had battled countless times before. Flint had challenged Cynthia more, but still—

Their win-loss record?

Fifty-fifty.

Flint's performance fluctuated, while Buck remained consistent.

"Pikachu, where's Volkner?" Johan asked.

"Pika…"

At the mention of Volkner, Pikachu's ears drooped.

It pointed inside the Gym, indicating that Volkner was working on some electrical projects and hadn't noticed the commotion outside.

"That guy…"

Flint gritted his teeth and pounded on the door again.

"Ka-kaah?"

As he knocked, Flint noticed Gengar sticking its head through the door, scratching its head in confusion.

Flint looked at the closed door.

Then at Gengar inside.

"…"

"Guess we'll have to take a more 'direct' approach." Johan sighed.

He turned to Rotom.

"Rotom, open the door."

"No problem! Roto~!"

Rotom zipped out of Johan's Pokédex and dove into the Gym's electrical system.

Click—

With a hiss, the heavy Gym doors slowly opened.

Zzzt—

Golden electricity crackled along the doors and walls.

"Pika!"

Pikachu immediately warned them—it was a security grid! Stay back!

But before Pikachu could finish—

The electric arcs vanished.

In fact, the Gym's power seemed to—

"Lo-lo-lo-lo~ Hic~"

Rotom re-emerged, glowing golden and letting out a satisfied burp.

"Pika?"

Pikachu blinked, a giant question mark forming over its head.

Did… did it just eat all the electricity?!

Even Buck was startled.

This Rotom wasn't even Johan's main Pokémon… and yet, it devoured nearly all of Sunyshore Gym's power?

And considering Gengar's bottomless appetite and the fact that Johan's Dragonite was twice the size of a normal one…

What kind of Pokémon does he even train?!

"Let's go!" Flint strode in confidently.

But—

A second door blocked their way.

"Volkner's security is something else," Buck muttered.

Johan calmly said, "Tinkaton."

A red flash—Tinkaton landed beside him, hammer in hand.

Johan patted its head.

"Smash it."

"Cha!"

With a grin, Tinkaton took a deep breath, raised its hammer—

BOOM!!!

The metal door shattered.

Inside, a voice finally called out—

"Who's there?!"

Johan smirked.

"Gym Challenge!"
