# 361: Johan – How About a Bet?

A Gym Challenge?

Wearing a work jacket and safety goggles, Volkner looked up, slightly surprised.

And…

Everything had been fine just moments ago, so why did the Gym's electricity suddenly vanish?

Compared to the challenge, this issue concerned him more.

His carefully designed Gym circuitry shouldn't have any problems, unless—

Then, he saw Johan, Flint, and Buck standing at the entrance.

"Flint, Buck… and Johan?" Volkner frowned.

"Pika~"

Pikachu ran up to Volkner, gesturing excitedly—there was a strong challenger here.

A Gym challenge?

Sitting on the floor, Volkner took off his goggles and lazily scratched his head.

"The badges should be outside the Gym. If you want one, just take it."

"Volkner!"

Flint called out sternly, "Someone's challenging the Gym—get up and react! Where's your energy?!"

Volkner glanced at his passionate friend and gave a mild smile.

"Aren't I already up?"

Flint clenched his jaw.

"Battle me, Volkner!"

Volkner ignored him.

"Hmm… He doesn't seem quite like the rumored eighth Gym Leader of Sinnoh," Buck remarked.

Neither Flint's encouragement nor Buck's provocation worked.

Meanwhile, Johan, who had just shouted about a Gym Challenge, was now casually surveying the battlefield.

He nodded approvingly.

"This arena looks good."

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar nodded along.

It seemed sturdy—less likely to be destroyed.

"Volkner," Johan suddenly called out.

"Hm?"

Volkner frowned.

They had met in Rustboro City and even worked together.

Later, Johan participated in the Indigo League and speedran the Battle Frontier. Volkner hadn't watched much, but he had heard about him.

He wasn't too familiar with Johan, but he wasn't a stranger either.

"Let's make a bet," Johan said.

"A bet?"

Volkner chuckled. "If I lose, I have to 'get my spirit back'? I think I'm doing just fine."

"No, no, no."

Johan waved a finger, smiling.

"If you win, I'll tell you why your Gym's electricity suddenly disappeared."

Volkner paused.

That actually caught his interest.

He was confident there were no issues with his Gym's electrical system, yet it had completely blacked out. That meant there was a flaw somewhere.

"You know electricity?" Volkner asked, his tone gaining a bit more energy.

"Electricity?"

Johan's mind flashed back to the horrors of Ohm's Law, resistance and conductance, electrical work and power, Joule's Law…

He smirked.

"I know a little."

Flint nudged Buck and whispered, "Why does Johan always 'know a little' about everything?"

Buck adjusted his glasses.

"When most people say 'a little,' they mean it literally. When Johan says it, he's just being humble."

Flint looked enlightened.

Johan felt a bit embarrassed.

This time, he really only knew a little.

But he ignored them and continued,

"Also, if you win, I can introduce you to a few people. In the field of high-tech electrical devices, they're among the best in the world."

Considering the trio's ability to build a mecha by hand, calling them "top-tier" wasn't an exaggeration.

If Volkner teamed up with them…

Johan wasn't sure what kind of wild inventions they'd create.

"Top-tier?"

Were there electrical experts out there he didn't know about?

Seeing Johan's confident expression, Volkner chose to believe him.

"And if you win?" Volkner asked.

"If I win…"

Johan glanced around the Gym's large battlefield and grinned.

"I'd like to borrow this place for a few days."

Volkner blinked.

"That's it?"

Johan nodded. "Yep, that's all."

"No problem!"

Volkner agreed immediately.

For more knowledge about electricity, what was one battle?

"He… agreed just like that?"

Flint watched as Buck pulled him toward the spectator seats.

He had yelled so much earlier, yet Volkner didn't care. But Johan only said a few sentences, and Volkner was in?

"It's psychology," Buck muttered.

He was starting to understand.

Johan had approached Volkner from his biggest interest—electricity—and slowly piqued his curiosity.

To Volkner, the bet seemed slightly unbalanced, but Buck had a feeling Johan might just take over the entire Gym…

He needed to start reading up on psychology—otherwise, he might get manipulated without even realizing it.

Though Volkner hadn't done Gym battles in a while, Sunyshore Gym was still fully equipped.

Hearing there was a battle, the chubby referee rushed back from outside.

"T-The rules for this battle…"

The referee hesitated for a moment before officially announcing the match.

Volkner stood with Pikachu at his side, while Johan's Gengar floated beside him, bouncing lightly on its feet.

"Alright, you're up."

Volkner tossed a Poké Ball.

"Garrr!!"

Out came one of his signature Pokémon—Electivire!

Unlike Volkner's laid-back attitude, his Electivire was brimming with energy. The two cables on its back crackled with sparks.

Johan also threw a Poké Ball.

"Alright, it's you—Tinkaton!"

"Cha!!"

Tinkaton landed with a commanding presence, hammer resting on its shoulder.

Another door to smash?

Wait—

It blinked.

This time, it wasn't breaking doors—it was battling!

"Cha! (Excited!)"

"…Tinkaton?"

Volkner squinted.

That door earlier had been smashed by this seemingly cute little thing, right?

A tiny, pink, twin-tailed Pokémon… with that much strength?

"Volkner seems to be getting serious," Flint observed from the stands, noticing Volkner's shift in expression.

Buck, legs crossed, said casually,

"Well, it is Johan."

That Tinkaton had recently smashed a Regigigas into the ground.

"Battle, begin!"

With the referee's announcement, the battle officially started.

But—

The battlefield fell silent.

Neither Johan nor Volkner made the first move.

Both stood still.

"This isn't an opening attack?!" Flint gritted his teeth.

This was the perfect opportunity! Volkner had really changed.

He felt frustrated.

"Not every opening attack is an advantage. It could also be a trap. Volkner is calmer than you," Buck said.

Johan chuckled.

"So, you're confident that Tinkaton isn't good at ranged combat?"

Then, he commanded,

"Tinkaton, Flash Cannon!"

"Cha!"

Tinkaton's hammer glowed silver, gathering metallic energy before firing a concentrated beam.

Volkner's eyes narrowed as the metallic beam approached.

His expression tensed. He was entering battle mode.

"Electivire, Thunder Punch!"

Electivire's back cables sparked violently, electricity surging like golden serpents into its fists.

"Garrr!!"

With a roar, it threw a punch infused with crackling electricity, colliding directly with the Flash Cannon.

"Interesting," Buck murmured.

Flint clenched his fists.

Boom!

Electivire shattered the beam with its electrified punch and charged forward, electricity crackling from its back, making it move even faster.

"It's here!" Flint whispered. "Volkner's signature Electric Engine acceleration!"

[Electric Engine: This Pokémon is immune to Electric-type moves. When hit by one, its Speed increases by one stage.]

Volkner's Electivire constantly discharged electricity to boost its speed—mimicking the Speed Boost ability.

"Electivire, Wild Charge!"

"Garrr!!"

Electivire became a blur of golden electricity, its amplifier at full power.

Finally interested?

Johan's eyes narrowed.

But this wasn't enough.

"Tinkaton, Play Rough!"

"Cha!"

Tinkaton didn't flinch. It gripped its hammer and swung without hesitation.

Golden lightning clashed with a storm of pink energy.

BOOM!

Electricity and shockwaves exploded outward, sending both trainers' clothes fluttering.

In Volkner's pale blue eyes, a glint of battle spirit finally emerged.

This was…

A real opponent!

Electivire grinned viciously, its eyes burning with excitement.

It had been too long.

Too long since it had faced a fight like this!

"Garrr—!!"

Electivire roared, its body erupting with golden light, illuminating the battlefield.

Even the Gym's power—previously drained by Rotom—started to flicker back on.

"Nice one, Electivire!" Flint stood up.

"Volkner! Look at your Pokémon—it craves battle!"

Volkner inhaled sharply.

"In that case…"

"Electivire, Gigavolt Havoc!"

Zzzzt—

Its amplifier roared at maximum output.

"Now that's more like it," Johan grinned, turning serious.

"Tinkaton, Gigaton Hammer!"

This wasn't about strategy anymore.

It was a direct clash of power!

"Cha!"

Tinkaton stomped forward—

CRACK—

The ground beneath it fractured.

With a mighty swing, its hammer became a blur, meeting Electivire head-on.

Lightning and steel collided.

BOOM!

# 362: This Isn't a Ride to Kindergarten!

You guys beat Naruto. Congrats. 10 extra chapters coming when powerstones reset. I'm tired as I am writing this, if this doesn't get to the top of the powerstone rankings. I don't know what I'll do. Anyways, save the powerstones until then. Thank you.

------

"Pika~ Pika~!"

Volkner's Pikachu, his very first partner, could sense the shift in his attitude more than anyone else.

From initial laziness to being completely absorbed in battle alongside Electivire, Volkner's buried fighting spirit had started to reignite bit by bit.

Though he wasn't fully back to his old self, he had already changed significantly from before.

That made Pikachu excited.

At the same time, its own battle instincts flared up.

After all, it had once rivaled Flint's Monferno. Its desire and enthusiasm for battle weren't any less than Flint's Infernape.

So—

Pikachu's eyes sparkled as it turned to Volkner, requesting to fight.

Volkner blinked, his expression slightly dazed.

Had he been so oblivious?

His Pokémon all loved battling this much?

Had they simply suppressed their instincts out of consideration for him?

Electivire was like that. And now, it seemed Pikachu was the same.

Taking a deep breath, Volkner nodded.

"In that case, you're up, Pikachu."

"Pika!"

Pikachu shivered with excitement, a smile spreading across its face as it patted its cheeks. Sparks flickered from its red electric sacs.

Then, it ran onto the battlefield.

Pikachu, huh?

Johan recalled Tinkaton and, after a moment of thought, threw another Poké Ball.

"Alright, I'll leave this to you—Charcadet."

"Goka!!"

Charcadet glanced at the Pokémon Egg in Gengar's arms and immediately became even more fired up.

Fetal education starts early! If the unborn little one could sense its passion, it would surely grow up to be a strong and reliable warrior!

"Charcadet?"

Volkner instinctively looked toward the stands, locking eyes with Flint.

Charcadet's burning determination reminded him of Flint's Monferno from the past.

Flint exaggeratedly wiggled his eyebrows at Volkner.

Volkner returned a confident nod.

Volkner: I understand, my friend.

Flint: No, you don't! Be careful of that Charcadet! Be very careful!

The battle continued!

"Charcadet, Flamethrower!" This time, Johan didn't wait for Volkner to act first—he took the initiative.

"Goka!"

Like a glowing ember, Charcadet's body turned red-hot as it clasped its hands together.

A surge of orange flames erupted from its palms!

The firelight reflected in Volkner's eyes.

And—

It ignited something in his heart.

A roaring pillar of flames burst forth, scorching the battlefield with an intense wave of heat!

This power…

Volkner finally understood Flint's warning glance.

But more than that—

This fire dragged him back to the past.

To when he faced off against that relentless, red-haired explosion of energy.

For the first time today, a smile appeared on Volkner's face.

"Pikachu, Quick Attack to evade—then Wild Charge!"

"Pika! Pika! Pika—!!"

A white light flashed over Pikachu's body as it deftly dodged the heart of the flames.

Then, golden electricity surged around it.

Turning sharply, it bolted toward Charcadet—switching from defense to offense!

It relied, of course, on Pikachu's innate, unrivaled speed.

"His reactions are much sharper than before. He quickly spotted a weak point in the fire and exploited it," Buck commented.

Flint grinned.

"Because that guy understands fire. I doubt anyone knows better than him how to fight a Fire-type Pokémon."

Humming—

One of Flint's Poké Balls opened on its own, and Infernape appeared beside him.

As an old friend and rival, Infernape wanted to witness Volkner's return too.

"Ku!"

"Right?"

Flint glanced at Infernape before turning his attention back to the battlefield.

"He's Volkner, after all!"

Volkner, huh?

Buck nodded slightly.

A faint smile crept onto his face.

"But his opponent is Johan!"

"Charcadet!" Johan called.

"Goka!!"

Charcadet took a deep breath.

Its eyes burned bright as waves of Aura radiated outward.

At the same time, its body glowed an even deeper red.

Whoosh!!

The fire Pikachu had seemingly dodged suddenly surged back like a living tide!

"Again…" Flint murmured.

His gaze drifted toward Infernape.

Should Fire-types really be eating charcoal?

"Ku?"

Infernape felt a sudden chill.

Meanwhile—

"Pikachu!"

Seeing the flames close in, Volkner's eyes widened.

"Pika! Pika!"

Within the inferno, Pikachu gritted its teeth, forcing itself through the blaze despite the searing pain.

Volkner had finally regained his fire.

Now wasn't the time to let him down!

"Pika~chu!!"

With sheer determination, Pikachu sprinted through the fire, slamming straight into Charcadet.

Boom!!

Charcadet was sent flying, but Pikachu also staggered upon landing, steam rising from its body as its fur turned slightly red.

It had been burned!

But its eyes remained unwavering.

Breathing heavily, Pikachu locked its gaze onto Charcadet.

"Pikachu…" Volkner murmured.

"Goka—"

Charcadet steadied itself and gave Pikachu a respectful nod.

That kind of resilience deserved recognition!

"Pikachu, Electro Ball!" Volkner commanded.

A trainer's emotions influenced their Pokémon.

But in turn, their Pokémon's emotions also influenced them.

Electivire… Pikachu…

He had trained them to this level.

He had spent every day with them.

How could he not understand their thoughts?

Especially now—

Watching Pikachu carve its way through fire.

Volkner felt something stir inside him.

Right now—

He only wanted to respond in kind.

Pikachu leaped high, gathering a golden sphere of electricity at the tip of its tail before launching it at Charcadet.

"Pika~chu!"

"The best way to counter lightning—is fire!"

Johan's eyes sharpened.

"Charcadet, Flare Blitz!"

Whoosh!!

Charcadet's plume shimmered.

Golden flames spread across its forehead before engulfing its entire body.

Boom!!

A massive explosion of dust, embers, and electric arcs filled the battlefield.

Volkner clenched his fists, eyes locked onto the blast's center.

Did Charcadet get hit?

The next second—

"Goka—!!"

Bathed in flames, Charcadet shot out of the smoke, slamming straight into Pikachu before it could react.

"Pikachu!"

Volkner rushed forward and caught Pikachu midair.

But Charcadet's momentum wasn't easy to stop.

Man and Pokémon crashed into the Gym's spectator wall.

Johan twitched.

Why were Pikachu trainers always like this?

Just because you're physically strong doesn't mean you should play reckless!

Referee, you're just gonna let this slide?!

Foul play!

But that impact—

It jolted something deep in Volkner's memory.

He remembered—

When he and Flint took on a poacher as kids.

When they lost.

When Pikachu was flung through the air.

And he had caught it in his arms, just like this.

"Pika…"

Pikachu quickly turned over, worriedly looking at Volkner.

"I'm fine."

Volkner shook his head.

Fine?

The wall was literally cracked.

He stumbled slightly but stood up, glancing at Johan with a look of apology.

"Sorry, Johan…"

Johan waved it off.

His goal had been to wake Volkner up.

Beating him up outright wouldn't work—Cynthia had already tried that, and it didn't help much.

So he had chosen Charcadet to face Pikachu.

A Fire vs. Electric battle to spark Volkner's memories.

Because fire ignites.

Both the opponent's will and one's own.

Volkner took a deep breath and nodded.

He and Pikachu locked eyes.

They didn't need words.

They had one goal—

Enjoy this battle to the fullest!

"Then… evolve, Pikachu!"

Johan: "??"

Volkner pulled out a Thunder Stone.

He had prepared this long ago but never used it.

Now—

It was the perfect time.

"Pika!!"

Without hesitation, Pikachu touched the Thunder Stone with its tail.

A radiant light burst forth.

"Rai~Rai!"

When the glow faded, Pikachu was gone.

In its place stood a larger, orange-furred Pokémon with a sleek, lightning-shaped tail—

Raichu!

Golden arcs of electricity flickered across its body.

"Rai~Rai!!"

Raichu charged back onto the battlefield.

And now—

The real fight was about to begin.

# 363: It’s Just an Emotionless Cameraman

You guys beat Naruto. Congrats. 10 extra chapters coming when powerstones reset. I'm tired as I am writing this, if this doesn't get to the top of the powerstone rankings. I don't know what I'll do. Anyways, save the powerstones until then. Thank you.

------

"Luxray is unable to battle. The winner is—the challenger, Johan!"

The referee wiped the corner of his eye as he announced the final result.

It had been so long since he last declared the outcome of a Gym battle. The wait had nearly made him gain weight from stress.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar waved at the now-conscious Luxray.

"Garrr—"

Luxray eyed it warily, its fur standing on end.

How was it that Electivire and Raichu had such exhilarating battles, while its opponent was Gengar?

It liked battling too, but it never wanted to fight Gengar again.

Too dirty!

Gengar immediately shook its head—Unacceptable!

Your Focus Sash can't compare to my Toxic Orb—besides, you can't even eat it!

That's when Luxray realized… it still had the Toxic Orb.

No wonder it felt weak right after eating a Sitrus Berry.

"Johan, I know you probably don't need it, but please accept this badge and this TM as a reward."

Volkner felt a bit drained now.

The first two battles had been great, but the third one? He wasn't used to that kind of fight.

Still, he couldn't deny that this battle had rekindled some of his old passion.

More importantly, it reminded him—there were so many powerful trainers out there waiting for him to challenge.

Strength shouldn't be confined to one's comfort zone.

There were strong trainers all around.

Flint, Buck…

Volkner's gaze landed on Johan and his Gengar, and his heart trembled slightly.

And Cynthia.

Yes—Cynthia.

Johan had successfully redirected Volkner's desire for a rematch onto Flint and the others—by traumatizing him with Gengar.

The TM he handed over was Charge Beam.

[Charge Beam: An Electric-type move with 50 base power. Due to its charge effect, it has a high chance of boosting the user's Special Attack.]

An offensive move that also had a buffing effect.

Though… that 70% chance was pretty unreliable.

For Johan, rounding that down was basically zero.

"If you pair Charge Beam with a Thunder Stone or a Magnet, its learning speed, effectiveness, and even its buffing success rate can improve," Volkner explained, even handing over a Charge Beam Learning Manual.

For Johan, the only Pokémon that could learn it was Rotom.

But Rotom… wasn't exactly into battling.

So—into storage it went.

Volkner continued,

"I already used my Thunder Stone, so take this instead."

He pulled out a special item—an Electirizer!

The evolutionary item that turned Electabuzz into Electivire.

"Wow, generous."

Johan chuckled and accepted it without hesitation.

Nice—Volkner knew how to be a good host.

As a Gym Leader, backed by Sunyshore's resources, plus the entire city's support, Volkner had way more wealth than broke Flint.

"Rotom, here's that generator I promised you."

Johan handed the still-warm Electirizer straight to Rotom.

Now, home electricity bills were a thing of the past.

And Rotom could probably run 24/7 now…

Tsk. So much money saved.

"Lo-Lo-Lo~(Д`)"

Rotom accepted it with tears in its eyes, unsure whether to be happy or sad.

"And this too."

Johan gave it the Charge Beam TM and the learning manual.

It could learn if it wanted to. If not, no pressure.

"Rotom?"

Volkner finally noticed it.

As an Electric-type specialist, he was very familiar with Rotom.

Recently, Rotom had been appearing everywhere—powering appliances, furniture, and even entire devices.

He had always wanted to catch one to assist in his research.

But… he had been too lazy to bother.

"Wait—don't tell me the Gym's power…"

Volkner's eye twitched.

Johan didn't even bother hiding it.

"Yep. Rotom ate it."

"E-EAT it?"

Volkner's eyes widened.

He knew how much power his Gym stored.

It ran on a complex electrical system, fueling countless mechanisms.

The sheer amount of energy inside was enough to power all of Sunyshore City for a short time.

And Rotom had eaten it.

"Uh… sorry, Roto!" Rotom stammered.

"N-No, it's not your fault…" Volkner muttered.

"Calm down."

Flint stuffed his hands into his pockets and smirked. Now that Volkner had snapped out of his slump, he was back to his usual slacker self.

He added, "Johan's Rotom was recognized by Raikou."

Raikou?!

Volkner stiffened.

Anyone into Electric-types knew about Raikou.

In Pokémon Adventures, Lt. Surge even used Raikou to power a magnetic train.

"Roto? (σ□｀)σ"

Rotom stared at Johan.

You sure we should be saying this?

Also, where did it get Raikou's recognition?

Didn't Tinkaton steal something off Raikou?

Flint, don't hype me up like this—I'm scared…

I'm just an emotionless cameraman.

Buck patted Volkner's shoulder.

"Don't get too excited. That Charcadet you fought? It has Entei's blessing."

Entei?!

Volkner instinctively turned to Flint.

"Don't look at me!" Flint blurted.

The way Volkner stared at him made him feel like a fraud.

How could a Fire-type specialist not have Entei's recognition?!

Unfazed, Buck continued,

"And that Gengar? It put Regigigas to sleep."

Volkner: "…"

Flint blinked.

"Oh, and Tinkaton smashed Regigigas."

Volkner took a deep breath.

He had only locked himself in his Gym for a while…

Why does it feel like the whole world changed?!

Hah! Cynthia—I'll challenge you again someday!

Seeing Volkner's overwhelmed expression, Buck and Flint exchanged glances.

Another one bites the dust.

"You guys are exaggerating," Johan muttered.

At this rate, people would think he killed Regigigas.

He scoffed, "Why not just say I kidnapped Dialga and Palkia?"

Volkner's eyes widened.

Buck calmly replied, "That part's fake."

Volkner sighed in relief.

But Flint, recalling Johan's power of manifesting nonsense into reality, mumbled,

"…It might become real."

Johan shot him a glare before walking off to treat his Pokémon's minor injuries.

Tinkaton and Charcadet had taken a few hits, but Gengar? Not a scratch.

Meanwhile, Gengar was discussing potential photo spots with Rotom.

Sunyshore's lighthouse looked nice.

And—

There was a giant Pokémon-shaped rock in Sunyshore, shaped like a Snorlax.

It'd make for a cool shot.

"Roto~()"

Rotom's eyes sparkled.

"Ka-kaah~~" Gengar grinned.

"By the way, Rotom, when are you gonna try battling?"

"M-Me, Roto?"

Rotom looked horrified.

"C-Can I, Roto?"

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar waved it off with a smile, saying it was just a joke.

But…

It might actually be strong.

Rotom didn't like battling, so Johan never forced it.

But every time the others trained, Rotom watched and followed along for better camerawork.

Which meant…

It had unknowingly trained with them.

Johan never underfed it either.

It ate high-quality Pokéblocks, absorbed Raikou's energy, learned new moves, and now had Charge Beam and an Electirizer.

Gengar was convinced—

Rotom wasn't weak.

After treating Tinkaton and Charcadet, Johan approached Volkner.

"I'd like to use your battlefield for training."

Since it was part of their bet, Volkner agreed immediately.

"No problem."

He even offered, "I've installed special mechanisms for training. You're free to use them, but…"

He frowned.

The Gym's electrical systems were shot.

They needed repairs.

See?

This is why high-tech stuff is great.

Automated training facilities? That'd boost progress instantly.

"Thanks, Volkner," Johan said sincerely.

Volkner shook his head.

"I should be the one thanking you, Johan. You reminded me to listen to my Pokémon again."

Johan patted his shoulder.

"We're partners. Growth is all about mutual understanding."

Then, he turned.

"Rotom, power mode!"

"Roger, Roto!"

Rotom zipped into the Gym's circuits.

BZZT!

The Gym's systems roared back to life.

Volkner blinked.

That power demand was insane.

"Johan… is Rotom your main Electric-type?"

Johan yawned and stretched.

"Nah. It's just an emotionless cameraman."

Volkner: "…"

Johan clapped his hands.

"Alright—training starts now!"

And at that moment—

Volkner finally realized…

The bet had always been fair.

# 364: The Hungry Duo (BONUS)

Sinnoh, Sunyshore City.

"Is Gym Leader Volkner renovating the Gym again?"

"This time, it feels even more intense than before…"

"Maybe he's working on some new tech? Feels like it's about to explode—let's get out of here!"

"…"

Outside the Gym, Volkner listened to the constant rumbling from inside and the passing pedestrians' chatter.

For a moment, he didn't know what to say.

"Today is… the third day?" Volkner muttered.

Suddenly, he felt impressed—both with himself and with the Gym he had built.

It had managed to last three days under Johan's training.

That's actually… incredible!

Flint patted him on the shoulder in consolation.

"It's fine. Out with the old, in with the new—just take it as completely shedding your past, lazy self."

Volkner gave him a look.

Easy for you to say.

It's not your Gym.

Not that he really cared about the damages.

He hadn't done any Gym battles for so long that all the League's allocated maintenance funds had just been sitting there untouched.

He had plenty to burn.

But he was curious—

What kind of training could cause this much destruction?

So… when Johan battled him, was he holding back?

"By the way, I heard that restaurant is holding another event. Want to check it out?" Flint suggested.

"Seven Stars Restaurant?"

Volkner immediately knew which one he meant.

Seven Stars Restaurant was the most upscale eatery in all of Sinnoh.

Many of the region's wealthiest people dined there.

It was also a famous battle restaurant—but unlike others, it specialized in Double Battles.

Every so often, Seven Stars Restaurant would hold a special event:

Win a Double Battle, and you could eat there for free.

Back before Flint left Sunyshore and Volkner became a Gym Leader, they used to frequent the place for free meals.

It was also where they had first shared their dreams and aspirations.

Flint figured—Volkner needed to reaffirm those aspirations.

And conveniently, the event was happening again.

"What about Johan?"

Volkner glanced toward the Gym as Johan emerged with his Pokémon, seemingly finishing up training for the day.

"He'll come too."

Flint grinned.

"Johan loves food—whether he's cooking it himself or tasting someone else's."

At the same time—

Johan was speaking to Dragonite.

"…That's the gist of it. Next time there's an Earthquake, feel the earth's flow first…"

Giovanni's book The Secrets of the Earth was finally proving useful after Dragonair's evolution.

Dragonite had become the only Pokémon on Johan's team capable of using multiple Ground-type moves.

It was a huge boost to his team's overall coverage.

Plus—

Volkner had built specialized training machines to help his Electric Pokémon resist Ground-type moves.

Perfect for Dragonite's training.

Gotta admit, Volkner's traps and gadgets were very useful for practice.

"Draaa~~"

Dragonite nodded earnestly.

During training, it had already gotten its tail stuck in Volkner's machines five times.

"Johan, want to grab a meal?" Flint called out.

"Ka-kaah!"

At the mention of food, the Pokémon instantly perked up.

"Sure."

Johan had no reason to refuse.

Who turns down food?

But—

"I mean… it's just a meal. Do we really have to bike this far?"

The sun was setting.

Johan, on a bicycle, sighed.

When Flint and Volkner took him along on their bikes, he had felt that something was off.

Why were they leaving the city just for food?

By the time they had been pedaling since noon until dusk, he knew—

He had been scammed.

Besides—

"If the restaurant is that far, why not drive?"

"Cycling is good for your health!" Flint beamed, flashing a thumbs-up.

"Back then, Volkner and I rode our bikes to Seven Stars. We're just reliving our childhood!"

You two are reliving memories—so why am I being dragged along?!

At that moment—

Johan swore to get himself a Rotom Bike.

"Where's Buck?" Johan asked, noticing he wasn't with them.

"Oh, Buck took a car earlier to reserve a table," Volkner said, smiling innocently.

Johan: "…"

You had a car all along?!

Don't think that just because you're smiling like a proper Sinnoh Champion I'll let this go!

Flint, you're on my list.

"We're here!"

Flint pointed at a grand, luxurious building—

Seven Stars Restaurant.

"Exquisite flavors beyond your imagination!"

That was their slogan.

Located by Lake Valor, Seven Stars Restaurant was part of a complex known as Lakefront Hotels.

Unlike Snowpoint's frozen Lake Acuity, Lake Valor had warm, resort-style buildings.

Every year, travelers and Sinnoh locals flocked to dine there.

Especially trainers—

Because a win in a Double Battle meant—

Free food.

Unlike buffets, which still aimed to make a profit, this restaurant's dishes were normally expensive.

But only during special events did battles determine whether you could eat for free.

It was simply the owner's personal hobby.

That meant—

Johan had no moral guilt about unleashing his Pokémon here.

When they arrived, long tables lined the restaurant's outdoor corridors, covered in food.

And—

It looked like a buffet.

Flint explained,

"These are just appetizers. The restaurant owner prepares them so every participating trainer and Pokémon can be in top condition before their battles."

Pure luxury.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Upon hearing this, a plump, shadowy hand extended from Johan's own shadow—

And swiped an entire table's worth of desserts, plates included.

The only thing left behind was a neatly stacked tower of empty plates.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Came the sound of Gengar munching.

Johan casually picked up a piece of cake and took a bite.

"Leave some for the others, Gengar."

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar's voice echoed from the shadow—it had already saved portions for the rest.

"Amie?"

Volkner suddenly spotted someone at a corner table—

With a mountain of empty plates in front of her.

Long chestnut hair tied into twin braids, a light blue dress—

A girl who looked extremely gentle and elegant.

Olivine Gym Leader Amie.

Her signature Pokémon were an Ampharos named "Lumi" and a massive Steelix.

People called her "The Girl of Impenetrable Defense."

"V-Volkner?"

Amie turned pink, hastily explaining,

"T-These were left by the last diner!"

She mumbled at the pile of plates.

Despite her delicate appearance—

Amie was a legendary big eater.

She was just too shy to admit it.

Johan glanced at her table, then at Gengar's stash.

Hmm.

Probably only Gengar could out-eat her.

Johan did remember her, though—

From the days of HeartGold & SoulSilver, when he spent ages looking for medicine for her Ampharos.

Sailing endlessly.

Battling dozens of Swimmers.

Volkner chuckled and introduced her.

"This is Flint. And this is Johan."

"Johan?"

Amie's eyes sparkled.

"It's an honor to meet you, Johan!"

She wasn't unfamiliar with him.

Partially because of battle livestreams—

But mostly because her best friend Erika constantly talked about him over the phone.

Flint jabbed a thumb at himself.

And me?

"Nice to meet you, Amie," Johan greeted her.

"Amie is the Olivine Gym Leader. She visits Sunyshore often," Volkner added.

"B-Because the lighthouse here reminds me of Olivine's…" Amie quickly emphasized.

Not because of Seven Stars' "Battle-to-Eat-Free" promotion.

If even Amie had come from Johto for this event—

It really showed how famous the restaurant was.

Just as Johan had this thought—

A voice called out from behind him.

"Johan?!"

Turning around, he saw a pink-haired girl in a blue tank top, white pants, barefoot, with a bandage on her nose.

"Maylene?"

Johan recognized her immediately.

Veilstone Gym's Fighting-type Leader—

Another human Machamp.

# 365: How Can an Alcremie Be This Cute? (BONUS)

Maylene hadn't been a Gym Leader for long—one could even say she had just taken on the role.

Before that, her life had been full of hardship.

Her father was a gambling addict who spent his days at Veilstone Game Corner, racking up massive debts.

As a result, Maylene not only had to train her Pokémon but also shoulder her father's debts, bearing a crushing burden at a young age.

In Pokémon Adventures, her poverty was even more extreme—often training on an empty stomach while convincing herself it was part of her hardcore discipline.

She earned the title "The Barefoot Fighting Genius" for a reason.

But she never gave up.

Instead, she pushed herself further, growing stronger.

With Sinnoh League's push for younger Gym Leaders, along with her sheer willpower and talent, Maylene was appointed as Veilstone's new Gym Leader.

However—

Even before that, she had frequently teamed up with Jasmine to challenge Seven Stars Restaurant.

For different reasons, both of them could only eat properly here.

The outgoing Maylene and the reserved Jasmine—

Their fates intertwined, and they naturally became partners.

Seeing them together, Johan started to understand why so many Gym Leaders had side jobs.

A Gym's salary was enough to raise a full team of Pokémon—

But it wasn't necessarily enough to raise them properly.

Especially for a newly appointed Gym Leader like Maylene.

"Maylene, you should consider picking up a side gig," Johan suggested.

Maylene scratched her nose sheepishly.

"I have! I've washed dishes, done laundry, farmed, worked as a cleaner… but I kept messing up."

Maylene was naturally strong, but she never realized it.

So whenever she took on those jobs, her sheer strength would cause disasters.

Plus, with her personality, she struggled with most jobs.

Fortunately, now that she was a Gym Leader, her life should become a lot easier.

"It'll get better," Johan reassured her, patting her shoulder.

There wasn't much else he could do to help—

But…

That gambling-addicted father of hers?

Johan could keep an eye on him.

No way that guy was still going to cause trouble with League police and Interpol watching.

Besides—

Veilstone City was home to one of Team Galactic's bases.

"Mm!" Maylene nodded firmly, full of determination.

—

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar sniffed the air, drawn to a dessert station in the corner of the plaza.

This was where most of the sweets were coming from.

A group of chefs in white uniforms bustled around, whipping up fresh desserts and sending them to different tables.

But—

Gengar's attention was locked on something else.

Sitting atop the dessert table—

A tiny Pokémon covered in whipped cream, decorated with strawberries, resembling a delicate little cake.

An Alcremie!

It held a tiny spoon, carefully scooping some cream from its own body and spreading it over a cake handed to it by a pastry chef.

All the while, it hummed a happy little tune.

"Ka-kaah~! (≧▽≦)"

It smells so good!

Gengar stared at the little thing and gulped.

"Y-Mai?"

Noticing the hungry ghost staring at it, Alcremie shuddered.

Thinking fast, it quickly scooped a bit of its own cream and flung it at Gengar.

CHOMP!

Gengar caught it midair—devouring it in one bite.

"Ka-kaah! (っ´ω`)っ"

Delicious!

"Y-Mai?"

Alcremie tilted its head, then sighed in relief, patting its chest.

Luckily, the cream it threw had calming effects—once consumed, the target should lose all will to fight.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar opened its mouth and pointed inside, signaling—

More, please.

"Y-Mai…"

Alcremie glanced at the nearby cakes, torn between its job and its survival.

Gengar's eyes gleamed mischievously—

And suddenly, it made a spooky face.

"Y-MAI!! (≧□≦)"

Alcremie panicked and flung another scoop of cream.

CHOMP.

Gengar devoured it instantly.

At that moment—

Gengar figured it out.

Scare it, get more cream.

"Ka-kaah~~"

"Y-Mai!!"

"Ka-kaah."

"Y-Mai!"

"Ka—"

"Y—!"

"…"

"Alcremie?"

Finally noticing Gengar's disappearance, Johan turned and saw the whole thing.

"Ka-kaah~!"

Gengar waved happily.

Johan, come try this cream—it's amazing!

Johan's forehead twitched.

Couldn't Gengar see that poor Alcremie was on the verge of tears?!

And the moment it spotted Johan—

"WAH!!"

Alcremie broke down crying.

"Y-Mai! ｡ﾟ(ﾟ´Д｀ﾟ)ﾟ｡"

Gengar froze—then panicked.

"Ka-kaah (Д`)ヽ(￣ω￣)"

Frantically, it grabbed a handful of Pokéblocks and tossed one into Alcremie's mouth.

"Y-Mai?!"

Alcremie instinctively chewed—

And immediately stopped crying.

It blinked.

Sniffled.

Then, its eyes curved into little crescent moons.

"Y-Mai! (＞＜)☆"

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar laughed happily, patting Alcremie on the head.

Then, realizing its hand was now covered in cream—

It promptly licked it clean.

Johan rubbed his chin, studying the cream-scented, vanilla-flavored Alcremie.

Alcremie was a unique Pokémon.

It could produce whipped cream, and the happier it was, the sweeter and more fragrant the cream became.

For this reason, owning an Alcremie was the dream of pastry chefs everywhere.

Its cream could be used for cakes, cookies, pastries—anything.

And in battle, it had the incredibly powerful Decorate move.

[Decorate: Greatly boosts the target's Attack and Special Attack.]

A double stat-boost—on par with Mustard's Coaching!

Plus, outside of battle, Decorate could make any food look more appetizing.

Most Alcremie weren't fighters.

Instead, when facing danger, they threw cream at their opponents, using its calming effects to neutralize threats.

Gengar had completely exploited this loophole, making poor Alcremie throw cream over and over.

And yet—

The calming cream had zero effect on Gengar.

"This Alcremie is amazing, isn't it?"

Huh?

Johan turned.

A young man—wearing a white suit, with short light-blue hair, exuding an air of nobility, around twenty-three or twenty-four years old—

Had appeared behind him.

"Wait… Are you Johan?"

The young man looked surprised upon recognizing him.

Johan raised an eyebrow.

The man smiled and introduced himself.

"I'm Thorne, owner of Seven Stars Restaurant."

Oh—so this was the boss.

Seven Stars' "Battle-to-Eat-Free" event was his idea, created alongside his fiancée, Chloe.

Johan shook his hand.

"So, you're Thorne."

Then, he glanced at Alcremie.

"This Alcremie—is it yours?"

There were pastry chefs nearby, but…

None of them seemed to be Alcremie's trainer.

Otherwise, they would have stopped Gengar already.

Thorne smiled and shook his head.

"No, it's an employee of the restaurant."

An employee?

Thorne explained—

Alcremie had originally worked at a bakery, but it had been miserable, leading to flavorless cream and eventually, the bakery's closure.

Afterward, Thorne took it in.

Over time, with proper care, Alcremie became happier, and its cream grew sweeter and richer.

"I see…"

Johan nodded—then frowned slightly.

A suspicion crept into his mind.

"That bakery owner… was he a—"

Johan didn't finish the sentence, mindful of Alcremie listening.

But Thorne understood.

He sighed.

"Yes. He bought it from a hunter."

Johan fell silent.

Another case of Pokémon poachers…

And yet—

At that moment, Gengar and Alcremie were feeding each other snacks and cream.

And Gengar was absolutely smitten.

How can an Alcremie be this cute?!

Johan's expression turned… odd.

Wait.

Was Gengar bribing Alcremie with food… in exchange for cream?!

Gengar had unlocked the ultimate food transaction.

Thorne chuckled.

"It seems happy."

And the happier Alcremie was, the tastier its cream became.

Johan suddenly had a thought.

Should he try…

Stealing Thorne's employee?

# 366: Johan: I Alone Can Take You All On! (BONUS)

Johan gently placed his hand on Alcremie's head.

"Y-Mai~ (≧▽≦)"

S-So comfy!

Feeling Johan's Aura, Alcremie was instantly overwhelmed by a sensation of pure bliss.

Sensing that something was off, Thorne immediately suggested:

"As Trainers, Johan, we should settle this in the way of Trainers!"

The way of Trainers?

Johan exchanged a glance with Gengar.

Then both of them grinned.

—

The Seven Stars Restaurant Tag Battle Tournament was taking place at Lake Valor's shores.

Since the number of participants wasn't always an even number—

And even when it was, randomly pairing Trainers might prevent them from performing at their best—

The tournament had a flexible rule:

The default format was tag battles. However, a single Trainer could enter alone—as long as they controlled two Pokémon at once.

—

"You're already in Canalave City?"

Holding his phone, Johan looked stunned.

Had Fitzwilliam already reached Canalave City?

No wonder…

No wonder Fitzwilliam had left early in the morning, while Johan and the others had only set out in the afternoon.

And on the other end of the call—

Fitzwilliam, who had just realized that Johan wanted to team up with him for the tournament, was absolutely regretting his life choices.

This could've been a free win!

Why had he rushed off so fast?!

He could've stayed back, teamed up with Johan, and completely steamrolled through the competition…

And maybe even bullied Flint a little in the process.

Huge mistake.

"Forget it," Johan sighed.

"Looks like I'll have to go solo."

He turned to look at his Pokémon.

Even though teaming up with Fitzwilliam would've been a solid strategy—since their battle styles were quite compatible—

The guy had run off, so there was no helping it.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Gengar, brimming with fighting spirit, rallied the entire team.

For food!

The moment the word "food" was mentioned, Johan swore he could see their eyes literally ignite with passion.

Even Marshadow, who rarely made an appearance, stepped forward to hype up the squad.

—

When the contestant list was officially announced—

It immediately caused an uproar.

"What?! Johan, Volkner, Jasmine, Maylene… Is this an all-star lineup?!"

"I never thought I'd see so many elite Trainers at a restaurant tournament!

"Johan defeated Brandon! If he's here, does that mean…"

"Relax! Volkner, Jasmine, and Maylene are all strong too! And besides—Johan is competing alone!"

"Yeah! He might be strong, but can he really beat all of us before his stamina runs out?"

"…"

Standing amid the excited chatter, Flint let out a deep sigh.

Seriously?

Am I really that irrelevant?

I'm supposed to be the future strongest Fire-type Trainer, and I don't even get a mention?

Even though this was a restaurant tournament, the competitors—aside from the Gym Leaders—weren't weak at all.

Seven Stars Restaurant was the most prestigious dining establishment in Sinnoh.

Naturally, only skilled Trainers would even make it past the preliminary requirements to enter the battle-for-food challenge.

Even Jasmine and Maylene had to team up to secure their free meal.

That alone spoke volumes.

Every contestant here had at least competed in one League Conference and achieved respectable results.

Some were seasoned veterans who had competed in multiple tournaments.

Of course—

There were exceptions.

Like—

Thorne and his fiancée, Chloe.

The definition of "bad at battling, but addicted to it."

—

In the end, eight teams and fifteen contestants entered the tournament.

Johan was the only solo participant.

—

Under the Seven Stars Tournament Rules:

Winning a single round would grant a free meal. Higher placements (Champion or runner-ups) would earn special luxury rewards from Thorne and Chloe.

With such a star-studded lineup—

The spectators' enthusiasm was sky-high.

As the crowd roared, the tournament officially began.

—

The first match featured Jasmine and Maylene—aka the Hungry Duo.

Their opponents?

A random mountaineer and an equally random bug catcher.

Though their Pokémon weren't weak—

They were still no match for Jasmine and Maylene.

"Success!"

After winning, Maylene excitedly hugged Jasmine—ecstatic that they had secured their free dinner.

Meanwhile, Jasmine—shy as ever—blushed furiously.

—

Then—

It was Johan's turn.

His opponents?

Thorne and Chloe.

Johan instantly realized—

This tournament was rigged.

But—

It actually worked in his favor.

This wasn't just about the free meal—

It was also a battle for "employee rights"!

—

"Ka-kaah~!"

Gengar cheered from the audience, holding two bouquets of who-knows-where-they-came-from flowers, playing the cheer squad.

Beside Gengar—

Alcremie, happily waving its own set of flowers.

And in Alcremie's shadow, barely visible—

Marshadow.

It sat motionless, eyes locked on the battle—

But if you looked closely, you'd notice—

Its gaze kept wandering toward Alcremie.

After a long internal struggle—

Marshadow finally made a move.

It stealthily extended a hand—

Swiped a tiny bit of Alcremie's cream—

And instantly popped it into its mouth.

"Marsha~! (´▽`ʃ♡ƪ)"

Delicious!

…Wait.

As a proud "Guide of Shadows", could it really leave such a weak Pokémon unprotected?

Marshadow's eyes sharpened.

It turned to Johan with determination.

"Chosen One"—win this battle, or else!

—

On the battlefield—

"Johan, please go easy on us," Thorne and Chloe said in unison, performing a noble bow before releasing their Pokémon.

A Snorlax.

And a Munchlax.

"Ka-Mii~"

"Ka-Bii~"

Seeing the massive, sleepy Snorlax and the smaller, but equally chubby Munchlax—

Johan's expression turned… odd.

Who enters a tag battle with a Snorlax and a Munchlax?

Was this a buffet battle?!

Aside from being adorable, this duo had zero synergy.

But since they were the owners, the audience still cheered enthusiastically.

Johan chuckled.

"Rotom, wanna give this a shot?"

"Me? Loto?"

Hovering beside him, Rotom looked stunned.

Johan smiled.

"Just asking."

"…Can I?" Rotom hesitated.

It never really enjoyed battling—

But after watching so many fights alongside Johan, it had definitely felt the urge to try.

Back in Volkner's Gym, Rotom had already been tempted to join—

It just hadn't realized it at the time.

Johan had noticed.

And that's why he asked.

This was the perfect chance.

"Of course."

Johan patted Rotom and gently encouraged it with his Aura.

"Go for it."

"Lo… Loto!"

Rotom took a deep breath—

And floated onto the battlefield.

Then, Johan sent out his second Pokémon.

"Charcadet, you're up."

"Goka!"

—

"Johan… you only have one body."

"There's no way you can take us all on!"

Johan smirked.

"One body is enough."

"I can take every single one of you."

Flint, Volkner, Jasmine, Maylene—bring it on.

Jasmine & Maylene: ??? We didn't even say anything?!

—

Above Lake Valor, a faint shadow flickered.

A powerful will had been ignited.

# 367: The Art of Playing from Behind (BONUS)

"Johan, please give us your guidance!"

For his second match, Johan faced Jasmine and Maylene, the famous hungry duo.

Since they had already won one battle, they no longer felt pressured about securing their meal.

So when they faced Johan, they were relatively calm and composed.

At the referee's signal, both sent out their Pokémon.

"Go, Steelix!"

"I'm counting on you, Medicham!"

Jasmine's Steelix was her signature Pokémon.

Though she was also known for her Ampharos "Light", it was her Steelix that truly earned her the title of "The Iron-Walled Girl".

Meanwhile, Maylene's Medicham had a distinctly humanoid appearance, resembling a yoga master with its wide, red pants and matching red hair—an iconic Fighting-type Pokémon.

"Medicham, the Meditative Pokémon! A Fighting/Psychic-type! It uses mental power to predict its opponent's movements and is said to be able to meditate for a whole month without eating, Loto!"

Having returned to the Rotom Dex, Rotom resumed its "narrator" role.

Notably, Medicham's ability to go a month without eating was an uncanny match for Maylene's lifestyle.

As the two Pokémon appeared on the battlefield, Johan released his own Poké Balls.

"Draaa!!"

"Ku!!"

With a flash of red light, Johan's Dragonite and Kubfu landed on the battlefield.

Their appearance alone sent a ripple of excitement through the audience.

After all, Dragonite was the pseudo-legendary of Kanto, a Pokémon of great fame.

And Kubfu was even rarer—so much so that most people in the crowd couldn't even name it without someone else explaining.

"Johan is getting serious," Flint muttered to Volkner.

He had witnessed firsthand both Dragonite's overwhelming strength and Kubfu's masterful grasp of Fighting techniques.

Especially—

Dragonite's "Controlled Outrage".

Back when Regigigas was taken down by Tinkaton, it was Dragonite's devastating impact that had set up the final blow.

Even without Flint's comment, Volkner could feel the intensity radiating from Johan's Pokémon.

It wasn't just them—

Jasmine and Maylene felt it too.

But unlike Jasmine—who was more cautious—Maylene was excited.

Finally!

A chance to test herself against "Fighting Master" Johan!

Even if it wasn't a direct physical battle, this was still a duel.

"Johan! We'll give it our all!" Maylene clenched her fists, full of fighting spirit.

Jasmine, though too shy for dramatic pre-battle declarations, nodded firmly.

Their teamwork had been honed over multiple tag battles, their synergy unquestionable.

Once the referee confirmed readiness, the match began.

"Battle, START!"

—

"Steelix, Autotomize!"

Jasmine, despite her timid demeanor, was razor-sharp when it came to battles.

She immediately commanded her Steelix to shed excess metal, reducing its weight and sharply boosting its speed.

"Rooooar—!!"

With a mighty cry, chunks of metallic plating fell from Steelix's body, making it lighter and faster.

"Cha?!"

In the audience, Tinkaton flinched—eyes locked on those falling metal pieces, clenching its fists.

Johan was suddenly relieved that he hadn't sent out Tinkaton for this battle.

Otherwise…

Tinkaton might have "helped" Steelix become even lighter.

—

"Medicham, Reflect!"

Maylene immediately followed up.

Medicham dashed in front of Steelix, sweeping its arms downward, forming a translucent barrier around their side of the battlefield.

One side was buffing its speed.

The other was fortifying defenses.

And notably—

Neither of them needed verbal or visual confirmation.

Their synergy was flawless.

In double battles, a two-person team had an inherent advantage.

They could divide responsibilities—

One Trainer focusing on offense, the other on support—

Making their reaction speed and battle pacing much smoother than a single Trainer controlling both Pokémon alone.

Of course—

That was only true for average Trainers.

For Johan?

Controlling two Pokémon at once was effortless.

"Dragonite, Dragon Dance!"

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite flapped its wings, whipping up powerful gales as its attack and speed steadily increased.

Kubfu, however—

Did not move.

It simply stood in front of Dragonite, remaining motionless.

Not because Johan couldn't command both at once—

But because he chose not to.

Johan was confident in his reaction speed and real-time strategy.

Kubfu's stillness wasn't hesitation.

It was bait.

Flint and Volkner immediately understood.

But most of the audience didn't.

Instead—

They assumed Johan was struggling.

—

"Steelix, Bind!"

Jasmine struck first.

BOOM!

Steelix—despite its gigantic size—moved shockingly fast, coiling its body around Kubfu.

"Dragonite!"

Dragonite flashed white, vanishing just before Steelix's tail could grab it, countering with Extreme Speed.

But—

Jasmine smirked.

Steelix's true target had never been Dragonite.

It had always been Kubfu!

The entire plan was to lure Dragonite into a counterattack, while eliminating the real threat—Kubfu!

Johan's eyes gleamed.

"Move."

Kubfu dodged—

But not fast enough.

Steelix caught it!

Flint's eyes widened.

Even Volkner looked surprised.

Had Johan finally miscalculated?

The audience exploded.

"Johan is struggling!"

"Well, he's fighting two Gym Leaders! Even he has limits!"

"Is Johan finally gonna lose?!"

"I'm kind of excited!"

…These "fans" were way too eager to see Johan take a loss.

—

On the battlefield—

Dragonite's attack was intercepted by Reflect.

Maylene saw her chance.

"Medicham, High Jump Kick!"

Johan smirked.

"Knew it."

"Cha-RAA!!"

Medicham launched itself into the air, bending its knees inward, before springing forward—

A full 130 base power Fighting move, boosted by Pure Power, a one-shot kill against almost anything.

Except—

"Detect."

"Ku~! (－ω－) zzZ"

Kubfu—

Slept.

Briefly.

Then snapped awake, shifting its body just enough to avoid Medicham's attack entirely.

BOOM!!

Medicham slammed into the ground, taking severe recoil damage.

—

The crowd erupted.

"He countered THAT?!"

"That was a setup! There was NO way to escape!"

"Even against two opponents, Johan is still this dominant?!"

Volkner narrowed his eyes.

"He wasn't just reacting—"

"He was waiting for that exact moment."

Flint let out a deep exhale.

"Johan didn't fall into their trap—"

"He set up his own."

—

Johan adjusted his glasses.

"Checkmate."

"Dragonite—Aqua Tail!"

Dragonite spun—

Its water-cloaked tail smashing Steelix aside.

Kubfu broke free and—

Unleashed the final blow.

Steelix was down.

And with Medicham weakened by recoil—

The battle was decided.

Johan had turned the tables.

And he had done it playing from behind.

—

"This…"

"This is the art of counterplay."

# 368: Our Little Ghost Is Also Unstoppably Cute! (BONUS)

"Marsha?"

Hiding in Gengar's shadow, Marshadow suddenly sensed something, turning its head slightly to look to the side.

There, in the dim light, was a shadowy figure—its outline indistinct, almost formless.

Marshadow gave Gengar's cloak a gentle tug.

"Ka-kaah?"

Following Marshadow's gaze, Gengar scratched its head, its round eyes filled with confusion.

This scene… felt strangely familiar.

Could it be…

The "friend" from Lake Acuity had followed them here?

That wouldn't be impossible.

After all, when it came to pursuing someone, who could compete with Marshadow?

It had chased Johan from Kanto to Johto, from Johto to Hoenn, and now had been tailing him throughout Sinnoh.

To be honest, it wasn't just Gengar—most of Johan's Pokémon had gotten used to Marshadow's presence by now.

Even Johan, whenever he prepared meals for his team, would always set aside an extra portion in the shadows.

After all, every time he went to clean up, the entire bowl would be spotless—not a single crumb left behind.

"Ka-kaah~ (o▽`o)"

Gengar waved at the shadowy figure, then gestured invitingly, pulling out a handful of Pokéblocks.

"Ying?"

The shadow tilted its head, its expression puzzled.

Who's this? Do we know each other?

But… the thing it was holding smelled amazing.

Gengar divided the Pokéblocks—some for Marshadow, some for Alcremie, keeping a share for itself, and placing the rest in the empty space beside it.

No need to be shy, we're all friends here.

"Yimai~ o(^▽^)o"

Alcremie, who had already grown close to Gengar, happily grabbed a Pokéblock and started munching.

As it chewed, it hummed a little tune, its tiny feet, hidden beneath its creamy skirt, gently swaying in delight.

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar nudged Alcremie slightly.

Hey… wanna come with us?

"Yimai?"

Go with them?

Gengar had successfully recruited [Alolan Ninetales] the same way before—so it figured it would give it a shot here too.

Meanwhile…

That shadowy figure, after a brief hesitation, finally gave in to the temptation of food and floated forward.

"Marsha~"

A nervous Marshadow awkwardly greeted the newcomer.

It could sense far more than Gengar.

It knew this wasn't Uxie from Lake Acuity—but rather, Azelf, the guardian of Lake Valor.

"Ying~"

Azelf greeted Marshadow in return, but seemed far more relaxed.

Then, without hesitation, it plopped down beside Gengar.

At that moment, the battle between Johan, Jasmine, and Maylene had officially begun.

As Azelf took its first bite of the Pokéblock, its eyes immediately lit up.

"Ying~ (＾＾●)"

Its tail and feet swayed rhythmically as it enjoyed its snack.

"Ka-kaah~"

Don't worry, there's more where that came from.

Today, Gengar was feeling generous—after making sure everyone had their fill, it pulled out another box of Pokéblocks.

Azelf paused, then suddenly asked:

"Ying?"

What's your dream?

Its voice echoed directly into their minds—Gengar, Ninetales, Tinkaton, Charcadet, and Rotom all heard it simultaneously.

"Ka-kaah?"

Dreams?

Gengar hesitated.

It had a lot of dreams…

Like helping Johan become the strongest Trainer, eating every delicious food in the world, or becoming the ultimate "Lord of Fear".

As it was thinking—

Alolan Ninetales suddenly spoke up.

"Oowoo~!"

To stay with Johan forever!

"Ka-kaah!"

"Cha!"

"Goka!"

"Rotom!"

The others froze for a moment—then enthusiastically agreed.

They weren't sure if it technically counted as a dream—but it was something they were deeply committed to.

"Ying?"

Then, something unexpected happened.

Azelf lifted its tiny hand, shielding its eyes—

As if blinded by something incredibly bright.

What did it see?

So dazzling!

From Gengar, Ninetales, Tinkaton, Charcadet, even Rotom—it saw a blazing aura, a manifestation of pure conviction.

Azelf was shocked.

It had never seen such solidified will before.

Even though, from a legendary perspective, these Pokémon's lifespans were limited—

Their resolve was undeniable.

They were willing to dedicate everything to this belief.

For the first time ever—Azelf felt compelled to acknowledge this level of determination.

So—

It left behind a tiny, white tooth where it had been sitting.

Then, without another word—

It vanished.

"Marsha…"

Marshadow blinked.

Wait… you're just gonna leave?

And you took the Pokéblocks with you?!

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar looked down at the tiny tooth, scratching its head.

Huh?

What's this?

This "friend" was so strange.

First, Uxie left behind a tiny claw.

Now, Azelf had left a tiny tooth…

Neat, but…

Couldn't they coat it in some poison first?

Would it kill them to add a bit of venom before gifting these things?

—

Back on the battlefield—

"Johan, we've lost."

With the match over, Jasmine and Maylene accepted their defeat graciously.

Honestly?

They thought their strategy was pretty solid.

Their timing was perfect.

If they had knocked out Kubfu, they might have been able to defeat Dragonite using Steelix and Medicham together.

But…

Their strategy had been read from the start.

And the biggest issue?

Johan had seen through their entire "High Jump Kick Medicham + Pure Power" setup—

Because he had used the exact same strategy before.

With the right combo moves, Medicham could be a devastating sweeper in double battles.

Unfortunately for them…

Johan knew it all too well.

—

"Wow… only a true Fighting Master could read Medicham's abilities this well," Maylene thought.

—

Johan patted Maylene's shoulder, smiling.

"Hey—I'll introduce you to someone in a few days."

A fellow "strong girl".

Maylene and Bea should have a lot in common.

And if he added Korrina from Kalos into the mix—

They could debut as the "Fighting-Type Trio"!

—

With his battle over, Johan returned to the spectator area, sitting beside his Pokémon.

As he glanced at Alcremie, now laughing and playing with his team, he smirked slightly.

He wasn't going to rush things.

After all—

"Bosses choose their employees…but employees also choose their bosses."

If Alcremie preferred to stay at Seven Stars Restaurant, he wouldn't force it.

"Ka-kaah~"

As soon as he sat down, Gengar tugged at his sleeve.

"Hmm?"

He looked down—Gengar opened its palm, revealing a tiny, white tooth.

Johan blinked.

"Wait… did you lose a tooth?"

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar's eyes widened in mock outrage, flashing its perfectly white, intact teeth.

How dare you question my dental health?!

—

Johan rubbed his temple.

Looking at the tooth in his hand…

Then at Charcadet's egg, where the tiny claw from Uxie was still attached.

Uxie's Claw.

Azelf's Tooth.

…And if they added Mesprit's Feather—

They'd have everything needed to craft the "Red Chain".

Oh no.

Was he actually about to get dragged into this mess?!

There was no way he was setting foot in Lake Verity now!

No way.

—

Still…

Johan glanced at Gengar, who grinned back at him.

...Oh well.

Our little ghost is still ridiculously cute.

# 369: Flint, Shaken by the Winds of Defeat (BONUS)

Flint and Volkner's opponents were quite strong—both had previously competed in the Pokémon League and achieved solid results.

However, against the "Fire & Thunder Duo," they were simply outmatched.

In the end, Flint and Volkner secured an overwhelming victory.

And now, the final match was set:

Johan vs. Flint & Volkner in a Doubles Battle.

Despite their time apart—Flint having traveled, while Volkner languished in boredom—the two had once been childhood training partners.

After their first two rounds, their long-lost battle synergy had fully returned.

Facing their combined strength, Johan felt a significant amount of pressure.

Of course, the audience's hype had reached its peak.

"I don't know who that wild-haired guy is, but I have to admit—he's actually pretty strong. He's not dragging Volkner down."

"Their coordination is on point. This won't be easy for Johan."

"One is calm and analytical, the other is hot-blooded and aggressive—it's actually a really solid combo."

"...Yeah."

Hearing the crowd's comments, Flint squatted down in a corner and started drawing circles on the ground.

He had finally been acknowledged.

But only as "that wild-haired guy."

—

With the tournament nearing its climax, the restaurant owners, Chiqiu and Chika, finally revealed the championship grand prize!

"As someone with some influence in the culinary world, I've arranged for a special reward for the champions…"

The winners would receive ten "Gourmet Passes", granting them free meals at any five-star restaurant affiliated with Seven Stars Restaurant worldwide!

In the Pokémon world, dining at high-end restaurants was extremely expensive—even a meal for two normal people could cost thousands of Pokédollars.

If you added Pokémon into the mix, tens of thousands—even hundreds of thousands—of Pokédollars per meal was normal.

And if you had Pokémon like Gengar or Dragonite at the table…

Johan didn't even want to think about the bill.

But with ten passes, they could eat for free—not just at Seven Stars, but at elite restaurants across multiple regions.

The moment they heard the prize—

Gengar and the others went wide-eyed.

Gengar immediately applied to enter the battle!

And not just Gengar—

All of Johan's Pokémon volunteered for battle.

Every single one of them swore to fight with everything they had.

Even Charcadet wanted in.

Normally, Charcadet had the smallest appetite out of Johan's entire team.

(That is, if you ignored its constant need to consume charcoal.)

But Charcadet wasn't fighting for itself—

It was fighting for the unborn Pokémon inside the egg.

It had a feeling—

That little one was going to be a big eater.

This was its veteran's instinct.

Previously, the free dinner had already ignited their fighting spirit.

Now, with ten gourmet feasts on the line…

They were ready to risk it all.

Even—

"Marsha?"

Marshadow stepped out of the shadows, holding a tiny wooden stick.

"Need my help?"

Marshadow?

Johan raised an eyebrow.

Wouldn't that be too unfair?

"Marsha! |*▼皿▼)"

Marshadow subtly tilted its head.

This isn't about the prize!

I just… haven't stretched in a while.

—

"We won't lose!"

Flint remained as confident as ever.

Even though he'd lost to Cynthia over 100 times, he never stopped believing he could win.

As the battle began—

Their Pokémon choices surprised Johan.

Volkner sent out Electivire.

Flint sent out…

"The One and Only King!"

Yes—Flareon.

Johan ultimately decided not to send out Marshadow.

After all—

Very few people even knew Marshadow existed.

And the little one wasn't fond of being in the spotlight—it was naturally shy.

For a mere ten gourmet meals…

It really wasn't worth forcing it to fight.

So instead—

Johan chose…

"Ka-kaah!"

"Oowoo!"

Gengar & Ninetales!

The first two Pokémon Johan ever caught—

And still, the two with the strongest bond.

The moment they appeared—

Flint and Volkner felt a wave of pressure crash down on them.

They knew exactly how powerful these two were.

Gengar had absolutely demolished Volkner's Luxray.

Ninetales had completely frozen Flint's Infernape.

"Johan is really going all out for these free meals…" Flint muttered.

But doubles battles were different from singles—and with their synergy, they still had a chance.

—

"Battle… start!"

—

Volkner immediately took the first move.

"Electivire—Discharge!"

BZ-ZZT—!

As snowflakes drifted down, Electivire's engines roared to life.

A golden surge of electricity exploded outward, spreading in all directions.

Normally, Discharge was a risky move—

It hit everyone on the field, including allies.

But that was exactly their plan.

Both Pokémon absorbed the attack.

Electivire's Speed skyrocketed thanks to its ability, Motor Drive.

And Flareon…

Became paralyzed on purpose.

"Guts-ability Flareon?"

[Guts: When affected by a status condition, Attack is boosted by 50%.]

As if to confirm Johan's thoughts—

Flareon threw back its head and howled.

Its fighting spirit surged.

"Brreeeaaaah!"

"Flint—now!"

"Flareon, Curse!"

For non-Ghost-type Pokémon, Curse lowered Speed but sharpened Attack and Defense.

But wait…

Flareon was paralyzed.

How was it still moving so smoothly?

Was this friendship hax?!

Or were they just cheating?!

Looking closely—

Flareon's entire body was cloaked in a faint layer of flames, flickering like a second coat of fur.

Just as Flint had said—

No one understood Fire-types better than Volkner.

And vice versa.

No one understood Electric-types better than Flint.

After countless battles and teamwork trials, they had developed a seamless battle style.

—

"Hax. They're both hacking."

Johan thought, rolling his eyes.

Still—

He didn't hesitate.

"Aurora Veil."

"Nasty Plot."

In the whirling snow, Alolan Ninetales cast a shimmering barrier—

Dampening all incoming attacks.

Meanwhile, Gengar smirked as it boosted its Special Attack.

Volkner and Flint were completely unfazed.

They had already expected this.

"Now!"

"Flareon—Flare Blitz!"

BOOM!

A pillar of flames erupted from Flareon's body—

As it charged straight for Alolan Ninetales!

At the same time—

"Electivire—Taunt!"

Electivire drew the enemy's attention, preventing them from using support moves.

Flint clenched his fist.

"We got them!"

Flareon's boosted Flare Blitz—

With Guts + Curse + type advantage—

Even if it didn't one-shot Alolan Ninetales, it would definitely cripple it!

But—

Johan simply smiled.

"Ninetales—Dig."

"Oowoo~!"

And in an instant—

Alolan Ninetales disappeared.

Flint & Flareon: (⊙_⊙) ???

Flint:

"...Wait. What do we do now?"

# 370: Gengar: Shadow Sneak, I Want to Learn! (BONUS)

Boom! Boom! Boom—

Under the night sky, dazzling fireworks cast a colorful veil over Lake Valor and the Seven Stars Restaurant.

The doubles battle tournament at Seven Stars Restaurant had concluded.

Johan successfully secured ten "Unlimited Dining Passes," handing one each to Jasmine and Maylene, leaving the two girls deeply grateful.

As for Volkner—

As the Sunyshore Gym Leader, known as the "Thunder of Sunyshore," he didn't need one.

And Flint? Well, he had Volkner.

Then came the moment everyone was looking forward to—the free dinner.

There was no denying that Seven Stars Restaurant was the most famous eatery in the Sinnoh region for a reason.

They practically swept through the entire restaurant like a storm.

Even Thorne and Aime, the restaurant owners, hadn't expected the meal to end because the food ran out!

They always stocked up well when hosting doubles tournaments, but this was the first time they'd completely run out.

The reason was simple—Johan's Pokémon had eaten to their heart's content.

After the feast—

Johan took his little ones on a walk around Lake Valor.

Earlier, he had been concerned about encountering Azelf, but since he had already met it—and even received the Fang of Azelf—there was nothing to worry about anymore.

Gentle ripples shimmered on the lake's surface, occasionally revealing wild Water-type Pokémon poking their heads out.

Had he not been so full, Gengar might have already put on swimming goggles and dived right in.

At that moment, Johan was on the phone.

"...The report has been submitted. The follow-up research funding should be approved soon."

Cynthia's voice came through the phone.

She had been staying in Celestic Town with Professor Carolina, completing their research report on the ancient giants.

Since Regigigas was officially registered by the Sinnoh League as a Pokémon requiring special monitoring, this report was of utmost importance.

So, they had rushed to complete it.

"Good work," Johan said.

After all, a portion of that funding would be his reward.

"Mm."

Cynthia's voice carried a hint of exhaustion.

"How's Sunyshore? Everything going well?"

"Not bad."

Johan briefly summarized their experiences over the past few days.

Overall, Volkner was gradually recovering his form. Johan's main goal for coming to Sunyshore was more than halfway complete.

"You guys joined the Seven Stars Restaurant doubles tournament?" Cynthia sounded surprised.

She had heard of it before.

Johan scoffed. "They called it doubles; I fought alone."

"Hmm?"

"Flint ditched me."

At this, Johan began to grumble.

Originally, he had planned to team up with Flint. Not only would the battles be easier, but with their tactical skills, they would've completely crushed that "clown" Flint.

But Flint bailed early, leaving Johan to fight alone.

"Then Flint and Volkner deliberately targeted me, using ridiculously tricky strategies. I almost lost!"

After a moment of silence, Cynthia asked, confirming—

"Flint?"

"Yep!"

At that moment, Johan was the picture of someone tattling.

Meanwhile—

Having finished dinner, Flint was lounging on the sofa with Volkner, reminiscing about their past visits to the restaurant.

Suddenly, he shivered violently and sprang up from the couch.

Volkner gave him a puzzled look as Flint rubbed his goosebump-covered arms.

"For some reason… I feel like my life is in danger."

The next morning, cloudy skies.

On the third floor of Seven Stars Restaurant, in the VIP suite—

Johan lazily woke up in bed.

Last night, he had a dream.

In it, there was a massive cake, which upon closer inspection, turned out to be a Gigantamax Alcremie.

Without hesitation, he had grabbed a bucket of sugar and charged at it. Fresh cream with sugar? Perfection.

Only after waking up did he realize—

That shouldn't have been his dream. It should've been Gengar's.

Glancing at the ceiling, he saw Gengar sprawled out, drooling, wrapped in a giant Spirit Cloth like a blanket.

Johan narrowed his eyes.

Could Gengar have influenced his dream?

"Looks like they really do love Alcremie."

After last night's battle, Alcremie had been busy in the kitchen, so Johan hadn't had a chance to talk to it.

But he could tell—Alcremie was interested.

Gengar and the others must've been working on "persuading" it.

A little more effort today, and he'd probably be able to recruit it.

After waking up, washing up, and having breakfast—

Johan took his Pokémon to the lakeside for their usual warm-up and training.

"Morning, Mr. Johan!"

He happened to run into Maylene, already drenched in sweat from practicing her martial arts.

Judging by her state, she had been training for quite a while.

"Morning, Maylene."

"Mr. Johan, can you teach me some fighting techniques?" Maylene asked earnestly.

To demonstrate her strength, she ran barefoot to a massive boulder—about two people tall and requiring three or four people to encircle it.

Then—

With a light shout, she pushed it, leaving a deep groove in the riverbank.

Johan: "..."

Teach?

Teach how to get beaten up?

"That's enough, Maylene," Johan quickly said.

"Oh." Maylene obediently stepped back.

"Ahem..."

Johan awkwardly adjusted his glasses and said with a serious face:

"Next time, for sure!"

Maylene, unfazed and completely missing the hidden meaning, clenched her fists and nodded.

"Alright!"

Then she ran off, full of energy, continuing her training.

Johan wiped his forehead.

Even though he had awakened his Aura powers, Pokémon World's "superhuman strength" still left him feeling outmatched.

Shaking off the thought, he clapped his hands, signaling the start of training.

Compared to move training, basic training was much quieter and could be done anywhere.

"Goka!"

Charcadet solemnly handed its Pokémon Egg to Johan before diving into training.

Johan could feel the warmth radiating from the egg.

His Aura told him that the little one inside was incredibly lively and full of energy.

Charcadet's "Flame Body" would not only speed up the egg's incubation but also nurture the Pokémon inside.

So Johan had temporarily entrusted the "Rainbow Feather" to Charcadet.

With "Flame Body," "Rainbow Feather," and the "Life Fire," plus the blessings of "Uxie's Claw" and "Mesprit's Fang"—

Johan had a feeling this little one would be something extraordinary upon hatching.

—

Elsewhere—

"Ka-kaah~╭(●｀●)╯"

Gengar approached Marshadow.

It wanted to see if it could learn a move from Marshadow.

Back when they fought Regigigas together, Gengar had been very impressed by the way Marshadow pounded on its head.

"Marsha?"

Marshadow shook its head.

It wasn't unwilling to teach—

But "Close Combat" just wasn't a move Gengar could learn.

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar scratched its head.

Wasn't there another powerful move?

Marshadow rubbed its chin thoughtfully.

Gengar stared, blinking.

Why was Marshadow starting to act like Johan?

"Marsha!"

A strong move? This one!

Marshadow clenched its fists and struck its palm.

Then, under Gengar's expectant gaze, it extended its hands toward a distant rock.

A shadow shot out from beneath Marshadow—

Boom!!

The rock shattered, and wisps of dark energy, like bats, returned to Marshadow's hands.

Gengar's eyes sparkled.

A ranged punch? With bat effects?

Marshadow shook its head again.

"Marsha?"

That move was even harder to learn.

Because it was its signature move—"Spectral Thief"!

But Gengar loved it.

"Ka-kaah!"

It had to learn it.

And it would.

Marshadow hesitated.

"Marsha..."

It wasn't that it didn't want to teach Gengar—

But whether Gengar could actually learn it depended on its own talent.

Signature moves weren't necessarily impossible for other Pokémon to learn, but it was incredibly rare.

For example, Smeargle could copy practically anything with Sketch, but that was a special case.

"Ka-kaah~(▼⊿▼)"

Gengar confidently puffed up its chest. It had excellent learning abilities!

To prove it, it spread its hands wide.

Dark energy emerged from its body, swirling together into two small bats, looking just like the ones Marshadow had recalled earlier.

How hard could it really be?

Shadow? It already mastered that.

Stealing? It could "borrow" Tinkaton's Pickpocket ability.

Attacking an opponent's physical strength? It knew Lick.

Negating stat boosts? It had Clear Smog as a reference.

If it combined all these skills, there was no way it couldn't pull it off!

Not to mention—

It had Johan, the strongest Trainer, guiding it.

This was totally doable!

"Marsha."

Marshadow gave a small nod, subtly tucking the three boxes of Pokéblocks into the shadows before beginning its explanation.

—

After finishing basic training—

Johan returned to the restaurant with his Pokémon, estimating that Alcremie's morning shift was nearly over.

So they waited for it inside.

"Gengar?"

Johan, while brushing Ninetales' fur, noticed how unusually quiet Gengar was today.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar scrunched up its round face.

It had to admit—Spectral Thief was the hardest move it had ever tried to learn.

At that moment—

"Yimay?"

Walking out of the kitchen with tiny steps, Alcremie immediately spotted Johan and his team.

Johan pulled out his ace.

He played a video on his phone—showing the Alcremie's potential future teammates, the Indeedee siblings, along with the pastries and cakes they had made.

"We have the world's greatest pâtissiers," Johan said. "All that's missing is you. Want to work together to create the most delicious cake in the world?"

Pastry chefs admired Alcremie, but Alcremie also sought the right pastry chefs.

"Ai~yimay~~"

On the other end of the video call, the Indeedee sister warmly extended an invitation to Alcremie.

A partnership built on mutual passion.

"Yimay... Yimay!"

Alcremie's eyes shone.

Johan knew—

It was on the verge of saying yes.
