# 379: Nothing Beats a Winter Hot Spring

January 8th, early morning, cloudy weather.

"Mmm…"

After spending the night at Professor Rowan's research lab, Johan rubbed his temples and sat up in bed.

Instinctively, he glanced at the Pokémon egg beside Charcadet and let out a small sigh of relief.

Last night, he had a strange dream where he caught a Larvitar, a [Little Snorlax], and a Gible.

Led by Gengar, all three had their mouths wide open, calling out to him: "Hungry! Hungry!"

That scene…

It had terrified him into a cold sweat.

Fortunately, waking up proved it was just a dream.

"Oowoo~~"

Still half-asleep, Ice Ninetales pawed at the air in dissatisfaction. Johan quickly offered his arm.

Ice Ninetales clung to it, nuzzled it, took a few sniffs, and, satisfied, drifted back into sleep.

Despite being an Ice-type Pokémon, it still insisted on lazing in bed during winter…

Johan lay back down, staring at the ceiling, where Gengar was also staring back at him.

Human and Pokémon locked eyes for a few seconds.

"Ka-kaah~(ˊ〇ˋ*)"

Gengar stretched out with a big yawn.

There was an urban legend…

Gengar's Pokédex weight was 40.5 kg, while Clefable's was 40 kg. Meanwhile, Haunter weighed only 0.1 kg. And if you added in a meal or two…

Johan shook his head.

"Gengar, take Dragonite and the others out for some air."

Come to think of it, Dragonite had been unusually energetic ever since evolving.

More precisely, after obtaining the fragment of the Verdant Orb, its energy had been through the roof—more than even Charcadet, who had Entei's blessing, or Kubfu, who trained even in its sleep.

If it didn't fly dozens of laps a day, it couldn't sit still.

Sure enough, the moment Johan woke up, Dragonite was already getting restless.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar gave him an "OK" gesture, and the chubby one led the bigger chubby one outside.

Noticing this, Tinkaton suddenly jumped out of bed, grabbed its hammer, and charged out with a battle cry.

"Cha! Cha~!"

A dragon knight couldn't miss a flight session!

The thing was… when Dragonite flew alone, it was fine, but when carrying a passenger—whether a human or Pokémon—it usually didn't end well.

"Wait a second… I feel like I forgot something?"

Having spent the night who-knows-where, Flint dusted off the grass from his clothes and greeted Johan, who was making breakfast in the courtyard.

"Morning, Johan-sensei!"

As he approached—

"Thanks again for everything yesterday, you and Cynthia both."

Johan finally remembered what he had forgotten.

After receiving Professor Rowan's bad news yesterday, he had completely forgotten about Flint.

"Want some?" he asked generously.

"Of course!" Flint nodded eagerly. Then he asked, "What is it?"

"A mixed-grain pancake."

Johan handed him a paper bag. "I made it using Charcadet's flames."

"Huh?"

Flint blinked.

Why did Johan-sensei always come up with these weird foods?

And more importantly… they were always delicious.

Whoever marries Johan-sensei will definitely live a happy life.

Taking a big bite, the crisp texture surprised him. In just a few bites, the pancake was gone.

Then…

He lined up with Gengar, Dragonite, Kubfu, and Garchomp, only to be met with their hostile stares.

"Ahem, Johan-sensei, what brought you to Professor Rowan's lab? Registering for the Lily of the Valley Conference?" Flint quickly changed the subject.

Lily of the Valley Conference…

Johan paused mid-action.

Oh right, he could register for the tournament here.

Originally, Johan had little interest in participating. But after learning that the egg was likely a Larvitar or Gible, he reconsidered.

Maybe it was time to… stir up some trouble—no, gain some high-level battle experience?

"All done."

Cynthia, sitting nearby, daintily licked her fingertips and looked at Johan expectantly.

"At your service!"

To Johan, Cynthia was no longer just a powerful Trainer or an exceptionally beautiful girl. She was also a walking wallet, someone he couldn't afford to offend.

"When are you heading back to Hoenn?" Cynthia asked as she waited.

"What's up?"

Instead of answering, Johan asked in return.

"There's a nice hot spring inn in Sandgem Town," Cynthia replied.

In winter, nothing was more relaxing than a hot spring soak.

She hadn't had a proper break in ages—always busy researching history, writing papers, or studying ancient ruins.

A hot spring?

At this, Gengar immediately raised both hands.

"Ka-kaah!"

Go, go, go! It had been too long since their last hot spring visit!

Tinkaton and Dragonite also voiced their agreement.

Even Ice Ninetales, though not overly fond of hot springs, was willing to tolerate it—so long as Johan was there.

As for Kubfu and Charcadet, they had never been to a hot spring with Johan before, so their eyes gleamed with curiosity.

A hot spring, huh…?

Johan's eyes lit up slightly. He hesitated before asking:

"Is it mixed-gender?"

STARE!

Garchomp's gaze, filled with murderous intent, instantly locked onto him.

Johan's mouth twitched, and he let out an awkward chuckle.

"Uh, just kidding! Don't take it too seriously."

Cynthia simply smiled and said nothing.

Meanwhile, Flint, slow on the uptake, suddenly realized something and widened his eyes in disbelief.

"You two are bathing together?!"

Suddenly—

He felt a tap on his back.

Turning around, he saw Garchomp's friendly smile.

It waved him over.

"Garrr—"

Come on, let's spar.

Gulp.

Flint swallowed hard.

They borrowed a jeep from Professor Rowan and drove toward the hot spring inn Cynthia had mentioned.

According to her, the area was once an active volcano, but after it went dormant, natural geothermal springs remained.

Back when Cynthia was an assistant at Professor Rowan's lab, she frequently visited this inn for relaxation.

For safety reasons, Johan insisted on driving himself.

The key word: safety.

"We should install a Rotom engine in the car. Then I wouldn't even have to drive," he mused.

January 8th, afternoon, light snow.

Snowflakes drifted gently from the sky as they arrived at the hot spring inn, nestled in the mountains about 30 kilometers outside Sandgem Town.

As they entered the wooded area, the temperature dropped noticeably, and the trees were dusted with a thin layer of snow.

But in such an environment, the thought of a hot spring soak became all the more enticing.

The wooden buildings and the cozy atmosphere welcomed them with rising plumes of warm mist.

A hotel attendant immediately recognized Cynthia and led them toward the baths.

"Our inn features natural hot springs rich in minerals. A meal will also be served later, prepared by our in-house chef…"

As the attendant explained, they handed out towels and bathrobes.

Cynthia, smiling, walked toward another hallway.

Clearly, mixed bathing was not a thing here either.

"Let's go, hot spring time!"

"Ka-kaah~!"

From the shadows, Gengar was beyond ready.

Inside the changing room.

Two grown men had no reason to be shy. Johan glanced at his reflection—well-defined chest, clear muscle tone, abs—before clicking his tongue.

"I barely work out, but my belly fat disappeared. Could it be… Aura has a fitness effect?"

He subconsciously glanced at Flint and smirked.

"Skinny dog."

Flint: "???"

The hot spring's bamboo pipes dripped with a rhythmic plop plop, while warm water gently cascaded into the pool.

The air carried a faint scent of sulfur, mixed with the freshness of bamboo and wood.

Since they had reserved a private bath, it was just Johan and Flint.

Naturally, they immediately released their Pokémon.

Johan leaned back against the smooth river stones, watching the scene unfold.

Nothing beat a winter hot spring.

He raised a brow and asked, "Anyone want macarons?"

STARE!

Instantly, eight pairs of eyes locked onto him.

Including one in the corner—Marshadow, clutching a towel in one hand and the railing in the other, hesitantly poking at the water with its foot.

# 380: Tonight’s Moon Is So Round

BTW, I'm on a break so my freetime has increased. Can you guys let me know which chapters feature, name mistranslations and less content. I'll start fixing them.

---------

"Oowoo~(≧▽≦)"

Ninetales swayed its body gently as the warm air from the hairdryer gradually dried its fur.

With its abilities, it could easily freeze the moisture on its body and shake it off, but it still enjoyed the feeling of Johan carefully drying it with the hairdryer.

On the other side, Charcadet was gently wiping the Pokémon egg with a towel, its body returning to its normal state.

Meanwhile, Kubfu and Gengar sat beside it with their hands stretched out… warming themselves by the fire.

Kubfu's fur gradually dried as well.

"Ku~ Ku~"

"Cha~"

Tinkaton exhaled lightly onto its hammer before wiping it down with a towel, producing a crisp "squeaky" sound.

Johan glanced at Flint and his Pokémon huddled in the corner and called out:

"Rotom."

"Here, Roto!"

"Fan mode."

"Understood, Roto!"

Gengar spit out a fan, and Rotom's body sparked with electricity as it dived into the fan, instantly transforming into Fan Rotom.

A gust of wind blew toward Flint and his Pokémon.

Whooosh—

"Ku?"

Feeling the wind's intensity, Flint's Infernape squinted its eyes, tears forming at the corners.

Why didn't they have all this fancy equipment?

Flint opened his mouth, and a gust of air rushed in. He quickly shut it again.

He exchanged a glance with Infernape—Actually, this isn't so bad.

Once everything was sorted out, Johan adjusted his posture and leaned comfortably against Dragonite's smooth, soft belly.

With the room warmed by Charcadet and Flint's Pokémon and the lingering warmth from the hot spring…

Lying against Dragonite's cool, squishy belly—nothing could be more comfortable.

Perfection!

Squeak—

The door slid open.

Cynthia, accompanied by her Pokémon, walked in.

She wore a loose-fitting purple bathrobe, wooden sandals clacking softly with each step. Wrapped up tightly, she still carried a lingering warmth from the hot spring.

Her waterfall-like golden hair cascaded down to her waist, and her fair cheeks were tinted with a faint pink hue from the bath. She smiled as her gaze swept over the scene before her.

Johan was sprawled out, completely at ease. Flint…

"Flint, did you change your hairstyle?"

Flint: (﹏)

Three people, a long, quiet, and comfortable night. Johan felt a thought stir in his heart.

"Let's play cards!"

His gambler's instincts reawakened.

Given the current setting, nothing could be more fitting than an intense game of Pokémon World Landlord!

"I'm in," Cynthia said, eyes curving into a smile.

"Let's do it!"

Flint rolled up his sleeves, suddenly energized.

As expected, no Trainer could resist the temptation of a card game.

As for the little ones, Johan had already prepared Pokéblocks and gourmet treats from the inn for them.

Some time later…

"This is unfair! Why isn't the Joker a Gengar?!"

—

Midnight.

The snow had stopped. A bright, full moon hung high in the night sky.

Johan and Cynthia sat at the edge of the open-air balcony, their feet soaking in the steaming hot spring.

Cynthia propped her chin up with both hands, watching as her pale feet swayed in the water like two tiny fish, mischief flashing in her eyes.

She gently kicked the water, sending droplets splashing onto Johan's face.

Her delicate feet, glistening in the moonlight, were as flawless as porcelain.

Johan: "…"

The golden-haired beauty chuckled like a playful child.

Noticing Garchomp's gaze from a distance, Johan pressed his lips together and temporarily suppressed his urge to retaliate.

Leaning back, he rested against Dragonite's belly.

Steady, even breathing came from beside him.

Cynthia, mimicking him, leaned against Dragonite's belly as well.

She had to admit—there were benefits to having a large, soft belly to rest on.

Charcadet sat cross-legged nearby, already fast asleep, yet it instinctively controlled its body temperature to keep the cold at bay for them.

Ninetales curled into Johan's lap, letting out a long yawn, its eyelids growing heavy.

"Oowoo…"

Johan gently stroked its head, smoothing down its fur as he whispered,

"Sleep."

"Oowoo~"

Ninetales nuzzled into his chest, found a comfortable position, and quietly drifted into slumber.

On the other side, Ice Eevee did the same, burrowing into Cynthia's arms.

Johan and Cynthia exchanged glances but didn't speak, mindful of the sleeping Pokémon.

Their eyes alone were enough to convey their understanding.

A gentle smile passed between them.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar and Rotom tiptoed sneakily from outside, hands clasped behind their backs, looking suspicious as ever.

Earlier, they had gone to gather materials, and their return was earlier than Johan had expected.

Crossing over Tinkaton—who was sprawled out in the shape of a star—and Kubfu, who was doing push-ups with a snot bubble hanging from its nose, they stopped before Johan and Cynthia.

"Ta-da!"

The little purple rascal proudly pulled out a small bouquet of flowers and presented it to Cynthia.

Cynthia's eyes sparkled like the stars in the sky. In a soft voice, she said:

"Thank you~"

Finding fresh flowers in winter was no easy task.

"Ka-kaah~()"

Gengar grinned widely.

Then it turned to Johan and smugly raised an eyebrow.

Not long after, it let out a yawn, kicked its feet, and floated into the air, falling asleep midair.

Johan shook his head, pried open its mouth, and pulled out its precious "Spirit Cloth" from within, gently draping it over Gengar.

Gengar smacked its lips, clutched the cloth in one hand, and rolled over in satisfaction.

"You and your Pokémon really share a deep bond," Cynthia murmured.

"Do we?"

Johan chuckled.

"These little guys keep me on my toes."

"That's exactly what makes your bond so strong…"

Truthfully, though the little ones were sometimes mischievous, when it truly mattered, they would always stand by Johan's side—or even in front of him.

Mischief was just their way of interacting with him.

And for Pokémon like Gengar, being playful was simply their nature.

Most Trainers tried to suppress that nature, making them more obedient. But Johan was different—he embraced their personalities and transformed their quirks into strengths.

Honestly, spending time with them was a joy.

"Oowoo~"

Ninetales, lying on Johan's lap, rolled over, revealing its soft belly.

He couldn't resist reaching out to rub it.

"Oowoo…"

The little one murmured sleepily.

Just then—

A sudden, ethereal sound rang out.

A hazy figure appeared in their line of sight.

"…Is that…"

Both of them froze.

Johan reacted almost instantly, asking,

"This hot spring inn… it's not near Lake Verity, is it?"

Cynthia blinked.

"Probably… not far."

Lake Verity was once a volcano. Over time, the crater had transformed into a massive natural lake.

And this hot spring inn was built atop the geothermal remnants of that volcano.

Well then.

Johan sighed, accepting fate.

What's meant to come will come. What's not meant to come… won't appear even if you search for it.

Cynthia seemed to catch on and hesitantly asked,

"…Is that Mesprit?"

Mesprit, the legendary Emotion Pokémon!

Johan nodded.

"Ying~~"

The phantom form of Mesprit drifted through the air, its twin tails swaying, scattering shimmering stardust in its wake.

The sheer emotion radiating from this scene was impossible for the Emotion Pokémon to ignore.

To Mesprit, Johan and his Pokémon, Cynthia and hers—even Flint, who was snoring inside, hugging Infernape—were like beacons of light.

And among them, the bond between Johan and his Pokémon shone the brightest.

"Ying~"

With a soft chime, a tiny, crystalline feather drifted down from the sky toward Johan.

Ding-ling~~

Like the chime of a bell, Mesprit vanished.

Johan stared blankly at the small feather in his hand—far smaller than the Rainbow Wing.

No surprises. No excitement.

The Wing of Mesprit!

With this, the set was complete.

Now, with the Claw of Uxie, the Tooth of Azelf, and the Wing of Mesprit, the binding tools were all gathered.

"Finally, it's complete," Johan murmured, gazing at the delicate feather in his hand.

"What will you do with it?" Cynthia asked softly, her golden hair cascading over her shoulders.

"I'm not entirely sure yet," Johan replied, a hint of uncertainty in his voice. "But having these might be crucial for future challenges."

Cynthia nodded, understanding the weight of his words. The legends surrounding these items were vast and varied, and their true purpose remained a mystery.

As the night deepened, the group settled into a comfortable silence, each lost in their thoughts. The gentle hum of the wind outside and the soft breathing of their Pokémon companions created a serene atmosphere.

Johan leaned back, feeling the steady rise and fall of Dragonite's belly beneath him. The warmth from Charcadet and the soothing presence of his team made him feel at peace.

"Tonight's moon is exceptionally round," Johan mused aloud, his eyes reflecting the luminous orb in the sky.

"It is," Cynthia agreed, her voice barely above a whisper. "A perfect night."

As sleep began to claim them, the last thing Johan saw was the gentle glow of the Wing of Mesprit, a reminder of the adventures that awaited them.

# 381: Wing Festival Tournament

January 9th, early morning, bright and sunny.

At the outdoor dining table of the hot spring inn, Johan took a satisfying sip of his savory soy milk before pulling out a fried dough stick and stuffing it into his mouth.

He had to admit—ever since Rotom's capabilities had been gradually "unlocked," his methods and variety of cooking had completely expanded.

Flint casually walked out of his room and sat at the table, pouring himself a bowl of milky white soy milk. After taking a sip, he scooped in two heaping spoonfuls of sugar, making Johan's eye twitch.

"You heretic!"

"Huh?" Flint looked confused.

"I feel like Dragonite would be a great contender in a racing tournament," Cynthia commented, watching Dragonite joyfully soaring through the sky.

"A racing tournament?"

Johan squinted up at the sky, where Dragonite was gleefully zooming back and forth, mouth wide open.

Why was he starting to get the image of an oversized, winged husky pretending to be a dragon?

Shaking his head, he quickly pushed the thought away.

If he kept thinking like that, every time he looked at Dragonite, he'd associate it with that image.

Still, he had to admit, despite its chubby build, Dragonite was incredibly fast.

The racing tournaments Cynthia mentioned were actually one of many Pokémon competitions in the Pokémon world.

At the very least, they were more popular than ice sculpting contests.

And they had a huge following.

These races were divided into land-based and aerial categories.

For land races, Pokémon like Rapidash and Rhyhorn were commonly seen.

For example, in Pokémon XY, Serena was a highly skilled Rhyhorn racer.

Aerial races, on the other hand, were split into individual sprints, relay races, and more.

With a mouthful of steamed bun, Flint muttered, "Isn't Fortree City hosting the Wing Festival Tournament soon?"

"Hm?"

Johan paused.

Fortree City was the northernmost city in Hoenn, and its Gym Leader, Winona, specialized in Flying-type Pokémon. She was known as the "Elegant Sky Dancer."

Due to the geographical locations of Kanto, Johto, Hoenn, and Sinnoh, Fortree City wasn't far from the southwestern part of Sinnoh, where they were now.

The Wing Festival Tournament was an annual event in Fortree City, celebrating the skies and featuring aerial Pokémon races.

However…

"How do you know so much about this?" Johan asked.

Flint didn't even own a Flying-type Pokémon.

Flint scratched his head sheepishly.

"Well… after last time… I, uh, thought about catching a Flying-type, so I started paying attention to the news."

"I'll introduce you to someone who can help."

Johan pulled out his phone and scrolled through his contacts.

Flint leaned in to look.

Even Cynthia glanced over curiously.

"L-Lance?!"

Flint froze when he saw the name.

He had once participated in an exhibition match in Sootopolis City alongside Cynthia, Lance, and Steven, so he was familiar with them.

Cynthia also paused for a moment when she saw Lance's name before smiling knowingly.

"Isn't Lance the 'Dragon Master'?" Flint asked.

Johan waved dismissively. "Doesn't matter. Trust me—if you need Flying-type Pokémon advice, he's your guy."

Flint imagined the scenario in his head:

He'd go to Lance for advice about Flying-type Pokémon.

Lance would glare at him and say, "Hmph! You're not even worth talking to!"

Flint shuddered.

Too risky.

But…

Now that he thought about it, most of Lance's Pokémon were Flying-types…

"I'll ask for you."

Johan, suddenly interested, immediately dialed Lance's number.

By now, Team Rocket should still be on their holiday break, right?

The call connected quickly.

"Lance, it's me. I have a friend, Flint, who wants to ask—"

Before he could finish, Lance's surprised voice came through.

"Johan? How did you know I was in Hoenn?"

"Huh?"

Johan blinked.

What was Lance doing in Hoenn?

"I was going to tell you once I arrived, but you called first. Is this what they call a bond between friends?"

Who wants a 'bond' with you, man?

Through the phone, Johan could also hear strong winds in the background.

"…Where are you?"

Before Lance could answer, another voice interrupted:

"Where do you think you're putting your hands?!"

The voice was slightly irritated but also had a hint of shyness.

That voice…

Clair?!

"Ah, sorry! I wasn't paying attention—" Lance hurriedly explained.

A mental image formed in Johan's mind:

Lance and Clair riding Dragonite, with Lance in front and Clair behind him…

And Lance's hand accidentally—

"Hmph!"

Clair's annoyed huff pulled him back to reality.

Johan shook his head.

If it were anyone else, he might have suspected it was intentional.

But Lance… this certified steel-hearted guy… probably did it by accident.

"Well, Johan, we're almost in Fortree City. You coming? The Wing Festival Tournament's happening," Lance said cheerfully.

Since it was a rare vacation, and the Investigation Bureau had nothing urgent, he had taken the chance to travel with Clair.

Hearing about the Wing Festival Tournament and the chance to meet old friends, they decided to go.

Johan glanced at Cynthia, ignoring Flint, who was frantically shaking his head.

Cynthia mouthed, We're still on vacation.

Johan got the message and said, "It's a bit far. We'll need some time."

"No worries. It hasn't started yet."

After a few more words, they hung up.

Johan shrugged and looked at Flint.

"See? A normal Dragon Master would join a Flying-type tournament."

Flint fell into deep thought.

Johan told his Pokémon about their plans to participate in the Wing Festival Tournament.

Hearing this, Dragonite was obviously ecstatic.

But the most excited one?

Tinkaton.

"Cha! ╰(°▽°)╯"

The little pink Pokémon rubbed its hammer, eyes shining.

Big birds… so many big birds!

—

Fortree City, Hoenn's northernmost city.

It wasn't a large city, but it was unique—known as the "Treehouse City," almost all of its buildings were constructed atop towering trees.

Frequent rainfalls caused the trees to grow taller than in most other places.

Wild Bug-type Pokémon could often be seen flitting about.

Studies even suggested that Fortree City's residents tended to be healthier and live longer.

But the city wasn't just forests and treehouses.

It also had open plazas and aerial platforms built for Flying Pokémon performances.

Johan and his group arrived at Fortree City's central plaza.

The area was bustling with activity.

Stalls lined the plaza, while various Flying-type Pokémon soared overhead—Pidgey, Pidgeot, Fearow, Crobat, Swellow…

There were even rarer Pokémon like Skarmory and Tropius.

"Tinkaton, stay calm. Don't forget our deal," Johan reminded, pressing a hand on its hammer.

The twin-tailed, pink Pokémon hesitated for a moment before nodding with difficulty.

"Cha…"

Who would've thought that this seemingly harmless little creature was actually a big bird hunter?

"So lively," Flint remarked.

Hands in his pockets, he smirked.

For a moment, he really looked like the protagonist of a martial arts film.

Johan glanced at him sideways.

A guy who didn't even own a Flying-type Pokémon… and yet, he was so invested in the Wing Festival Tournament.

"Flint, ever heard this saying?"

"What?"

"That year, I walked with my hands in my pockets, not knowing what defeat was."

Flint grinned—this totally fit him.

Then Johan added,

"The next year, I walked with my hands on my head, not knowing how to fight back."

Flint: "…"

Just then, Cynthia's eyes gleamed as she pointed at a dango stall.

"Johan, that one."

Clearly, she was struggling to decide and was asking for help.

"Three orders of brown sugar dango!" Johan declared generously.

—

"First, let's head to the café. Lance is waiting for us there."

# 382: Wallace and Winona

At a café in Fortree City's central plaza, a man and a woman in capes were leisurely sipping coffee.

Noticing people approaching from afar, the young man with short, dark red hair called out:

"Three more cups of coffee, please."

"Understood, please wait a moment."

Then, he smiled and waved toward the plaza.

"Johan, over here."

Walking toward them were Johan, Cynthia, and Flint.

"It's been a while, Lance, Clair."

After sitting down, Johan greeted them casually.

"It really has been some time," Lance replied.

The two exchanged a knowing smile.

Clair, arms crossed, let out a soft huff, clearly not in the best mood.

"There seem to be a lot of people here today," Cynthia said as she finished the last dango, speaking with her mouth half-full.

Flint glanced toward the central registration area of the plaza, where a massive crowd had already gathered.

"Looks like even signing up won't be easy."

"Don't worry," Lance smiled slightly. "I already registered for you all."

Flint cautiously glanced at Johan and Cynthia, then pointed at himself.

Lance's smile remained unchanged. "You're all signed up, no need to thank me."

Flint took a deep breath, forced a smile, and said, "Thanks…"

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar emerged from Johan's shadow, pointed toward the nearby food stalls, and drooled.

"Go on," Johan ruffled its head, "just don't cause trouble."

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar called Rotom along.

Come on, how could you not trust me?

"Rotom, Ninetales, keep an eye on it," Johan instructed.

Bzzzt—

A Poké Ball on his waist popped open, and Ninetales emerged.

"Are… are you serious, Roto?"

"Oowoo~"

Rotom alone might not have been able to rein in Gengar, but with Ninetales around, it was definitely possible.

Gengar could be mischievous, but it wouldn't cross the line in broad daylight.

Humming a tune, the three Pokémon trotted off.

"Where were we?" Lance asked.

"Oh, Flint wanted to ask you for advice on catching a Flying-type Pokémon," Johan said without hesitation.

"Flying-type? Me?"

Lance pointed at himself, confirming.

"You know a lot about them, and he specializes in Fire-types."

"I see…"

Lance rubbed his chin in thought and murmured,

"Charizard is an excellent choice."

Flint's eyes lit up. That was pretty much what he expected.

Then Lance continued,

"Moltres is also a good option."

Flint's breath caught. Then, he nodded rapidly—this suggestion was even better!

No doubt about it, Lance truly lived up to his name.

Johan, watching the two seriously discuss the matter, was momentarily stunned.

One dares to suggest, the other dares to listen…

"Johan, Cynthia, Lance, and Clair, welcome to Fortree City."

As they chatted, a tall, elegant woman in a light blue flight suit approached them.

It was none other than Fortree City's Gym Leader—Winona!

Flint covered his face.

Will no one recognize me?

"Oh, my apologies. Welcome, Flint," Winona added after noticing him.

"N-no problem!"

Flint shivered, eyes brimming with tears.

Finally, someone acknowledged me!

The group exchanged greetings.

After learning they were participating in the Wing Festival's aerial race, Winona smiled and said:

"The weather today is excellent—perfect for flying. However, based on my experience, the optimal flying conditions haven't arrived yet. You may have to wait a little longer."

Winona wasn't just the Gym Leader of Fortree City; she was also the chief Gym Leader of Hoenn, overseeing various Gym-related matters.

Like Erika in Kanto.

So she easily struck up a conversation with the group.

Johan and Lance were first-time participants, but as the host, Winona had attended many times and generously shared her insights.

"The best flying conditions aren't necessarily when the air is completely still. It's about reading the air currents, understanding them, and using them to your advantage…"

Technically, Pokémon were not allowed to attack each other during the race.

However, there were no absolute rules.

For example, a Pokémon flying in front might use an aerial maneuver to disturb the airflow, making it harder for those behind to keep up.

Likewise, positioning and flight paths could be used to gain an advantage.

Additionally, the racecourse itself was designed to be unpredictable, featuring water surfaces, forests, rock formations, and other obstacles—requiring Trainers to make quick decisions.

"Ahem… what if a Trainer isn't great at riding a Pokémon?" Johan asked, clearing his throat.

The group turned to look at him.

Johan quickly added,

"I'm asking for Flint."

Flint: "???"

Everyone nodded in understanding.

Only Cynthia, smiling mischievously, glanced at Johan with a knowing look.

Winona chuckled and explained,

"Trainers can fly separately from their Pokémon. The event provides hot air balloons, though this makes it harder to give commands in real-time."

She paused, then added,

"If you insist on flying together, protective gear is highly recommended. It may slightly hinder movement but ensures safety."

Protective gear!

"Or…"

Winona's smile grew.

"You could just let your Pokémon carry you."

Johan immediately pictured Red being carried by an Aerodactyl, held up by his backpack straps.

And if he fell…

Winona continued giving tips on aerial racing until another person arrived.

"Everyone."

As expected—

It was Wallace, the current Sootopolis City Gym Leader.

The moment Wallace appeared, Winona's warm and friendly expression instantly turned cold. She stood up and said,

"My apologies, I have some preparations to attend to. I'll take my leave."

After a brief, polite bow, she turned and walked away.

Wallace looked awkward.

Johan nudged him.

"Go after her."

Wallace hesitated for a moment before realizing Johan's intent. He gave Johan a grateful look before hurrying after Winona.

"Winona!"

The group exchanged glances.

Clair, ever perceptive, immediately caught on.

"Are Wallace and Winona…?"

Flint, on the other hand, suddenly exclaimed,

"Do they have a grudge?"

Everyone: "…"

"You seem to know something?" Cynthia asked Johan.

She had noticed how he prompted Wallace.

Johan held up both thumbs and gently bent them.

However, no one seemed to understand the gesture.

How can you not recognize this universal sign?

Sighing, Johan explained,

"They're a couple. Or at least, they used to be."

Clair's gossip-loving instincts ignited. She stared at Johan and said seriously,

"Details."

Even Cynthia looked intrigued.

Seriously?

Johan eyed the bag of sunflower seeds that had suddenly appeared in Clair's hands.

Do you also have a Gengar hidden somewhere?

His knowledge about Wallace and Winona came from Pokémon Adventures.

Wallace and Winona had been dating for a long time.

When Wallace was younger, he participated in the Pokémon League and won, leading many to believe he had the potential to become an Elite Four member—or even a Champion.

But instead, he chose to follow his mentor, Juan, and became Sootopolis City's Gym Leader.

Winona, also a Gym Leader, found it suffocating to have a stronger partner always by her side, predicting her every move. It put immense pressure on her, leading to their breakup.

However, she never realized…

The reason Wallace made that choice was because she had just taken over as Gym Leader, and he wanted to stay close to support her.

But Wallace never told her this, fearing it would hurt her pride even more.

And so…

Their relationship remained in a delicate balance—still in love but unable to bridge the gap between them.

After hearing Johan's brief explanation, the group fell silent.

It wasn't about who was right or wrong.

But…

It was a shame they never moved past it.

Johan also recalled how, in Pokémon Adventures, both Wallace and Winona had tragically perished during the Hoenn crisis—until Celebi reversed time and saved them.

Thank Arceus for Celebi.

Not long after, Wallace returned with a resigned look.

Clearly, this wasn't his first attempt… or his first failure.

Lance patted him on the back in sympathy.

Only to be immediately glared at by Clair.

Do you even have the right to be giving out relationship advice?

Lance, baffled by Clair's sudden hostility, had no idea what he did wrong.

At that moment, Flint suddenly stood up.

"Let's battle!"

Eyes burning with determination, he scanned the group.

In his mind, there was no problem that couldn't be solved with a Pokémon battle.

And if there was?

Just battle more!

Lord of the Manor in Pokemon

In the year 447 of the Kingdom's calendar, 3,000 years before the founding of the Pokémon League…

Survival, hunting, gathering, farming, building a territory, constructing a manor, taming Pokémon…

Thus, a fortified manor rose overnight, where wild Pokémon of all kinds lived alongside humans.

Three years later, when the army of the ancient Unova Kingdom heard the news and arrived, they were utterly dumbfounded as they gazed at a human commanding terrifying beasts.

# 383: Tinkaton’s “Final Form”

"Boss, I need a custom set of protective gear."

At the Wing Festival Tournament in Fortree City, Johan and Cynthia arrived at a protective gear shop with Dragonite.

"Coming, coming."

A middle-aged man stepped out from his temporary store.

"Whoa—"

Upon seeing Dragonite beside Johan, the man couldn't help but exclaim.

"What a massive one."

Johan's Dragonite stood at least a head taller than a normal one, and its body was at least twice as broad. Without a doubt, it was a true giant.

The shop owner's remark pleased Dragonite immensely. It flexed its chubby arms, showing off its pudgy muscles.

"Draaa! (ง •̀_•́)ง"

Power!

Cynthia, grinning, patted Dragonite's belly, which made a distinct "duang duang" sound.

She knew firsthand how comfortable that belly was.

"But does such a sturdy Dragonite even need protective gear?" The shop owner voiced his doubt.

Among all rideable Flying-type Pokémon, Dragonite undoubtedly provided one of the best riding experiences.

Its broad back, stable sitting area, easily grippable arms and neck, and its superb control over air currents made it an ideal mount.

On the other hand, Pokémon like Pidgeot, Fearow, and Dragonair were the ones that really needed protective gear.

"Just tell me—can it be equipped or not?" Johan cut to the chase.

Ignorant! Foolish!

This was a televised event! What if kids watched and tried to copy?

Not everyone was a Super Rookie!

As a mentor, he had to set a good example.

Johan had already prepared his solid reasoning in advance.

"Of course, it can!"

Faced with Johan's firm stance, the shop owner immediately puffed up his chest in confidence.

He then took out a set of professional tools and equipment.

"This Dragonite is huge. I'd recommend this universal gear. It's easy to detach, adjustable in size, and simple to install…"

As he introduced it, he displayed a few images.

Suddenly, a thought popped into Johan's mind.

Could this gear be installed on Rayquaza?

Or maybe… Giratina?

But as soon as that dangerous thought arose, he immediately crushed it.

"Cha~"

Tinkaton suddenly emerged from its Poké Ball, staring intently as the shop owner hammered away, installing a complete and stylish set of protective gear onto Dragonite's back.

As it watched, a look of realization flashed across its face.

"Draaa?"

After the installation, Dragonite spun around twice in front of Johan and Cynthia.

Does it look cool?

Without hesitation, Johan responded, "Cool!"

Cynthia also smiled knowingly and nodded.

Hearing the affirmation, Dragonite beamed with satisfaction. As it walked down the street, it knew it was the most stylish one around.

Especially with its size and status as a pseudo-legendary Pokémon, it drew nearly every gaze its way.

Dragonite instantly became even more confident, striding with a majestic air.

"Cha!!"

Suddenly—

Tinkaton, who had been dragging its feet in a daze at the back, snapped out of its trance with a jolt.

It immediately ran up to Johan, tugged on his sleeve, and pointed at itself, then at Dragonite's protective gear.

"Cha~ Cha~!"

"You want… to modify this gear?"

Johan quickly understood.

But… this gear wasn't cheap—it cost over ten thousand Pokédollars!

"Cha cha!"

Yet, Tinkaton nodded furiously, looking absolutely certain.

Since it was so insistent and Dragonite had no objections, Johan couldn't bear to dampen the little one's enthusiasm.

After all—

Tinkaton had been contemplating how to build armor for Charcadet for ages but hadn't made a move yet. That meant it was no longer the reckless little "battle maniac" it used to be.

Thus, they moved to a secluded grove.

Tinkaton swiftly removed Dragonite's protective gear in just a few moves.

Judging by its skilled hands, it had completely "digested" the structure of the gear.

"Cha!"

But instead of getting to work right away, Tinkaton called out toward Johan's waist.

Bzzt—

Hearing the call, Charcadet immediately ran out.

"Goka!"

Then—

Johan, Cynthia, and Dragonite all sat down in a row, hugging their knees, watching the two Pokémon work.

Tinkaton effortlessly disassembled the protective gear, then, after examining it closely, took a piece and began heating it over Charcadet's flaming hands.

A hammer-wielding blacksmith and a living forge—was this the ultimate forging duo?

It looked like Tinkaton had truly found the perfect way to enhance itself, unlocking its final "blacksmith form."

For the first time, Tinkaton was seriously focused on forging.

"It actually looks very reliable," Cynthia murmured, hugging her knees.

She had seen this little blacksmith hatch from an egg, and now it was already crafting armor—it was nostalgic.

With rhythmic hammering and bursts of sparks flying, the protective gear slowly transformed.

"Cha~"

Tinkaton signaled to Dragonite.

"Draaa~"

Dragonite opened its mouth slightly and exhaled a gentle stream of water.

Ssss—

Steam rose as the gear underwent its final tempering.

Then, Tinkaton carefully reassembled the parts.

Compared to the original, the newly reforged gear looked much more streamlined.

More importantly—

In a demonstration, Tinkaton snapped it onto Dragonite's back in just one click, the entire process taking less than a second.

With its modifications, the gear was now easier to install and remove, more compact for travel, and interfered with Dragonite's flight even less—while also improving Johan's riding experience.

Johan couldn't help but ruffle Tinkaton's head while sighing emotionally.

"You've grown up."

A father's pride.

Maybe, one day, Tinkaton really would become the world's top Pokémon gear specialist—like the Pokémon Adventures character Riley, who built equipment for Pokémon.

If Riley could forge battle armor for Kubfu and Urshifu, calling it "Armor Warrior," maybe Tinkaton could do the same.

Would that mean… the Rusted Sword and Rusted Shield could also be reforged?

With its new gear, Dragonite felt even more like the king of the skies.

Woohoo, time to fly!

—

At the central plaza.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar dashed over, holding bags of snacks and cakes, handing them to Johan.

Seeing Gengar return with treats, Johan smiled and took them. "Good work."

"Ka-kaah~ (๑´ڡ`๑)"

Johan, Cynthia, Gengar, and the rest of the Pokémon sat neatly on the edge of a flower bed, all simultaneously munching on cake.

The aerial race was about to start. Contestants gathered at the central square one after another.

The vast blue sky was clear, a gentle breeze rustling through the treetops.

One by one, trainers and their Pokémon made final preparations for takeoff.

There were the usual competitors—Pidgeot, Fearow, Butterfree, Noctowl, Braviary…

Rarer ones included Skarmory, Tropius, and Noivern.

And then, there were the unexpected ones—like Jumpluff and Drifloon, currently warming up.

But the real standouts included:

A Hydreigon from Unova,

Lance and Clair's Dragonite,

Wallace's Pelipper,

And Winona's "dragon traitor"… Altaria.

All of them were clear top contenders.

Some trainers mounted their Pokémon, while others readied hot air balloons.

"What's Flint doing?" Cynthia asked, glancing at the training field.

Johan's expression turned weird.

"He's… warming up."

He hadn't expected Flint to actually compete.

But the real question was—he didn't own a Flying-type Pokémon.

Johan was curious to see what he'd pull off.

As Fortree City's mayor finished his speech and Winona explained the track rules, the competitors lined up for takeoff.

The race had three phases:

Takeoff & Forest Section – Navigating dense trees and wild Pokémon. Obstacle Section – Avoiding falling rocks and stone pillars, the best place to gain an advantage. Final Sprint – A long, open stretch—but tricky air currents would make it difficult.

"Let's go."

Johan turned to Cynthia.

"Mm."

She nodded, summoning Garchomp.

On the launch platform, Johan climbed onto Dragonite, gripping the gear tightly.

"Dragonite, safety first, race second."

"Draaa! (^^)"

Finally, time to fly free!

Below, Gengar led the cheer squad, pumping Dragonite up even more.

Johan wiped his forehead.

Flying races counted as competitions, right?

Would Dragonite's "lucky streak" trigger?

"Johan, how about a race?"

Lance, with his cape billowing, grinned confidently.

Johan twitched his mouth but said nothing.

As the whistle blew, contestants took off—

And Flint stole the spotlight.

"Magmortar, FLAMETHROWER!"

Magmortar blasted fire straight downward—

Launching Flint like a rocket.

"AHHHH, HOT HOT HOT!!"

"REF! IS THIS EVEN ALLOWED?!"

"DAMN, THIS GUY—"

Johan wiped his face.

Magmortar-powered jet flight?

Flint… was truly a genius.

Let's just hope he makes it out of Fortree City alive.

# 384: Johan: Don’t Rush, Let My Soul Keep Flying~

An unexpected opening—Magmortar took the lead, charging ahead at full speed.

But some competitors got caught in the crossfire.

For instance, an unnamed pseudo-camera operator later recounted in a post-race interview:

"Gah! (﹏`)"

Translation:

"You don't understand. Just as I was about to take off, a blast of fire shot toward me. At that moment, I could already smell roast duck, deep-fried sparrow, and grilled pigeon…"

Fortunately, since Flint and Magmortar didn't directly attack other competitors, they weren't disqualified.

"Woohoo!" Flint, standing on his hot air balloon, cheered excitedly. "Magmortar, full throttle!"

Watching this utterly flashy display, Johan momentarily forgot about the thrill of Dragonite's takeoff. He absentmindedly muttered:

"If even Magmortar can fly… is there anyone who can't?"

However, Magmortar's rocket launch technique obviously couldn't be sustained for long. After briefly leading the race, it was quickly overtaken by the majority of competitors.

After all, if a Flying-type Pokémon was slower than Magmortar, that would be downright embarrassing.

And worse—they risked taking multiple rounds of collateral damage.

Taking the true lead—was none other than our future Flying-type Elite Four, Lance!

Right behind him was Fortree Gym Leader Winona, soaring on Altaria's back.

As for Johan…

He clung to Dragonite's back with a deadpan expression, gripping the harness tightly.

One undeniable fact—this harness made the riding experience significantly better.

Even with the wind roaring past on both sides, making him feel like his body was in the front while his soul lagged behind, at least—

"I think… I can do this again!" Johan took a deep breath.

How could such a practical invention like a flight harness exist?

"Draaa?"

Dragonite turned its head, letting out a soft call.

Johan forced a smile.

He knew Dragonite loved flying and even more so carrying him along. But after some… unpleasant flying experiences in the past, the little guy had developed a tiny bit of concern.

It wanted to soar through the skies with Johan, but at the same time, it worried about hurting him.

So—

Seeing Johan's thumbs-up, Dragonite let out a joyous roar.

Nearby, Garchomp and Cynthia—who was crouched stylishly on its back, looking infinitely cooler than Johan—glanced over curiously.

Johan gave her a thumbs-up as well.

Cynthia smiled slightly, patting Garchomp's neck.

"Garrr—!!"

Garchomp instantly stopped holding back, letting out a fierce roar as it picked up speed.

"Draaa!"

"Yeah, no problem—GO—GOOOOO!!!"

With a sudden downward dive, Dragonite shot into the forest section of the race, launching Johan's soul even farther behind.

"Look at that!"

The race commentator shouted excitedly into the broadcast mic:

"Johan's technique is so professional! See how he presses close to Dragonite's back? Do you know why?"

"This reduces air resistance, allowing for faster command transmission and, most importantly—minimizing exposure to unknown dangers!"

"Everyone, are you taking notes?!"

Fwoosh, fwoosh, fwoosh—

Entering the dense forest caused a massive disturbance, startling the wild Pokémon within.

"Watch out for Pidgeotto!"

"Crap—Venomoth's Poison Powder!"

"Wait, why are all these Beedrill only chasing me?!"

The difficulty spiked instantly, as many competitors faced an unceremonious "welcome" from the local wildlife.

Especially those trainers who rode their Pokémon.

Thus highlighting Johan's superior flying posture.

One by one, other competitors started mimicking his stance.

Before he knew it, Johan had unintentionally become the textbook example for flight courses everywhere.

But for experienced trainers and Pokémon, these obstacles barely slowed them down.

Whoosh—

Lance remained at the front, bursting out of the forest into the rocky canyon section.

The others followed closely behind.

Now was the moment for competitors to truly widen the gap—each revealing their own tricks.

"Tropius, charge!"

"Jumpluff, it's your time to shine!"

The Chlorophyll ability activated under the clear skies, doubling the speed of Pokémon who possessed it.

[Chlorophyll: When in sunny weather, this Pokémon's Speed is doubled.]

"Tropius and Jumpluff are catching up fast! They're closing in on the leading pack!"

The so-called leading pack consisted of Lance, Winona, and Cynthia.

Lance's Dragonite was beyond question—countless emergency missions with the International Police had honed their flying skills to an elite level.

Winona and Altaria were equally formidable. As the local Gym Leader, she knew the terrain like the back of her hand.

From her calm, measured pace, it was clear—she and Altaria hadn't even gone full speed yet.

(Anyone who runs knows—the second-place runner benefits immensely from reduced air resistance.)

As for Cynthia…

Was there any flight scenario more extreme than launching off Mt. Coronet at full speed?

And Johan?

Firmly in the second tier.

According to him—this was tactical positioning.

Joining him were Wallace and Clair.

Seeing Tropius and Jumpluff rapidly closing in on the lead, Wallace glanced at Winona's back… and smiled faintly.

"Pelipper."

Dark clouds gathered above.

The race commentator shouted:

"It's—it's Drizzle! The Chlorophyll boost is gone!"

Johan glanced at Wallace's gentle smile.

Honestly, he wanted to shake this man.

"Dude. If you like her, just TELL HER."

"If you make sacrifices, SAY IT OUT LOUD."

"How's she supposed to know if you don't tell her?!"

"Otherwise, don't come crying when you turn into some tragic romance drama protagonist!"

"Whoa!! Flint's Magmortar is catching up!" The commentator's astonishment filled the air.

"Go, Magmortar!!"

While the rocky canyon was a nightmare for flying Pokémon…

It was perfect for Flint.

Magmortar landed on a towering rock pillar and blasted fire downward—

BOOM!

Jet launch, Round 2.

More competitors screamed in protest.

It seemed that getting out of Fortree City unscathed was becoming increasingly unlikely for Flint.

Even Johan felt the scorching heat closing in behind him.

Losing to Lance, Winona, or Cynthia was fine.

But losing to Flint in a flying race?

Johan clenched his teeth.

"Dragonite—FULL SPEED AHEAD!"

No bad luck so far—meaning Dragonite's luck balance had shifted into the positive zone.

Time to go all out.

"Draaa!!"

Hearing that, Dragonite roared in excitement, flapping its wings powerfully as it surged forward.

The final stretch—a narrow mountain pass—lay ahead.

The leading group prepared to make their final push.

Winona took the initiative.

"Altaria—Dragon Dance!"

Lance, unwilling to fall behind, called out:

"Dragonite—Dragon Dance as well!"

"Garrr!!" (I'm not losing to you!)

Cynthia's Garchomp roared, and—

Just as it was about to overtake Johan—

Dragonite flew past it.

This was a true high-level match.

A powerful headwind blasted through the pass.

Winona stayed composed.

"Altaria—Tailwind!"

Using the airflow to slingshot forward, she overtook both Lance and Cynthia.

Meanwhile, Lance called out to Johan:

"Johan! Now's our real showdown!"

To Lance, Winona and Cynthia weren't even a threat—

But Johan didn't respond.

Lance assumed he was focused.

In reality—

Johan was just too drained to speak.

He simply pressed his forehead against Dragonite's back, praying for dear life.

As the commentator hyped up the final sprint, all the top competitors unleashed their finishing moves.

Winona—Brave Bird!

Cynthia—Dragon Rush!

Lance—Dragon Rush!

Johan?

He mentally screamed:

"Screw it! Dragonite—GO FOR IT!"

"DRAAA!!"

With one final burst, Dragonite surged forward—

Crossing the finish line first!

L

ance, in second, simply sighed.

"Of course Johan had a trump card."

Winona: "That… was a high-rank match."

Cynthia's Garchomp: "...Where's Flint?"

As Johan stumbled off Dragonite, his legs gave out—

Cynthia, subtly, caught him just in time.

"Congrats, Johan," Lance said.

Johan raised a hand, stopping them all.

"Don't rush…" he muttered.

"Let my soul… keep flying for a bit…"

# 385: Professor Johan, the Love Expert?

"I can't believe Professor Johan actually won in the end."

"He must have been holding back, lowering his opponents' guard before seizing victory at the last moment. This guy is terrifyingly strategic!"

"Did you see that flight harness? Now that's what I call professional!"

"..."

During the final sprint, aside from the commentator, the entire audience had held their breath in silence. It was only after Johan crossed the finish line that they erupted into cheers and discussions.

After the awards ceremony, Winona personally delivered the prizes to Johan and the others on behalf of the competition organizers.

As the champion, Johan received the item Sharp Beak as well as a complete Feather Set.

Although Dragonite was both a Dragon-type and a Flying-type Pokémon, it actually didn't know that many Flying-type moves.

And, well…

A beak for Dragonite? That just felt weird.

"Whatever, something is better than nothing. I'll just let Dragonite play with it."

Johan thought to himself while smiling at Winona. "Thank you, Miss Winona."

"And also this."

Winona handed him a badge that looked like a delicate white feather. "This is another prize for the champion."

The Feather Badge from the Fortree Gym!

Winona continued, "I heard that Professor Johan enjoys collecting Gym Badges. I figured this would be a nice fit for your collection."

Johan: "???"

Since when did I get labeled as someone who likes collecting Gym Badges?

I mean, yeah, I do have a few more than usual, but…

"Of course, there's this as well," Winona added, handing over a Technical Machine.

It was a TM for Roost.

[Roost]: The user lands and rests its body. It recovers nearly half its HP but temporarily loses its Flying-type attribute.

"Then I won't say no."

Johan happily accepted the prize.

The badge? Whatever, it could go in Gengar's collection of trinkets.

But the TM was definitely useful.

Roost was a fantastic move for keeping Dragonite in peak condition.

And in certain strategic situations, it could even be used to negate quadruple weaknesses to Rock- or Ice-type attacks.

Lance, as the runner-up, and Winona, who placed third, also received their respective rewards.

As for Cynthia, although she didn't place in the top three, she still earned a prize.

"Here, take this."

Cynthia handed Johan the TM she had received.

"Hmm?"

"I don't need Flying-type moves for now," Cynthia explained.

The TM she got was Air Slash.

[Air Slash]: Base power 75. Has a 30% chance to make the target flinch.

Johan immediately thought of Serene Grace Togekiss + Air Slash flinch chains—a true nightmare for anyone on the receiving end.

If a Pokémon had Serene Grace, the chance of secondary effects happening would double. Meaning… Air Slash now had a 60% flinch rate.

The ultimate flinch-hax strategy.

For non-lucky players? Well…

"Serene Grace Togekiss, Air Slash, Missed five times in a row."

Johan shook his head. Let's not talk about that.

But still, this move was absolutely deadly for lucky trainers.

In his memory, Cynthia was supposed to catch a Togekiss at some point.

But at this stage, she didn't have one yet.

So for Johan and Dragonite…

If they added Air Slash to their moveset, considering Dragonite's special talent for luck-based battles…

Could this be a budget Serene Grace Togekiss in the making?

"Hmm…" Cynthia murmured thoughtfully, then casually said, "What should we have for dinner tonight?"

Johan instantly understood her meaning.

"Leave it to me!"

—

With the Flying Race finished, Dragonite proudly flaunted its champion medal in front of the other Pokémon.

Flint rejoined the group, shaking his head regretfully.

"Such a shame… I almost won."

If not for the fact that his face was bruised and battered, Johan might've believed him.

This guy had single-handedly ruined the takeoff for nearly half the competitors and nearly caused a forest fire.

The fact that he made it back alive was honestly a miracle.

Still, Flint did manage to make a name for himself. After today, many Flying-type trainers would have a tough time holding their heads high.

—

That evening.

Johan once again prepared a feast, and Cynthia took the opportunity to invite Wallace and Winona.

However, throughout the meal, Wallace and Winona exchanged fewer than three sentences.

And those three sentences?

All initiated by Wallace.

And all completely ignored by Winona.

Faced with this awkwardness, Wallace looked completely helpless.

He would've asked for help…

But seeing that Johan had zero romantic experience, Lance was a steel-hearted battle freak, and Flint was… well, Flint…

He decided to give up.

—

After dinner, Flint suggested another round of battles.

This time, everyone gladly agreed.

Most took part in one or two friendly matches, except for Johan, who preferred to sit out and watch.

Then came an interesting matchup—Flint vs. Winona.

"Holy crap—Flint is really going all out!" Clair murmured under her breath.

Flint was always reckless in battles, sometimes going too far.

But to be fair, Winona wasn't weak either.

"I think he's doing great," Lance said, completely serious.

"Going all out is the greatest form of respect for your opponent."

Clair shot him a glare.

So that's why you never hold back against me, huh?!

In the end, the battle ended just as expected—Winona lost.

Not that surprising, considering she was using a Skarmory against Flint's Fire-types.

Flint, completely oblivious to the tension in the air, turned around with a grin.

"Alright! Who's next?"

Everyone exchanged glances.

Then Wallace stood up, smiling.

"I'll go next."

Johan, sitting nearby, suddenly felt a chill run down his spine.

He turned his head—only to immediately sense an overwhelming killing intent directed at Flint.

Wallace was out for revenge.

Even Clair unconsciously shrank back a little.

But she still muttered, "…That's kinda manly, though."

Meanwhile, Winona simply frowned.

And then—

Gengar suddenly appeared beside Johan, holding a bucket of popcorn from who-knows-where.

"Ka-kah~"

This is gonna be good.

Gengar turned and offered the bucket to Cynthia.

Cynthia chuckled. "Thanks."

"I want some too!" Clair ran over excitedly.

At that moment, Flint was still blissfully unaware.

Then—

"Gyarados, Hydro Pump!"

Wallace immediately called out his attack without hesitation.

Even a Physical Gyarados finishing with Hydro Pump?

Yeah.

Flint never stood a chance.

—

Before Wallace could call for the next challenger, Winona silently stepped forward, releasing her Altaria.

From an outsider's perspective, it looked like Wallace was avenging Winona.

But in Winona's eyes, Wallace was showing off his strength—which was exactly why they had broken up in the first place.

She had never wanted to be seen as "Wallace's girlfriend" or respected because of him.

She wanted to earn that respect herself.

The entire group instinctively went silent.

Even Flint and Lance, who weren't the most perceptive, could feel the tension.

In the audience, only Gengar continued crunching on popcorn.

"Winona…" Wallace frowned.

"Please take this battle seriously, Wallace," she said flatly.

It was the first time she had spoken to him all night.

Wallace took a deep breath.

"Alright."

Johan nearly fell off his chair.

Alright, my ass!

These two were about to fight to the death!

—

Cynthia gently tugged Johan's sleeve.

"We can't let them finish this battle," she whispered.

If they fought to the end, their relationship would be beyond saving.

Johan sighed.

"Okay. I have a plan."

Johan took a deep breath and said, "Cynthia, Lance, when I give the signal, step in and stop them."

"Got it!" Cynthia nodded.

"Huh?" Lance blinked. "Aren't they battling just fine—"

But after a sharp glare from Clair, he quickly shut his mouth and sighed. "...Fine, I got it."

—

On the battlefield, Altaria and Milotic clashed once again before separating.

That was the moment Johan called out, "Now!"

"Garchomp!"

"Dragonite!"

Cynthia's Garchomp and Lance's Dragonite instantly rushed onto the field.

In the stunned gazes of Wallace and Winona, the two Pokémon firmly blocked Altaria and Milotic from continuing the battle.

"That's it?" Clair asked, tilting her head.

Johan adjusted his glasses.

"No, now we move to the next step."

He stepped onto the battlefield and called out, "Gengar!"

"Ka-kah!!"

Gengar let out a mischievous laugh and raised its hands.

"Hypnosis!"

Wallace and Winona, caught completely off guard, were immediately put to sleep.

"You two keep saying you can't talk things out properly, right?" Johan muttered.

"Fine. Then you can talk in a dream."

He and Gengar were experts in dream manipulation, especially nightmares.

They would recreate the events of the Hoenn Crisis—when Team Aqua and Team Magma had held Winona hostage, forcing Wallace into a terrible dilemma.

And if that wasn't enough…

Well, if one nightmare wasn't enough, they'd make it two. If two weren't enough, they'd make it three.

Because deep down, Johan knew—

Wallace still loved Winona.

And Winona still loved Wallace.

That was the foundation of everything.

If that didn't exist, no amount of "shared experiences" would help.

To the rest of the group, only a few minutes had passed.

But for Wallace and Winona, inside the dream, they had gone through countless crises together.

When they finally woke up, both looked disoriented.

And yet, as soon as their eyes met—

They understood.

Everything was clear now.

Even if they weren't ready to get back together immediately, at least their relationship could start to heal.

Johan let out a long sigh of relief, motioning for Gengar to stop applying nightmares.

—

Back in the seating area, Clair suddenly approached Johan.

Her face was slightly red, and she hesitated before whispering, "Um… That thing you just did…"

Johan turned to her.

"Can you… do that to Lance too?"

Johan: "..."

Meanwhile, Flint, who had finally caught on, gasped in realization.

"Wait a second… Does this mean Professor Johan is actually a love expert?!"

—

Somewhere in Sinnoh—White Forest.

Under the hazy moonlight, a shadowy figure stood alone, gazing up at the night sky.

Its expression flickered slightly.

"Nightmares…"

----------

LORD OF THE MANOR OUT NOW!

# 386: Johan – This is My House!

5 bonus chapter's coming out at 11 AM EST. So I ask that you save you're powerstones until then. BTW, Lord of the Manor in Pokemon is out now!

-----------

January 10th, clear weather.

Early morning.

Amidst the misty morning air and the rising sun, Johan and Cynthia were seated on a private plane heading to Rustboro City.

Just this morning, after breakfast, Johan received a call from the older Gallade sister.

On the phone, she reported that she had noticed some unfamiliar faces suddenly appearing around the café.

These people followed a certain pattern, often lingering in specific corners, quietly observing the café.

This made the older Gallade sister wary.

She had once led her younger brother in an escape from the clutches of Pokémon hunters, so she had developed keen observation skills and a strong sense of impending danger.

With her powerful psychic abilities, she was confident in handling potential threats.

But now, the café was not just home to her and her brother.

There was also the adorable Alcremie.

And judging by the numbers, this group wasn't small.

So, for safety reasons, she decided to inform Johan.

As a result—

Johan and Cynthia immediately departed from Fortree City, heading straight to Rustboro.

Johan already had a strong suspicion regarding the identity of these people.

Most likely—

They were the Pokémon hunters who had once captured the Gallade siblings.

After seeing the strength that the older Gallade sister displayed in online challenge videos, they were tempted again.

This was undoubtedly the perfect opportunity to reclaim the Gallade siblings' Poké Balls.

Back then, Johan had deliberately allowed the older Gallade sister to take on challengers and even let them post their battle videos online with this very goal in mind.

After all, searching for these Pokémon hunters across the vast Pokémon world was far less effective than letting them come to him.

He just hadn't expected—

That they would actually have the guts to show up!

Johan had already notified Officer Jenny in Rustboro, Roxanne at the Rustboro Gym, international police operating near the city, and Hoenn League investigators.

Honestly, before this, Johan hadn't realized that he now had the influence to mobilize so many people in Rustboro City.

And this wasn't even counting the Rustboro Pokémon Academy and the Devon Corporation.

However, Johan did not order an immediate operation.

If nothing unusual happened at the café, they were to remain in place—no need to startle the targets.

Of course, to ensure the safety of the Gallade siblings and Alcremie, he did emphasize: If a battle or any sign of conflict broke out at the café, then they were to take immediate action.

Reclaiming the Poké Balls and capturing these Pokémon hunters was important, but it was nothing compared to the safety of the three Pokémon inside the café.

"Don't worry," Cynthia said, seeing Johan deep in thought and offering him a reassuring smile.

Johan snapped out of his thoughts and grinned.

"I'm not worried. I'm just really looking forward to telling them... Welcome to my territory."

Hearing Johan's "dominant" words, Cynthia chuckled softly.

"Speaking of which, Flint and the others should be in position by now, right?"

Johan nodded.

"As long as they don't blow their cover, we should be good."

Hoenn, Fortree City.

At a café in the plaza of the Wing Festival, Flint slouched in a chair, hands in his pockets, his expression blank and "despairing."

A steaming cup of coffee sat in front of him, but he hadn't taken a single sip.

Because across from him sat a man and a woman.

The man wore a stylish black suit, a round-brimmed hat, and a monocle, looking impeccably handsome.

The woman, on the other hand, draped a large black trench coat over her shoulders, her long blonde hair cascading down her back...

From a distance, they looked exactly like Johan and Cynthia, but in reality, they were Wallace and Winona in disguise!

"Winona," Wallace smiled gently at the girl he admired.

"Hmph~"

Winona gave a soft snort, her fair cheeks tinged with a light blush.

Even though it was just a snort, her attitude today was a stark contrast to yesterday.

Yesterday, she had been cold and distant, practically hostile.

But today... she seemed more like a girl acting coy?

Seeing this, Wallace's smile deepened, and he gently took Winona's hand.

She struggled symbolically for a moment before giving in.

After last night's series of "nightmares," Wallace and Winona had finally sat down for a genuine, heart-to-heart conversation.

Though they hadn't officially gotten back together, their relationship had significantly improved.

For this, Wallace was immensely grateful to Johan.

Those "nightmares" may have been unsettling, but they had served their purpose in breaking down the barriers between him and Winona.

As for today's disguises, those were all part of Johan's plan.

Sitting across from them, Flint had a look of utter despair.

Why?

Why was he sitting here?

In the shadows of the plaza...

Two masked individuals held cameras, snapping photos of Flint, Wallace, and Winona from various angles.

"That's Johan and Cynthia, right?"

"Relax, it's definitely them. Look at the blonde hair, the trench coat, the suit, the glasses—how could we be wrong?"

As they took photos, they kept updating their reports.

"They're holding hands!"

"No way?!"

A freshly printed photograph showed Wallace and Winona clasping hands—while still in their Johan and Cynthia disguises.

"Shit, this is a bombshell! If we post this online, it'll go viral!"

It was easy to imagine tomorrow's headlines—

"Shocking! Famous teacher and beautiful student caught holding hands in public!"

"My teacher is broke, but a rich woman is head over heels for him!"

"Unbelievable! Who stole our beloved Cynthia?!"

"Viral my ass! We're Pokémon hunters! If this goes viral, we'll get arrested!"

"...Fair point."

Despite their excitement, they continued their work diligently, unaware that—

Just a short distance behind them, two other figures sipped their bubble tea, quietly observing them.

Lance and Clair.

"So these are the guys Johan was talking about?" Lance muttered, chewing on the tapioca pearls.

"How does he even know?" Clair sipped her drink, puzzled.

Lance smirked.

"Johan's an advisor to the investigation unit and a chief inspector for the International Police..."

"Wait—so if we nail this operation, your vacation gets extended by another week, right?" Clair confirmed.

At this, Lance's expression grew serious.

"That's the plan."

"Good!"

Clair squinted slightly, a dangerous glint flashing in her eyes.

They didn't intend to make a move just yet.

These two were nothing—just small fry.

They needed to track them back to their hideout and take down their entire operation.

"What was that phrase Johan said?" Clair asked.

"'The mantis stalks the cicada, unaware of the oriole behind,'" Lance replied.

"Oh, right."

"We move when they do."

As a seasoned investigator, Lance's skills in this field were unquestionable.

If Johan had been present, he might've summed up the situation as:

You're high and mighty taking some photos, smugly bragging over your radio, oblivious to the trackers on you, and soon, your entire crew will be wiped out. Death was always creeping closer.

Hoenn, Rustboro City Outskirts.

Johan and Cynthia disembarked early from their plane.

To avoid alerting the targets, they remained out of sight for now.

However, for security purposes, Gengar had already taken Cynthia's Spiritomb and Marshadow ahead, using shortcuts to reach the café.

The Pokémon hunters had set up a net around the café.

Little did they know—

A much larger net had been cast around them.

Johan's goal was simple.

One: capture them all.

Two: minimize the disruption to the city.

"Let's go," Johan said.

"Right," Cynthia nodded.

Rustboro City, outside Johan's café.

A scruffy-looking man pressed two fingers to his earpiece.

"Confirmed?"

"Confirmed! Johan and Cynthia are still in Fortree City!"

The man exhaled in relief.

"Move in!"

Inside the café, the doorbell jingled.

As the hunter reached for his Poké Balls—

Ka-kaah~!

A grinning, sunglasses-wearing Gengar appeared, blocking the capture beam.

Then—

Johan descended the stairs, holding the two stolen Poké Balls.

With a cold gaze, he crushed them underfoot.

"This is my house."

----------

# 387: Drake – I Agree; Agatha – I Initiated It

5 bonus chapter's coming out at 11 AM EST. So I ask that you save you're powerstones until then. BTW, Lord of the Manor in Pokemon is out now!

-----------

Johan?!

The young Pokémon hunter was completely stunned when he saw Johan.

Wasn't he supposed to be in Fortree City?

There was no way he could have teleported here—it was too far…

But before he could process his thoughts, Gengar delivered a complete "nightmare package." The man collapsed to the ground, foaming at the mouth before he even had a chance to summon a Pokémon.

At the same time—

The League officers, International Police, and Investigators who had been lying in wait sprang into action.

Like a thunderstorm, they swiftly apprehended the Pokémon hunters they had been tracking, leaving them with no time to react.

However, the scruffy, middle-aged leader of the Pokémon hunters had a quicker reaction.

To be the leader of a group like this, he had to have some skills.

The moment he realized that the young hunter hadn't come back out of the café, and his earpiece remained silent with no updates, he knew something was wrong.

"Damn it!"

Without hesitation, he summoned a Yanmega and leapt onto its back, attempting to escape.

But before he could take off—

A Garchomp dropped from above, slamming into Yanmega with a Dragon Rush, knocking it out instantly.

"Cynthia…"

He gritted his teeth and spat out her name.

Then, from the side, Cynthia stepped forward, her face cold as ice.

At the same time, Johan walked out of the café, heading toward him with Gengar and the older Gallade sister at his side.

"Johan…"

Buzz—Buzz—

As he walked, the Poké Balls on his belt opened one by one.

Ninetales, Tinkaton, Dragonite, Kubfu, and Charcadet all appeared in succession, their expressions filled with hostility.

Someone had targeted their friend—no, their family.

It wasn't just Johan who was furious; his Pokémon were just as enraged.

The middle-aged hunter instinctively summoned his Ariados and Venomoth, but he couldn't stop himself from swallowing hard.

To his left was Cynthia and her Garchomp.

To his right was Johan and a whole squad of pissed-off Pokémon.

Cold sweat dripped down his forehead.

He was screwed. This was an unwinnable battle!

Johan spoke coldly: "Don't damage the surroundings."

That was his only request.

No unnecessary destruction—it wouldn't look good for the city.

Then he added: "We're having a feast tonight."

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar grinned menacingly.

Johan's Pokémon also flashed eerie smiles.

Their expressions… made the hunter shudder.

Why did they look more like villains than he did?

"Cha~!"

Brothers, beat him up!

After the Battle

"Johan!"

Officer Jenny saluted Johan.

"Sir!"

The International Police operations leader followed suit.

"Advisor."

The Hoenn Investigation Department's team also greeted him with respect.

For a group of barely a dozen Pokémon hunters, it almost felt like there were more law enforcement officers than criminals.

But each division had its own jurisdiction.

The League Police maintained security within Rustboro City.

The Hoenn Investigation Unit focused on regional security.

And the International Police covered the entire Pokémon world.

None of them had superior authority over the others—it was simply a matter of different responsibilities.

This group of Pokémon hunters would be subjected to three rounds of interrogation—by the League Police, the Hoenn Investigation Unit, and finally, the International Police.

Johan gave a sincere nod. "Thank you, everyone."

"This is our duty," they responded.

"What's the status in Fortree City?" Johan asked.

"According to Investigator Lance, they've already located the hunters' temporary base in Hoenn. They're preparing for a raid—most of them should be arrested by tonight!" a Hoenn Investigator reported.

"We suspect they might be linked to the Pokémon hunter group that recently fled from Sinnoh after the League's crackdown. This could lead to further investigations," added the International Police operations leader.

The Pokémon hunters who escaped the Sinnoh League's raid…

Hunter J?

This feud was getting serious.

Johan thought for a moment before saying, "You've all worked hard."

With that, the officers escorted the captured hunters away.

Cynthia walked over with Garchomp and smiled.

"I didn't realize you had so many connections in Hoenn."

Johan scratched his head and laughed awkwardly.

"It's just… people being polite."

Nearby, Charcadet, who was clutching its Pokémon egg, suddenly understood something.

So this is what it really means when people 'give face'…

Johan patted the older Gallade sister on the head and said softly:

"You're truly free now."

"Gallade~"

The older Gallade sister nodded repeatedly, stars twinkling in her eyes.

So… can I battle now?

Hoenn, Fortree City – Deep in the Coastal Forest

Due to its location and dense foliage, this area was the perfect hiding place for Pokémon hunters who had fled from Sinnoh.

It was difficult to find, provided plenty of wild Pokémon to sustain them, and offered easy access to the sea for escape.

But today, this secret hideout was far from peaceful.

"Boss! There's League police spotted to the south!"

"Boss! Unknown groups approaching from the east and west!"

The burly leader of the Pokémon hunters, his face darkened, glared at the two men in front of him.

"I sent you to take some pictures, and this is what you bring back?!"

The two men shrank back, afraid to speak.

They truly didn't understand.

The Wing Festival had so many people—how did taking a few pictures attract suspicion?

And judging by this setup…

It didn't seem like a spontaneous raid.

The leader sighed, deciding there was no point in blaming them anymore.

At this point, the only thing that mattered was escape.

"We'll retreat north! Get to the boats and leave by sea!" he ordered.

However, just as he finished speaking, a nervous voice piped up:

"B-Boss… our boats… they've been taken over…"

"Taken over?"

The leader narrowed his eyes and waved his hand.

"Forget it. The League isn't good at naval combat—we'll retake the boats and escape!"

It made sense.

After all, the reason they had escaped the Sinnoh League's last raid was because of the sea.

However, that was Sinnoh.

This was… Hoenn.

"B-Boss, I don't think that's gonna work…"

"Huh?"

Just as the leader was about to explode in anger, the man stammered:

"T-The one leading them… is Drake, the Hoenn Elite Four."

Drake?!

They were just a small-time group—how did they end up provoking an Elite Four member?!

And not just any Elite Four member—Drake, a man known for conquering the seas.

The leader took a deep breath and activated a special communicator.

"Boss J… I think… we're finished."

This was a complete and utter trap.

BOOM!!

The ceiling of the hideout exploded.

Three figures descended.

Lance, Clair, and… Flint!

"HERE COMES THE GREAT FLINT!"

Flint: I'M FINALLY GONNA BLOW OFF SOME STEAM!

Somewhere in the Hoenn Sea – Pokémon Hunters' Offshore Stronghold

Hunter J lowered her communicator, her expression cold.

They had already suffered major losses after the Sinnoh League's raid.

Now, another team had been wiped out in Hoenn.

This was a disaster.

Originally, they had help from Team Galactic in Sinnoh, which allowed more of them to escape.

But the operation in Snowpoint City had been stopped ahead of schedule, leaving the Sinnoh League free to focus entirely on the hunters.

And now…

Hunter J narrowed her eyes.

Two names kept appearing in these raids:

Johan. Cynthia.

Especially the café owner this latest team had targeted.

It was impossible for her not to take notice of Johan.

"Johan…"

She rubbed her temples, her headache growing.

This wasn't just about losing another team.

The bigger issue was… the overall situation for Pokémon hunters.

The Sinnoh League was after them—that was expected.

Normally, they could still operate in Kanto, Johto, or Hoenn.

But recently, Pokémon hunters in Kanto and Johto were being targeted by Team Rocket.

And now, Hoenn was turning hostile too.

If she had known how deeply involved Johan was in all of this, she might have taken him out immediately.

Taking a deep breath, Hunter J made a call.

To Cyrus.

Sinnoh was dangerous, but at least Team Galactic needed them.

"Hello, it's me."

Hoenn Sea – North of Fortree City

Drake, the Hoenn Elite Four, stood at the bow of his ship.

His coat billowed in the wind as the sea raged around him.

A transparent screen in front of him displayed four figures.

Johan would have recognized them instantly:

Agatha, Bertha, Looker, and the International Police Chief.

They were discussing—

A joint Hoenn-Sinnoh operation to eliminate Pokémon hunters.

Currently speaking was Looker.

"…That concludes the summary of this operation. Personally, I believe Professor Johan is fully capable of taking on the role of command."

Drake nodded in agreement.

"He's young, but if it's Johan, I believe there's no issue."

Saying that, he turned his gaze to Bertha.

Noticing Drake's look, Bertha paused for a moment before responding.

"I was the one who initially proposed this."

The suggestion to have Johan lead this operation had come from her.

Drake was momentarily stunned.

Johan was from Hoenn.

Why did it feel like Bertha was trying to claim him for Sinnoh?

That wouldn't do!

He was the one who first recognized Johan's potential, and as he watched Johan grow stronger, he had even started considering when he could retire and enjoy a life at sea.

"Haha, then it's settled."

The elderly Jenny, head of the International Police Bureau, smiled as she made the final decision.

It didn't matter who had recognized Johan first—right now, he was the Superintendent of the International Police!

Aaron, who had little say in the discussion, quietly let out a sigh of relief.

At this moment, he had only one thought—

Professor Johan, please hurry up and take some of this workload off me!
